Committing to plans, the reasoner's or a fixed explore rule's, opens more doors and makes an actor that stands still play; with the rule the agent dies more often. Told that some doors need keys, the reasoner takes ordinary doors for locked ones, which the state cannot tell apart; without that knowledge it goes back to collecting. We release code, prompts, data and logs.

</details>


### [70] [Noise, Denoise, Correct: MCMC Posterior Sampling with Diffusion Priors in Three Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09407)
*So Takao,Gregory David Bellchambers,Luke Ye,Sanmitra Ghosh,Michalis Michaelides*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出扩散华尔兹：一种基于SDEdit提议与Metropolis-Hastings校正的精确后验采样MCMC方法，用于非线性、不可微前向模型的反问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型是反问题的强大先验，但在非线性、不可微前向模型下的后验采样仍然困难。

Method: 使用SDEdit式加噪-去噪作为提议分布，通过Metropolis-Hastings校正实现无需先验评估的精确后验采样；并利用无梯度集合卡尔曼更新将观测注入提议，同时保持精确性。

Result: 在不可微Navier-Stokes初始条件恢复任务上，扩散华尔兹在不同噪声与非线性程度下均优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 扩散华尔兹为非线性、不可微反问题提供了一种精确且有效的后验采样框架。

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models are powerful priors for inverse problems, but posterior sampling under nonlinear, non-differentiable forward models remain hard. We introduce diffusion waltz, an MCMC method using SDEdit-style noising-denoising as a proposal, corrected via Metropolis-Hastings for exact posterior sampling without prior evaluation. We further propose injecting observations into the proposal while preserving exactness, using a gradient-free ensemble Kalman update. On a non-differentiable Navier-Stokes initial condition recovery task, diffusion waltz outperforms existing baselines across different noise and nonlinearity regimes.

</details>


### [71] [Stability and Diversity of Networked Self-Consuming Generative Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09409)
*Xiukun Wei,Yang Zhang,Xueru Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文首次研究了网络化自消耗生成模型，即多个模型通过复杂交互路径相互消费合成数据，提出了基于有向加权图的理论框架来分析其长期行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的广泛部署导致合成数据不可避免地被纳入未来模型的训练流程，形成自消耗训练循环；已有研究多局限于孤立模型或两个模型间的简单交互，缺乏对多模型网络化自消耗的系统分析。

Method: 将模型表示为有向加权图中的节点，边权控制合成数据在模型间的流动；在此框架下分析重训练动态下网络化模型的长期行为，建立收敛条件并刻画固定点，进一步研究各模型对真实数据的访问、跨模型数据消费以及交互图结构对系统长期稳定性和多样性的影响。

Result: 建立了网络化自消耗生成模型收敛的条件，并刻画了其固定点；揭示了真实数据访问、跨模型数据消费和交互图结构如何影响系统的长期稳定性和多样性。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解网络化自消耗生成模型的长期动态提供了理论基础，表明多模型交互会显著影响系统稳定性和多样性，为未来生成模型训练管线的设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The widespread deployment of generative AI has made it increasingly difficult to distinguish synthetic content from real data. Consequently, synthetic data is inevitably incorporated into the training pipelines of future model generations, forming a self-consuming training loop. Prior work has studied the effects of such recursive self-consuming training, but analyses have largely been limited to isolated models, where a model consumes only its own synthetic data, or to simplified interactions between two models. This paper takes a first step toward understanding networked self-consuming generative models, in which multiple models consume synthetic data generated by one another through complex interaction pathways. We introduce a theoretical framework representing models as nodes in a directed, weighted graph, with edge weights governing the flow of synthetic data among models. Using this framework, we analyze the long-term behavior of networked models under retraining dynamics, establishing conditions for convergence and characterizing the resulting fixed points. We further investigate how the system's long-term stability and diversity are shaped by each model's access to real data, cross-model data consumption, and the structure of the interaction graph.

</details>


### [72] [Self-Evolve With a Reference:Anchored Training of Tool-Integrated Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09856)
*Wenjie Liao,Liangjie Zhao,Zehong Cao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出AnchorLoop，在自进化工具集成智能体中引入上一轮执行器的冻结副本作为历史参考，用于执行器与课程生成两方面，无需外部监督即可提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体仅依赖当前执行器提供反馈，存在两个局限：全共识时组相对优势消失；基于不确定性的课程奖励偏好分歧，但无法判断生成任务是否支持进一步学习。因此需要当前执行器之外的额外参考。

Method: AnchorLoop将上一轮执行器的冻结副本作为历史锚点，并在训练循环两侧复用：对执行器，锚点提供交叉参考优势，将当前输出与当前及历史多数答案对比；对课程，锚点基于采样多数一致性的差异提供一致性参考。由于课程训练时执行器与锚点参数相同，该比较仅作为任务选择的代理，而非版本间改进或正确性的证据。

Result: 在13个推理基准上，AnchorLoop相对Agent0在数学推理上提升2.5%，在通用推理任务上提升2.8%；同时保持更高的有效优势方差，并在后续迭代中持续改进，而无锚点基线收益递减。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在无外部任务或答案监督的情况下，为自进化工具集成智能体引入轻量级历史参考是有益的。

Abstract: Self-evolving tool-integrated agents learn from tasks and feedback generated within their own training loop. A Curriculum Agent generates tasks, while an Executor Agent learns from self-consistency signals through reinforcement learning. However, relying solely on the current Executor for feedback has two limitations: group-relative advantages vanish under full consensus, while uncertainty-based curriculum rewards favor disagreement without showing whether the generated tasks support further learning. These limitations motivate an additional reference beyond the current Executor. We propose \textit{AnchorLoop}, which introduces a frozen copy of the previous iteration's Executor as a historical reference and reuses it on both sides of the training loop. For the Executor, the anchor provides a cross-reference advantage that evaluates current outputs against both current and historical majority answers. For the Curriculum, it provides an agreement-based reference based on differences in sampled majority agreement. Since the Executor and anchor have identical parameters during Curriculum training, this comparison serves as a proxy for task selection rather than evidence of inter-version improvement or correctness. Across 13 reasoning benchmarks, AnchorLoop improves over Agent0 by 2.5\% on mathematical reasoning and 2.8\% on general reasoning tasks. It also maintains higher effective-advantage variance and continues improving in later iterations as the unanchored baseline shows diminishing gains. These results demonstrate the benefit of introducing a lightweight historical reference into self-evolving tool-integrated agents without external task or answer supervision.

</details>


### [73] [LiveMACE: Process-Aware Evaluation of LLM Agent Capabilities in Evolving Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09872)
*Jun Zhao,Leiming Fu,Yanbo Wen,Yiding Wang,Xuantong Liu,Yang Shu,Yuyang Lu,Xuanran Xing,Jingqi Tong,Hao Xu,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出过程感知基准LiveMACEBench，用实时金融市场评估LLM智能体，揭示结果与能力之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭结果评估智能体会掩盖产生结果的能力，在动态环境中问题尤为突出，因为结果反映智能体行为与外部条件的闭环交互。

Method: 以实时金融市场为自然演化测试床，让五个前沿LLM在工具使用、持久记忆、规则遵循和多智能体协作配置匹配下连续运行，基于完整决策轨迹进行结果评估和机制特异性诊断，持续30天实时评估。

Result: 发现显著的结果-能力差距：实现回报常与能力测量背离，相似结果可能源于截然不同的机制使用模式；轨迹诊断揭示不同能力的瓶颈，表明机制可及性、有效机制使用和下游表现并非可互换的智能体能力度量。

Conclusion: LiveMACEBench使这一区分可测量，将实时市场从性能排行榜转变为智能体能力的诊断环境。

Abstract: Evaluating agents by outcomes alone can obscure the capabilities that produce them. This problem is especially pronounced in evolving environments, where outcomes reflect a closed-loop interaction between agent behavior and changing external conditions. We introduce LiveMACEBench, a process-aware benchmark that uses live financial markets as a naturally evolving testbed for persistent LLM agents. Five frontier LLMs operate along continuous trajectories under matched Tool Use, Persistent Memory, Rule Following, and Multi-Agent Collaboration configurations. We evaluate them through both realized outcomes and mechanism-specific diagnostics derived from complete decision traces. Across 30 days of live evaluation, we find a pronounced outcome-capability gap: realized returns often diverge from capability-specific measurements, and similar outcomes can arise from markedly different patterns of mechanism use. Trace-level diagnostics further expose distinct bottlenecks across capabilities, demonstrating that mechanism access, effective mechanism use, and downstream performance are not interchangeable measures of agent capability. LiveMACEBench makes this distinction measurable, turning live markets from a performance leaderboard into a diagnostic environment for agent capability

</details>


### [74] [Think Before You Paint: Recursive Latent Reasoning for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09876)
*Paweł Skierś,Małgorzata Grzanka,Wojciech Masarczyk,Jan-Willem van de Meent,Kamil Deja*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出PaTh，将递归推理机制引入像素空间扩散模型，无需符号监督即可解决视觉推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型能生成逼真图像，但在数独、迷宫等视觉推理任务上表现差；已有递归方法依赖离散符号表示，而像素空间无符号表示可用。

Method: PaTh包含小型递归网络Thinker，对编码噪声图像和条件的学得token网格进行推理，在每步去噪中精炼隐状态，并通过ControlNet适配器引导冻结的扩散模型Painter；仅用标准重建损失训练，无需符号目标、求解器或验证器。

Result: 在困难MNIST数独上解决92.5%（此前最佳75%），极端数独71.2%（此前最佳4.1%），参数量10M对比标准扩散模型82M；在迷宫、Queens和指定空间关系的CLEVR场景上也有提升，优势随问题规模增大；诊断实验表明PaTh能修复扩散模型无法修复的注入错误，尤其当大量单元格错误时。

Conclusion: 为符号数据开发的推理机制可无需符号监督地集成到像素空间扩散中，为在日益复杂约束下生成数据开辟了道路。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate realistic images but often fail on visual reasoning tasks, such as filling in a Sudoku or drawing the path through a maze. When a discrete symbolic representation is available, recursive methods such as the Tiny Recursive Model (TRM) solve even hard instances of these puzzles. We ask how such reasoning can be carried over to pixels, where no symbolic representation is available. We propose Painter-Thinker (PaTh): a small recursive network (the Thinker) reasons over a grid of learned tokens that encode the noisy image and the conditioning, refines a latent state within every denoising step, and steers a frozen diffusion model (the Painter) through ControlNet adapters. The Thinker is trained with the standard reconstruction loss alone, without symbolic targets, a solver, or a verifier. PaTh solves 92.5% of hard MNIST Sudoku puzzles (prior best 75%) and 71.2% of extreme ones (prior best 4.1%), with 10M parameters against 82M for a standard diffusion model. It also improves on mazes, Queens, and CLEVR scenes with specified spatial relations, and its advantage grows with problem size. Diagnostic experiments show that PaTh recovers from injected mistakes that the diffusion model cannot repair, especially when many cells are wrong. Together, these results show that reasoning mechanisms developed for symbolic data can be integrated into pixel-space diffusion without symbolic supervision, opening a path toward generating data under increasingly complex constraints.

</details>


### [75] [Loud Failures, Quiet Failures: Fault Detection and Recovery in Tool-Using Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10062)
*Obada Kraishan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该研究通过故障注入实验揭示了多轮工具调用智能体在工具故障处理上的缺陷：智能体依赖错误信号而非结果内容，显式报错时能识别故障，但静默错误（格式正确但值错误）常被忽略，且推理模型并不优于指令模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中工具故障频发（超时、端点消失、参数名变化、结果看似正确但错误），现有研究已表明语言模型过度信任静默失败的工具输出，但尚不清楚这种过度信任在多轮智能体故障处理各阶段的表现。

Method: 将函数调用基准的可执行环境包裹故障注入层，在轨迹受控点注入四种类型故障，记录智能体是否察觉、改变计划、恢复任务或重复行为。六个模型（三个家族，半数推理变体）在24个多步任务上运行1920次试验。

Result: 显式报错时91.3%试验识别故障，返回看似合理错误值时仅58.8%，无故障时误报率26.8%。推理模型相比指令模型察觉更少（-9.3点，p<.001），改变计划更多（+10.4点，p<.001），恢复率无差异（p=.512）。无故障两次运行状态一致率仅63.3%。缺失工具显著降低恢复率（39.9%），超时、模式漂移和损坏在运行间变异内。故障后连续三次以上调用同一工具高达22.2%。提示检查结果未提升检测。

Conclusion: 智能体响应的是错误通道而非工具返回内容，因此格式符合预期的故障会被漏过；推理能力并未改善故障处理，静默失败是主要盲区。

Abstract: Tool-using agents are usually scored on whether they finish a task while the tools work. Deployments are less forgiving: services time out, endpoints disappear, parameter names change, and results come back well formed but wrong. Prior work has shown that language models over-trust tool outputs that fail silently; we ask how that over-trust plays out across the stages of failure handling in multi-turn agents. Wrapping the executable environments of an established function-calling benchmark in a fault-injection layer, we inject one of four typed faults at a controlled point in the trajectory and record whether the agent notices, changes plan, recovers the task, or repeats itself. Six models from three families, half of them reasoning variants, ran 1,920 trials over 24 multi-step tasks. Agents treat a failure as a problem in 91.3% of trials when the tool returns an explicit error, but in 58.8% of trials when it returns a plausible wrong value, against a 26.8% rate of reporting problems when nothing was wrong. Reasoning models are not better placed: paired against instruct siblings, they notice less (-9.3 points, p < .001) and change plan more (+10.4 points, p < .001), and recovery is unchanged (p = .512). Because agents are stochastic, two fault-free runs of the same task end in the same state only 63.3% of the time; against that baseline, only a missing tool clearly lowers recovery (39.9%), while timeouts, schema drift, and corruption stay within run-to-run variation. After a fault, agents return to the same tool three or more times in a row in up to 22.2% of trials, though strictly identical repeats are rare. A prompt line asking the agent to check each result did not move detection. Agents respond to the error channel rather than to the content of what a tool returns, so failures that stay inside the expected format pass through.

</details>


### [76] [CircuitGate: Logic-Consistent Circuit-Level Functional Modeling for And-Inverter Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09549)
*Qifan Zhang,Ruijie Li,Fangzhou Zhang,Qian Ma,Hui Li,Furui Zhan,Yongpeng Wang,Liying Hao,Shikai Guo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CircuitGate，一种功能感知的AIG表示学习框架，从门级语义提升到电路级功能建模，显著提升等价门识别与信号概率预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的AIG表示方法依赖局部门级消息传递，难以捕捉电路级功能上下文，且对拓扑特定模式敏感，缺乏对功能保持变换的鲁棒性。

Method: CircuitGate显式编码全局主输入支持集，建模扇入间支持重叠感知的重汇聚，并引入逻辑启发的布尔约束以促进功能一致的表示学习。

Result: 在ForgeEDA、EPFL和ITC'99基准上，等价门识别与信号概率预测任务中均优于现有方法，MAE分别最多降低21.7%和14.2%；在ForgeEDA到OpenABC直接迁移下取得最佳等价门识别性能。

Conclusion: 建模超越局部拓扑的电路级功能依赖关系是有效的，CircuitGate具备强跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: And-Inverter Graphs (AIGs) are fundamental representations for logic synthesis and verification in Electronic Design Automation (EDA). As structured representations of complex digital systems, AIGs require models to capture functional dependencies beyond local structure and remain robust to functionality-preserving transformations. In learning-based AIG representation, existing approaches are predominantly based on GNNs and rely on local gate-level message passing, limiting their ability to capture circuit-level functional context and making the learned representations sensitive to topology-specific patterns. Therefore, we propose CircuitGate, a function-aware AIG representation learning framework that advances from gate-level semantics to circuit-level functional modeling. CircuitGate explicitly encodes global primary-input (PI) support and models support-overlap-aware reconvergence between fanins, while incorporating logic-inspired Boolean constraints to encourage functionally consistent representations. We evaluate CircuitGate on the large-scale ForgeEDA benchmark and further validate it on the EPFL and ITC'99 benchmarks. Across equivalent-gate identification and signal-probability prediction tasks, CircuitGate consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving up to 21.7% and 14.2% reductions in MAE, respectively. Under direct ForgeEDA-to-OpenABC transfer without fine-tuning, CircuitGate also achieves the best equivalent-gate identification performance, demonstrating strong cross-dataset generalization. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of modeling circuit-level functional dependencies beyond local topology.

</details>


### [77] [HGP:An on-device personalized agent memory via hybrid graph storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10071)
*Ran Zhou,Xueming Han,Jiaheng Liu,Yuyao Zhang,Fanyu Meng,Junlan Feng,Yuxiang Ren*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出HGP混合图记忆框架，通过多类型图记忆和轻量分类器实现个性化交互，解决长期痕迹的异质、多类型和隐式约束问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在个性化交互任务中面临异质、多类型且隐式约束的长期痕迹挑战，现有记忆机制在设备端难以准确路由和检索，单向量表示模糊了类型区分和关系结构。

Method: HGP采用轻量自增强分类器进行个性化记忆路由，将情景、语义和程序记忆构建为图，并提取工作记忆作为状态轨迹以捕获当前状态和隐式约束。

Result: 在两个基准测试上，PAL-Set解决方案选择中HGP的S-score为35.58，比最强基线高近7分。

Conclusion: HGP通过混合图记忆和轻量分类器，减少大模型调用，支持设备端部署，实现准确检索和增量用户画像优化，显著提升个性化交互性能。

Abstract: LLM-based agents face challenges in personalized interactive tasks due to heterogeneous, multi-typed, and implicitly constrained long-term traces. Existing memory mechanisms struggle with accurate routing and retrieval, especially on-device where personalization is critical. Most methods use single-vector representations, blurring type distinctions and relational structure. We propose HGP, a hybrid graph memory framework. HGP employs a lightweight self-enhancement classifier for personalized memory routing and constructs episodic, semantic, and procedural memories as graphs. It also extracts working memory as a state trajectory to capture current state and implicit constraints, ensuring reliable decision-making. The classifier reduces large-model calls, enabling on-device deployment, while graph storage enables accurate retrieval and incremental user profile refinement. Experiments on two benchmarks show that on PAL-Set solution selection, HGP achieves an S-score of 35.58, nearly 7 points above the strongest baseline. Code and data are at https://github.com/Ouan6/HGP-.git.

</details>


### [78] [A Framework for the Systematic Review of ML Assets in AI Registries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09551)
*Alexandra González,Quim Motger,Xavier Franch,Silverio Martínez-Fernández*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该文探讨如何将系统综述方法应用于机器学习资产的检索，提出一个结构化、可复现的ML资产发现框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ML资产（预训练模型、数据集、基准）的探索、选择与复用缺乏系统检索方法，导致选择常基于非正式理由，缺乏可追溯、证据驱动且可更新的过程。

Method: 分析科学文献中的系统综述实践，将其规划、执行和记录阶段适配到AI注册库，将ML资产视为一级分析单元，整合注册库感知搜索策略、跨注册库模式对齐和依赖驱动的ML资产探索。

Result: 将ML资产检索概念化为系统且可复现的过程，而非临时活动，并提出结构化ML资产发现框架。

Conclusion: 系统综述原则可扩展到科学文献之外，支持对不断演变的AI注册库进行证据合成。

Abstract: Background: Modern software systems increasingly rely on Machine Learning (ML) assets (i.e., pre-trained models, datasets, benchmarks) for building, evaluating, and integrating ML-based systems. However, current exploration, selection and reuse practices of ML assets are not supported by systematic retrieval methodologies comparable to those used in traditional evidence synthesis. Consequently, in practice, ML asset selection is often presented as a settled design decision, supported by informal justification rather than a traceable, evidence-based, and updatable selection process. Aims: This paper explores how systematic review methods can support ML asset retrieval. In doing so, we aim to make their selection transparent and reproducible, grounded in explicit evidence, and ultimately better suited to its intended use. Method: We analyze established systematic review practices from scientific literature and adapt their phases (i.e., planning, conducting, and documenting) to Artificial Intelligence (AI) registries, treating ML assets as first-class units of analysis. The resulting framework integrates registry-aware search strategies, cross-registry schema alignment, and dependency-driven ML asset exploration. Results: We conceptualize ML asset retrieval as a systematic and reproducible process rather than an ad hoc activity, and propose a framework for structured ML asset discovery. \textbf{Conclusions:} This work illustrates how systematic review principles can be extended beyond scientific literature to support evidence synthesis over evolving AI registries.

</details>


### [79] [SkillSandbox: Skill Verification via Dynamic Scenario Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10088)
*Serin Kim,Kwangwook Seo,Dokyung Song,Jinyoung Yeo,Dongha Lee*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SkillSandbox框架，为每个技能动态合成技能相关且新颖的任务与环境，通过Proposer、Builder和Verifier验证技能可复用性，决定是否纳入技能库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化智能体从任务经验中提炼技能以供复用，但技能可能编码错误流程或不可迁移知识，因此验证技能的可复用性至关重要。然而现有任务可能无法暴露目标技能实际可被运用的情境。

Method: SkillSandbox动态合成每个技能的任务与环境：Proposer指定需保留的条件与需变化的源特定细节，Builder构建可执行场景，Verifier比较使用与不使用技能的执行情况，评估可执行性、效用和效率，给出Keep或Reject判定以决定技能是否进入库。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上使用三个模型，SkillSandbox持续取得最强的下游性能和更高的执行效率。进一步分析检验了这些增益是否反映对技能可复用性的准确评估，并识别了SkillSandbox各组成部分的贡献。

Conclusion: SkillSandbox能有效验证技能可复用性并提升下游性能与效率，其组件对增益有明确贡献。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents distill task-solving experience into skills for future reuse, but these skills can encode incorrect procedures or non-transferable knowledge. It is therefore critical to verify each skill's reusability: whether its guidance remains useful beyond the experience from which it was distilled. Such verification requires observing how a skill affects execution in new tasks, yet existing tasks may not expose the situations where the target skill can actually be exercised. To construct such situations, we propose SkillSandbox, a framework that dynamically synthesizes a task and its environment for each skill that are skill-relevant yet novel. A Proposer specifies the conditions to preserve and the source-specific details to vary, a Builder constructs an executable scenario, and a Verifier compares executions with and without the skill. The Verifier assesses executability, utility, and efficiency to assign a Keep or Reject verdict, determining whether the skill enters the library. Across ALFWorld and WebShop with three models, SkillSandbox consistently yields the strongest downstream performance and improved execution efficiency. Further analyses examine whether these gains reflect accurate assessment of skill reusability and identify which components of SkillSandbox contribute to them.

</details>


### [80] [UniCSI Towards a Universal Wi-Fi CSI Encoder for Ubiquitous Human Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09559)
*Daniel Eckhoff,Hua Kang,Zhitang Chen,Jie Chuai*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: UniCSI是一种统一基础架构，可直接处理异构CSI并保持原生波形完整性，通过物理信息RF分词器和频谱聚合器解决设备配置差异导致的建模难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wi-Fi感知中CSI因设备配置（子载波数、带宽、频段）不同而存在频谱分辨率和张量形状差异，标准架构难以处理，常需有损预处理从而损害信号。

Method: 提出UniCSI，包含两个核心创新：(1) 物理信息RF分词器，依据频率通道在物理频谱中的分数位置而非固定数组索引进行编码，保持频谱相干性，实现与频率分辨率无关的处理；(2) 频谱聚合器，将可变长度通道序列蒸馏为固定大小的频谱签名，解耦特征维度与物理子载波间隔。

Result: 在25个异构公开数据集（14至2048个子载波，20至160 MHz带宽，2.4和5 GHz频段）的大规模评估表明，原生异构输入在监督和自监督训练下均显著提升跨域迁移，尤其在固定网格架构无法泛化的场景中。

Conclusion: UniCSI能够直接处理异构CSI并保持原生波形完整性，有效提升跨域迁移性能，克服了固定网格架构的泛化局限。

Abstract: Wi-Fi sensing promises to turn the everyday wireless signals that already surround us into ubiquitous sensors for human sensing. However, a fundamental obstacle is that CSI is acquired under diverse device-specific configurations, including different subcarrier counts, bandwidths, and carrier bands. Consequently, the resulting CSI tensors vary in both spectral resolution and tensor shape, making heterogeneous modeling challenging. Standard architectures struggle with such heterogeneity, forcing lossy pre-processing which compromises the underlying signal. To bridge this gap, we present UniCSI, a unified foundation architecture that directly operates on heterogeneous CSI while preserving the integrity of the native waveform. UniCSI hinges on two core innovations: (1) a physics-informed RF tokenizer that encodes each frequency channel based on its fractional position within the physical spectrum rather than rigid array indices. It preserves intrinsic spectral coherence and enables seamless, frequency resolution-agnostic processing across arbitrary sensing configurations. (2) a spectral aggregator that distills variable-length channel sequences into a fixed-size spectral signature, effectively decoupling the feature dimensionality from the physical subcarrier spacing. Extensive evaluations on a large-scale corpus of 25 heterogeneous public datasets, spanning 14 to 2048 subcarriers, 20 to 160 MHz bandwidth, and the 2.4 and 5 GHz bands, demonstrate that native heterogeneous ingestion substantially improves cross-domain transfer under both supervised and self-supervised training schemes, particularly in regimes where fixed-grid architectures fail to generalize.

</details>


### [81] [EvoSignal: LLM-Guided Evolutionary Design of Modular Traffic Signal Control Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09563)
*Leizhen Wang,Peibo Duan,Zhenlin Qin,Yancheng Ling,Jian Xu,Yue Wang,Hao Wang,Zhenliang Ma*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: EvoSignal：LLM引导的模块化进化框架，自动设计可检查的交通信号控制程序，无需在线LLM推理，在真实路网多场景下优于20个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制策略难以自动适应动态交通需求与网络条件，人工反复设计与调整成本高；直接用LLM选择信号相位会导致决策规则黑箱化，并带来持续的推理成本与延迟。

Method: 将TSC建模为模块化程序设计问题，提出EvoSignal：利用交通知识与性能反馈进行LLM引导的进化搜索；模块化表示分离交通特征提取、局部相位优先级排序和可选的基于网络的优先级调整；从若干已有策略出发，依据拥堵与信号运行反馈迭代改进，并保留具有不同性能权衡的策略；最终程序运行时无需在线LLM推理。

Result: 在两个真实路网、五个场景的仿真中，选定的默认EvoSignal程序相比各场景中20个传统、强化学习和LLM基线的最低等待时间降低16.8%–49.2%；一个优先考虑旅行时间和排队长度的程序在搜索场景中三项指标均优于全部20个基线，并无改动迁移到其余四个场景时每项指标仍保持前三。

Conclusion: 研究支持自动设计可检查、可跨路网与交通需求迁移的控制程序，代码已开源。

Abstract: Effective traffic signal control (TSC) requires policies that respond to changing traffic demand and network conditions while meeting different control objectives. However, adapting existing strategies often involves repeated manual design and adjustment, making it difficult to systematically explore better control rules for a target network. Large language models (LLMs) can automate this process, but directly using them to select signal phases leaves decision rules embedded in black-box models and incurs recurring inference costs and latency. This paper formulates TSC as a modular program design problem and proposes EvoSignal, an LLM-guided evolutionary framework using traffic knowledge and performance feedback. The modular representation separates traffic feature extraction, local phase prioritization, and optional network-based priority adjustment. Starting from several established strategies, EvoSignal improves programs through feedback on congestion and signal operation, retaining strategies with different performance trade-offs. The resulting programs operate without online LLM inference. Simulation experiments across five scenarios on two real-world road networks show that the selected default EvoSignal program reduces waiting time by 16.8--49.2\% relative to the lowest waiting time achieved by the 20 conventional, reinforcement learning-based, and LLM-based baselines in each scenario. A program prioritizing travel time and queue length outperforms all 20 baselines on all three metrics in the search scenario and remains among the top three on each metric when transferred unchanged to the other four scenarios. These findings support automated design of inspectable control programs that transfer across the evaluated road networks and traffic demands.Code is available at https://github.com/georgewanglz2019/EvoSignal.

</details>


### [82] [Agentic AI-Assisted Modeling for Production Scheduling: Assessment in Constraint Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10184)
*Ángel Sánchez-Fernández,Javier Pernas-Álvarez,Diego Crespo-Pereira*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文评估通用大语言模型（LLM）作为智能体，在无需任务特定训练的情况下，从自然语言问题描述自动构建并实现约束规划（CP）模型的能力，并对比单智能体、多智能体与直接LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产调度优化建模依赖大量专家工作；现有LLM研究要么面向混合整数线性规划且依赖专用训练或问题特定架构，限制工业部署，要么假设优化模型已存在，缺乏从自然语言到约束规划建模与实现的端到端自动化研究。

Method: 将单智能体与多智能体架构与Model Context Protocol（MCP）服务器集成，通过上下文感知检索求解器文档以缓解实现阶段的幻觉；在六个面向工业的问题（流水车间、作业车间、柔性作业车间、资源受限仓储调度）上，使用三种LLM，与直接LLM基线比较建模准确率、执行成功率、延迟和token消耗。

Result: 当前LLM基本能够完成建模，但实现是主要瓶颈；多智能体工作流将生成脚本可直接正确运行的比例从直接LLM调用的14.8%提升至59.3%，在四个较简单问题上达到80.6%，而紧耦合的内部物流模型仍是未解决的挑战。

Conclusion: 通用LLM无需任务特定训练即可在较大程度上完成约束规划建模，多智能体编排结合MCP文档检索可显著提高实现成功率，但复杂紧耦合调度问题仍待进一步研究。

Abstract: Developing optimization models for production scheduling requires substantial expert effort. Research on large language models (LLMs) has followed two directions: specialized approaches for automated modeling, mostly for mixed-integer linear programming, which often rely on dedicated training or problem-specific architectures that limit industrial deployment; and agentic artificial intelligence for operational decision support, which generally assumes that the optimization model already exists. This study bridges both directions by assessing whether general-purpose LLMs, orchestrated as agents without task-specific training, can formulate and implement constraint programming models from natural-language problem descriptions. Singleagent and multi-agent architectures are integrated with a Model Context Protocol server that provides context-aware retrieval of solver documentation to mitigate hallucinations during implementation. Both are compared with a direct LLM baseline on six industry-oriented problems covering flow-shop, job-shop, flexible job-shop and resource-constrained warehouse scheduling, using three LLMs and assessing modeling accuracy, execution success, latency and token consumption. Formulation proves largely within reach of current LLMs, whereas implementation is the main barrier. The multi-agent workflow raises the share of scripts that run correctly as generated from 14.8% with a direct LLM call to 59.3%, reaching 80.6% on the four less complex problems, while tightly coupled intralogistics models remain an open challenge.

</details>


### [83] [Collaborative Reasoning Distillation via Cross-Feedback and Coherent Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09587)
*Taehoon Kim,Seunggeun Cho,Dongsu Han*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出协同推理蒸馏框架CRD，通过教师交互反馈、细粒度步骤质量评估和连贯步骤拼接，在预算约束下优化学生模型推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需大量计算预算或难以有效蒸馏推理能力；标准蒸馏依赖结果奖励，无法区分合理推理与侥幸猜测。

Method: CRD包含三项创新：1）教师交互式交叉反馈，迭代批评彼此推理；2）细粒度逐步质量评估，独立于最终答案捕捉逻辑有效性；3）连贯感知步骤拼接，合成互补优势。学生通过推理质量优化（RQO）在预算约束下训练。

Result: CRD-4B在MATH-500上达97.3%，AIME'25上达70.3%，超越基线，仅用50K训练样本，比可比模型数据集小至多12倍。

Conclusion: CRD能高效提升紧凑模型推理能力，在少量数据下取得优越性能。

Abstract: Reasoning capabilities are critical for advancing Large Language Models, yet current approaches either require massive computational budgets or struggle to effectively distill reasoning to smaller models. Standard distillation methods rely on outcome-based rewards, failing to distinguish between sound reasoning and lucky guesses. We propose Collaborative Reasoning Distillation (CRD), a framework that enhances reasoning in compact models through three innovations: (1) interactive cross-feedback where teachers iteratively critique each other's reasoning, (2) fine-grained step-wise quality assessment capturing logical validity independent of final answers, and (3) coherence-aware step stitching that synthesizes complementary strengths. Students are trained via Reasoning Quality Optimization (RQO) with budget constraints. Our model, CRD-4B, achieves 97.3% on MATH-500 and 70.3% on AIME'25, surpassing baselines while using only 50K training examples, up to 12 times smaller than the datasets of comparable models.

</details>


### [84] [COPC: Coupled Off-Policy Correction for Asynchronous LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09597)
*Zicheng Hu,Zhijian Zhou,Xuan Zhang,Yuchen Liu,Cheng Chen,Yuan Li,Qi Gu,Yan Feng,Hongyan Hao,Chao Qu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 针对异步强化学习中旧轨迹导致的价值估计偏差问题，提出耦合离策略校正方法 COPC，联合校正策略权重与优势估计，显著提升训练性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步RL通过解耦轨迹生成与优化加速大语言模型后训练，但训练数据来自陈旧策略。现有方法仅在actor目标中通过重要性比率控制校正token级策略不匹配，作者发现这种策略侧校正不足以消除优势估计从行为策略延续中继承的偏差，即优势陈旧性问题。

Method: 推导通用双通道actor更新的精确偏差与方差分解，揭示策略权重与优势估计误差之间的不可分离耦合：其交互产生乘性偏差项，而策略权重的平方会放大梯度方差中的优势不确定性。基于此提出COPC（耦合离策略校正），一种actor-critic方法，结合token级比率掩码与对TD残差的双侧裁剪比率加权，用于回报和优势估计。

Result: 跨陈旧水平的联合参数扫描支持耦合假设：一个校正参数的效果依赖于另一个参数，甚至可能随其反转。COPC在工具集成数学推理与搜索任务上达到最高报告性能，在每种设置下均优于最强的异步基线，并提供宽广的高性能参数区域和更好的训练稳定性。在搜索任务中，COPC全程稳定训练，而多数异步基线在训练后期崩溃。这些增益在64步策略陈旧下依然保持。COPC相对异步PPO仅增加极小步时开销，并保持相对同步PPO的1.7倍步时加速。

Conclusion: 策略侧与优势侧校正需要协同进行，COPC通过耦合校正有效解决优势陈旧性问题，在异步RL后训练中实现性能、稳定性与效率的兼顾。

Abstract: Asynchronous RL accelerates large language model post-training by decoupling rollout generation from optimization, but trains on stale trajectories. Existing methods primarily correct token-level policy mismatch through importance-ratio control in the actor objective. We show that this \emph{policy-side correction} alone is insufficient: advantage estimates also inherit mismatch from behavior-policy continuations, which we term \emph{advantage staleness}. We derive exact bias and variance decompositions for a general two-channel actor update, revealing nonseparable coupling between policy-weight and advantage-estimation errors: their interaction induces multiplicative bias terms, while squared policy weights amplify advantage uncertainty in gradient variance. This motivates the hypothesis that policy- and advantage-side correction should be coordinated. We introduce Coupled Off-Policy Correction (COPC), an actor--critic method combining token-level ratio masking with two-sided clipped-ratio weighting of TD residuals for return and advantage estimation. Joint parameter sweeps across staleness levels support this hypothesis: the effect of one correction parameter depends on, and can reverse with, the other. COPC achieves the highest reported performance on tool-integrated mathematical reasoning and search, outperforming the strongest reported asynchronous baseline in each setting. It also offers a broad high-performing parameter region and improved training stability. In search, COPC remains stable throughout training, while most evaluated asynchronous baselines collapse late in training. These gains persist at 64-step policy staleness. COPC adds minimal step-time overhead over asynchronous PPO and retains a $1.7\times$ step-time speedup over synchronous PPO.

</details>


### [85] [Lightweight and Versatile Learned Optimization by Recombination of Gradient History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09604)
*Minyoung Choi,Dalta Imam Maulana,Wanyeong Jung*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级通用学习型优化器，通过动态重组按不相交时间跨度平均的梯度历史来更新参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型优化器参数量大、训练成本高、难以泛化到未见任务；同时长梯度历史存储开销大，难以独立访问各历史梯度贡献。

Method: 将梯度历史表示为多个不相交时间跨度的平均值，预测空间简化为每个梯度平均一个标量系数并由多个参数共享；采用渐进平均旧梯度以降低长历史的内存开销，同时保持各历史贡献可独立访问。

Result: 37k参数网络在0.87 GPU小时内完成训练并零样本泛化到未见任务：BERT-Tiny与GPT-Tiny验证损失分别降低9.1%与0.4%；Vision Transformer测试准确率较Adam提升3.5个百分点，九个图模型平均提升2.7个百分点，FLOPs开销低至0.3%。

Conclusion: 该优化器以极小参数与计算开销实现跨任务零样本泛化，在多种架构上优于Adam。

Abstract: This paper presents a lightweight and versatile learned optimizer that dynamically recombines gradient history, represented as averages over disjoint time spans. The optimizer reduces the prediction space to one scalar coefficient per gradient average, shared by multiple parameters. Progressively averaging older gradients minimizes memory cost of long history, while keeping their contributions independently accessible. A 37k-parameter network trained in 0.87 GPU-hours generalizes zero-shot to unseen tasks, lowering validation loss by 9.1% and 0.4% on BERT-Tiny and GPT-Tiny, and improving test accuracy over Adam by 3.5 %p on a Vision Transformer and by 2.7 %p on average across nine graph models, with FLOPs overhead as low as 0.3%.

</details>


### [86] [Stale, Misattributed, or Late: Where Personal Memory Fails Before Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10265)
*Haonan Deng,Park Sinchaisri*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文主张在生成前评估语言智能体的个人记忆，并分离存储状态有效性、身份消解、弃权和 serving 延迟四个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统记忆评估仅看最终答案正确性，掩盖了生成前记忆块中的过期值、错误人物事实或缺失有用事实等错误，需要直接测量这些失败。

Method: 以 Personal Fact Memory 为参考层，在受控修订基准上测量时间有效性、槽位分配与身份消解；比较 participant-aware BM25 与参考排序器、四种 LLM key assigner 与规则抽取器，并在 LongMemEval 与 LoCoMo 上测试合并召回和同名暴露。

Result: 仅服务正确键控槽位的活跃值可消除过期暴露；未做更新消解时 70.3% 提示暴露被取代值。participant-aware BM25 在预设 0.02 边界内等价于参考排序器。LLM key assigner 键召回更高但干净检索率更低，LongMemEval 开放域合并召回不超过 0.062。实体后验降低受控数据同名暴露，但无法区分 LoCoMo 中同名说话者。两个冻结语言模型复现提示错误，检索延迟因排序器而异，但提示预填充主导回合级延迟。

Conclusion: 应生成前评估智能体记忆，并分别评估存储状态有效性、身份消解、弃权和 serving 延迟。

Abstract: Personal memory for language agents is usually judged by whether the final an- swer is correct. That score hides errors that arise before generation: the memory block may contain an obsolete value, a fact about the wrong person, or no use- ful fact before the serving deadline. We measure these failures directly. Using Personal Fact Memory (PFM) as a reference layer, we find that temporal validity is primarily a property of memory construction in our setting. On a controlled revision benchmark, serving only the active value of each correctly keyed slot eliminates observed stale exposure; without update resolution, 70.3% of prompts expose a superseded value. Once retrievers share the same active store and par- ticipant information, participant-aware BM25 is equivalent to the reference ranker within a prespecified 0.02 margin. The harder problem is assigning revisions to the right slot. Missed merges leave stale values active, whereas false merges silently remove current values; four LLM key assigners achieve higher key re- call than a rule extractor yet produce lower clean-retrieval rates, and open-domain merge recall on LongMemEval never exceeds 0.062. Misattribution survives va- lidity filtering: an entity posterior reduces same-name exposure on controlled data but cannot distinguish identically named speakers in LoCoMo. Two frozen lan- guage models reproduce prompt errors in generated text. Retrieval latency varies across rankers, but prompt prefill dominates turn-level latency on our hardware. These results argue for evaluating agent memory before generation, separating stored-state validity, identity resolution, abstention, and serving latency.

</details>


### [87] [When does a network's training history predict its future learning better than its current state? Evidence from a response probe and a forecasting screen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09621)
*Martin Hofmann,Patrick Mäder*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文探究神经网络的训练历史是否比其当前状态能更好地预测未来学习，通过主实验（MLP多历史训练与探针测量）和辅助实验（合成回归）发现，历史仅在当前状态尚未提供目标信息时具有预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 损失可塑性与关键期研究表明训练路径会影响后续学习，但尚不清楚路径是否携带当前状态测量所缺失的信息；因此需要检验训练历史对网络未来学习的预测能力是否优于当前状态。

Method: 主研究训练小型多层感知机于三种历史机制（42种历史），在四个检查点用短探针（在新任务上训练100次更新的网络副本）测量未来学习；先验证探针（对隐藏单元的功能保持缩放单调响应、重测一致性ICC=0.940、重初始化效应随时间消失）。辅助筛选在1,560次合成回归运行中，比较历史模型与当前验证误差对最终误差的预测。

Result: 主研究中，至多四维的历史状态未能优于当前状态的校准模型（增益-21.4%，90%区间[-91.9, 8.1]，预先要求10%）。辅助筛选中，历史模型在最多240轮中的前12轮后预测优于当前验证误差（紧凑状态30.3%，[15.8, 39.4]），48轮后两者无法区分。

Conclusion: 两项研究中，历史仅在对目标而言当前状态尚未具有信息量时提供信息；该解读为结果形成后得出。

Abstract: Networks that behave alike now can still learn differently when training continues. Work on loss of plasticity and critical periods shows that the path to a state shapes what follows; it does not show whether the path carries information that a measurement of the state itself misses. We ask when the training history of a network predicts its future learning better than its current state. In a main study, small multilayer perceptrons were trained under three history regimes (42 histories), and future learning was measured at four checkpoints by a short probe: a copy of the network trained for 100 updates on a new task. Before the prediction result was read, the protocol checked the probe. It responded monotonically to a function-preserving rescaling of hidden units, repeated measurements agreed (intraclass correlation 0.940, [0.903, 0.997], in the least reliable class, mean of three repeats), and a re-initialisation of units was visible directly after it but not 100 to 200 updates later. A history state of at most four dimensions did not improve on a calibrated model of the current state (gain -21.4%, 90% interval [-91.9, 8.1]; required in advance: 10%). A companion screen on 1,560 synthetic regression runs asked the same question for a target further away, the final error of the run. There, history models forecast better than the current validation error after 12 of up to 240 epochs (compact state 30.3%, [15.8, 39.4], a contextual comparison) and were not distinguishable from it after 48. In both studies the history was informative only while the current state was not yet informative about the target; this reading was formed after the results.

</details>


### [88] [Coding-Agent Benchmarks Should Match Their Users' Task Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09633)
*Igor Slinko,Yaroslav Golubev,Sergey Titov*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析JetBrains IDE中真实工程师的4,782个交互式编码会话，发现现有基准任务与真实任务流存在显著差异，并提出SWE-TaskFlow方法将基准任务转换为目标任务流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编码智能体评估追求真实感，但基于issue的基准任务与实际用户会话在任务类型和交互切换上差异显著，且不同交互语料的任务流互不相同，因此需要针对特定用例校准评估。

Method: 收集4,782个真实工程师的Production Sessions，聚焦至少三条用户消息的长会话（占33%）；对比三个公开交互语料的长会话样本，分析任务流（会话长度、任务类型及类型间转移分布）；提出SWE-TaskFlow，通过提示拆分和可验证仓库QA将issue基准转换为目标任务流，并用任务流对齐分数（TFAS）选择生成轨迹。

Result: 长会话与issue基准任务显著不同：用户请求涵盖更广泛的任务类型（代码问答、规划、评审、重构、执行），且会话中任务类型不断切换；不同公开语料的任务流差异明显。在700个SWE-Bench Pro任务试点中，顺序多步求解任务使智能体成本约翻倍，但解决率无稳定变化。

Conclusion: 不存在普遍真实的交互分布，基准应明确目标用例并据此校准；交互协议本身是评估的重要维度，SWE-TaskFlow可将基准任务流对齐到目标场景。

Abstract: The evaluation of coding agents generally strives to be as realistic as possible. In our study, we collect 4,782 agent sessions of real software engineers in JetBrains IDEs, which we call Production Sessions. Since our subject is interactive agents, we study the sessions with at least three user messages (33% of the sample). These long sessions differ from issue-derived benchmark tasks in two ways: (i) user requests span a far wider mix of task types - questions about the project's code, planning, review, refactoring, execution - and (ii) users switch between types throughout a session. Long-session samples from three public interaction corpora exhibit markedly different Task Flows (the distributions of session lengths, task types, and type-to-type transitions), so no single interaction distribution is universally realistic: benchmarks should name a target use case and calibrate to measurements from it. We present SWE-TaskFlow, an approach for transforming any issue-derived benchmark: it preserves the verified tasks and tests while steering the interaction toward a target Task Flow through prompt splitting and verifiable repository QA, with a TaskFlow Alignment Score (TFAS) for selecting among generated trajectories. In a pilot on 700 SWE-Bench Pro tasks, solving the task sequentially in several steps approximately doubles agent cost without a stable change in resolve rate: the interaction protocol itself is an important dimension of evaluation.

</details>


### [89] [RunningTab: Direct Workspace Interaction with Environment-Side Tabs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10444)
*Jinheon Baek,Soyeong Jeong,Yumin Choi,Dongsu Han,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出 RunningTab 框架，为 LLM 智能体的直接工作区交互（DWI）增加环境侧任务记录标签，跟踪任务需求、已读文件摘要与未打开候选文件，从而提升交付物完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识工作常需从工作区已有文件生成新交付物，LLM 智能体开始接手此类任务；直接通过终端搜索读取文件（DWI）虽能触达文件，但上下文窗口无法记录任务要求、已读内容及列出未打开的文件，导致遗漏关键内容（如提取了图表却未纳入报告）。

Method: RunningTab 在环境侧维护每任务标签：智能体添加需求，环境将每次读取文件记录为带来源的摘录、将列出但未打开的文件记录为候选；智能体可查看每个需求与其最佳匹配摘录及顶部未打开候选，据此解决需求或附理由搁置，若完成时仍有未解决需求则触发完成检查。

Result: 在三个基准、三个 LLM 上验证，RunningTab 持续优于普通 DWI 以及将记录保存在模型内的基线方法，且其标签通常能保留交付物所需的数值。

Conclusion: 环境侧任务标签能有效弥补 DWI 的上下文遗忘问题，提升智能体从多文件生成交付物的完整性与可靠性。

Abstract: Much knowledge work produces new deliverables from files a workspace already holds, and LLM agents are beginning to take such work over. Through direct corpus interaction, an agent can search and read any of those files from a terminal with no indexing, and producing a deliverable from many of them in this way is what we call direct workspace interaction (DWI). Reaching the files, however, is only half the task: nothing keeps track of what the task asks for, what has been read, and what was listed but never opened, all of which slip through the context window without leaving a trace, so an agent may extract a figure and still deliver a report without it. To address this, we present RunningTab, a framework that equips direct workspace interaction with an environment-side tab: a per-task record of what the task still owes, kept by the environment alongside the agent. Specifically, the agent adds its requirements, while the environment records every file read as an excerpt with its provenance and every listed but unopened file as a candidate; the agent can then see each requirement beside its best-matching excerpts and top unopened candidates, resolve it against matching content or set it aside with a reason, and, should it try to finish with requirements still open, receive them in a finish check. We validate RunningTab on three benchmarks with three LLMs, where it consistently outperforms plain DWI and baselines that keep the record in the model, while its tab usually holds the values a deliverable needs once seen.

</details>


### [90] [EmbodiedRSI: Active Continual Robot Learning Through Hypothesis-Guided Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10498)
*Python Song,Zhixuan Liang,Kelsey Fu,Mengdi Wang,Junfeng Yang,Shilong Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出EmbodiedRSI，一种自进化智能体框架，通过快慢双系统架构与假设图高效选择物理实验，实现代码与技能协同进化，显著提升机器人基础模型在新物体位置或任务指令下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型在物体位置或任务指令变化时性能下降，而通过遥操作等收集大量机器人数据进行后训练成本高昂；现有自进化框架在决定代码和技能变更时对机器人试验利用效率低。

Method: EmbodiedRSI采用快慢双系统架构，在假设图中维护竞争的代码与技能假设；利用信息价值实验选择挑选能区分假设的物理实验，其结果指导代码-技能协同进化；慢系统构建分层记忆，并通过奖励基记忆学习选择对后续快系统改进有价值的记忆。

Result: 在RoboCasa365上达到77.0%总体成功率和71.3%的Composite-Unseen成功率，最佳基线为40.1%；在LIBERO-Pro上达到86.8%总体成功率；零样本迁移到真实机器人，在多个挑战任务上达到71.3%总体成功率。

Conclusion: EmbodiedRSI能高效利用物理交互自主探索并改进代码与技能，在仿真基准和真实机器人上均显著优于基线，展示了自进化智能体框架在提升机器人基础模型适应性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Robot foundation models provide strong visuomotor control, yet their performance can degrade when object positions or task instructions change. Further improvements often require post-training on substantial robot data, which can be costly to collect through methods such as teleoperation. Agentic harnesses can adapt around the model, but current self-evolving harnesses use robot trials inefficiently when deciding which code and skill changes to pursue. We introduce EmbodiedRSI, a self-evolving agentic harness that autonomously decides where to explore next and turns the resulting physical interaction into improved code and skills. EmbodiedRSI realizes this through a Fast-Slow Dual-System Architecture, in which competing code and skill hypotheses are maintained in a Hypothesis Graph. Value-of-Information Experiment Selection chooses physical experiments that can distinguish these hypotheses. Their outcomes guide Code-Skill Co-Evolution. The Slow System builds Hierarchical Memory, and Reward-Grounded Memory Learning selects effective memory according to their value for later Fast-System improvement. On RoboCasa365, EmbodiedRSI reaches 77.0% overall success and 71.3% on Composite-Unseen, compared with 40.1% for the best baseline. EmbodiedRSI also reaches 86.8% overall success on LIBERO-Pro. Beyond benchmark performance, EmbodiedRSI transfers zero-shot to real-world robot, achieving 71.3% overall success across multiple challenging tasks.

</details>


### [91] [CERO: Where and When to Allocate Rollouts for RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09679)
*Yiming Zong,Yige Wang,Xing Hu,Jiashuo Jiang,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CERO，一种在线原始-对偶调度器，在整个训练周期内协调有限的rollout预算用于提示选择和预算节奏控制，在多个数学推理基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的组相对强化学习自适应rollout方法在每次更新时对提示分配固定的预算，缺乏对整体训练周期内有限rollout预算的协调机制。

Method: 将问题建模为累积提示暴露的凹代理效用，引入CERO在线原始-对偶调度器，通过紧凑的Fenchel表示线性化对累积暴露的依赖，并使用投影在线梯度下降更新提示特定的支持斜率和共享预算价格，利用奖励变化反馈和预算偏差进行自适应调整。

Result: 在匹配训练响应预算下，CERO在三个骨干模型和五个数学推理基准上均取得最高的avg@16宏平均；机制分析将提示选择与组内奖励对比关联，多随机种子消融显示自适应节奏优于均匀和预设支出计划。

Conclusion: CERO通过在线原始-对偶调度有效协调训练周期内的rollout预算，自适应选择提示、重访频率和每轮生成组数，实现了优于固定策略的性能，并建立了路径级理论保证。

Abstract: Adaptive rollout methods for group-relative reinforcement learning typically allocate a fixed per-update budget across prompts. We instead study how to coordinate a finite rollout budget over the entire training horizon. We formulate this problem using a concave surrogate utility of cumulative prompt exposure and introduce CERO, an online primal dual scheduler for prompt admission and budget pacing. In our experiments, each admitted prompt receives a fixed-size response group. CERO instead adapts which prompts are selected, how often they are revisited across rounds, and how many groups are generated in each round. A compact Fenchel representation linearizes the dependence on cumulative exposure, while projected online gradient descent updates prompt-specific supporting slopes and a shared budget price using reward-variation feedback and budget deviations. We establish pathwise guarantees for the surrogate allocation objective against fixed-rate and same-path time-varying benchmarks, with explicit terms for proxy discrepancy and rate variation. Under matched training-response budgets, CERO attains the highest avg@16 macro-average on each of three backbones across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Mechanistic analyses link CERO's prompt choices to within-group reward contrast, while multi-seed ablations show gains from adaptive pacing over both uniform and preset spending schedules.

</details>


### [92] [RECAST: Learning to Compute the Right Context through Adaptive Evidence Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10507)
*Yilun Hao,Krishna Sayana,Isabella Ye,James S Ren,Sukhdeep Sodhi,Craig Boutilier,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出RECAST框架，将证据构建建模为对异构检索与计算操作的序贯决策过程，使证据可被主动推导而非仅检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG依赖固定相似度检索，智能体变体虽能调整查询与工具但仍以检索为中心；许多任务所需证据不在单一来源中，而需跨多条来源进行过滤、聚合或计算推导。

Method: 轻量RouterLM迭代选择并构造原语操作或为冻结的CompilerLM指定定制操作以翻译为可执行代码，判定证据充分后交给冻结的AnswerLM生成最终答案；训练采用监督微调（SFT）后接组相对策略优化（GRPO）。

Result: 在六个异构基准族上平均成功率达75.6%，超过最强大型模型基线15.9%；训练使Qwen3.5-9B RouterLM比免训练的Gemini 3.5 Flash RouterLM高5.0%；在三个留出基准上平均超过最强基线15.0%。

Conclusion: RECAST通过可学习的证据推导与操作路由，显著优于检索中心基线，并在任务与异构来源表示上展现强零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly applied to tasks grounded in long, heterogeneous information sources. Conventional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) relies on fixed similarity-based retrieval, while agentic variants adapt queries and tool use but remain largely retrieval-centric. However, in many tasks, the evidence required for a solution is not explicitly present in any single source item. Instead, it must be derived through filtering, aggregation, or computation across multiple source items. In this work, we introduce RECAST (Routing Evidence through Computation, Access, and Synthesized Tools), a learned framework that formulates evidence construction as a sequential decision process over heterogeneous retrieval and computation operations, allowing evidence to be actively derived rather than merely retrieved. A lightweight RouterLM iteratively selects and formulates primitive operations or specifies customized operations for a frozen CompilerLM to translate into executable code. Once it judges the evidence sufficient, RouterLM passes the accepted evidence to a frozen AnswerLM to produce the final solution. We train RouterLM with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by group relative policy optimization (GRPO). Across six heterogeneous benchmark families, RECAST achieves a mean success rate of 75.6%, outperforming the strongest large-model baseline by 15.9%. Moreover, training enables the Qwen3.5-9B RouterLM to outperform a training-free Gemini 3.5 Flash RouterLM by 5.0%. On three held-out benchmarks, RECAST improves over the strongest baseline by 15.0% on average, demonstrating strong zero-shot generalization across tasks and heterogeneous source representations.

</details>


### [93] [RoboJEPA: Scaling Robotic Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10515)
*Artem Zholus,Nicolas Beltran-Velez,Jianhao Yuan,Sarath Chandar,Tushar Nagarajan,Daniel Severo,Koustuv Sinha,Michal Drozdzal,Adriana Romero Soriano,Jeannette Bohg,Nicolas Ballas,Mahmoud Assran*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboJEPA，一种基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的世界模型，在涵盖12种机器人形态的大规模数据集上训练，首次建立了多形态机器人世界模型的缩放定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型在预测未来状态和规划方面表现出色，但缺乏原则性方法来估计其能力如何随模型规模、数据和计算量扩展，这一问题阻碍了领域进展。

Method: 构建RoboJEPA，基于JEPA架构，在12种机器人形态的大规模数据集上训练；分析其想象误差（潜在展开的误差）与计算量的关系，并评估下游机器人规划性能。

Result: 想象误差随计算量呈二阶幂律关系，可预测远超拟合尺度的模型质量；下游规划性能随计算量可预测地提升，且想象误差与其强相关，可作为真实机器人评估的可靠代理；潜在世界模型可零样本部署为机器人智能体，通过单一目标图像规划完成真实硬件上的长时程任务。

Conclusion: 首次为基于真实机器人数据训练的多形态机器人世界模型建立缩放定律；RoboJEPA（80亿参数）是迄今最大的JEPA预测器模型，所有模型检查点及训练与部署代码已开源。

Abstract: Latent world models have shown a remarkable ability to predict future states and to plan in the real world. In practice, however, we lack a principled way to estimate how their capabilities scale with model size, data, and compute, an open problem that slows progress in the field. In this work we present RoboJEPA, a world model based on the Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) and trained on a large-scale dataset spanning 12 robotic embodiments. We show that RoboJEPA's imagination error, the error of its latent rollouts, follows a second-order power law in compute, allowing us to predict model quality well beyond the scale at which the law is fit. We further show that downstream robotic planning performance improves predictably with compute, and that imagination error is strongly correlated with it, making it a reliable proxy for real-robot evaluation. Finally, we demonstrate that latent world models can be deployed zero-shot as robotic agents, planning toward a single goal image to solve tasks requiring long-horizon planning on real hardware. We release all model checkpoints together with our training and robot deployment code. To our knowledge, this is the first work to establish scaling laws for multi-embodiment robotic world models trained on real robot data, and RoboJEPA, at 8B parameters, is the largest JEPA predictor model trained to date.

</details>


### [94] [EC-EarthFlow: Probabilistic emulation of daily transient global climate model simulations with flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09715)
*Kirien Whan,Nikolaj T. Mücke,Karin van der Wiel*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出EC-EarthFlow生成式流匹配模型，以低计算成本复现EC-Earth3气候模拟的温度场，支持自回归长时预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理气候模型（如EC-Earth3）计算成本极高，需要快速且能保持统计与物理特性的替代模型。

Method: 基于EC-Earth3的瞬变模拟（1950-2166，SSP2-4.5）训练流匹配生成模型，输入前几日温度场及年均温度，自回归预测次日温度场，rollout周期为一个月至延伸季节。

Result: 仅使用温度变量即可复现EC-Earth3的日变率、空间格局、年循环和长期趋势，计算成本显著降低；长时间推理稳定，并能学习EC-Earth3模拟的物理关系。

Conclusion: EC-EarthFlow可作为高效稳定的气候模拟替代模型，在低计算成本下保持关键气候特征与物理关系。

Abstract: We introduce EC-EarthFlow, a generative flow matching model that emulates simulations from the physical climate model EC-Earth3. The model is trained on transient simulations from EC-Earth3 (1950-2166, SSP2-4.5) to predict the day ahead temperature field from the previous days temperature as well as annual mean temperature. Predictions are made auto-regressively with rollout periods of between a month and an extended season. Using only this variable of interest, we are able to reproduce the daily variability, spatial patterns, annual cycle and long-term trend from EC-Earth3 at a substantially lower computational cost than the physical model. We demonstrate that EC-EarthFlow is stable for long inference periods, and that it can learn the physical relationships as simulated in EC-Earth3.

</details>


### [95] [EntroPrefill: Renyi-Guided Context Pruning with Conditional Stability Guarantees for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09757)
*Inbasekaran S*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出EntroPrefill，一种基于Renyi引导的预填充剪枝方案，并给出其理论约束、可行性上界与扰动界，但未报告实验加速或精度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预填充阶段剪枝可减少深层Transformer处理的序列长度，但仅凭注意力集中不能证明被丢弃上下文可舍弃，需要显式约束丢弃的注意力质量。

Method: 将EntroPrefill构建为Renyi引导的提议机制，并对丢弃注意力质量施加显式约束；采用汇点隔离、正则化的头池化以兼容分组查询注意力；推导混合到头删除包络、可行token删除的可计算上界、自适应选择剪枝层时仍有效的有限样本观察者保证，以及带显式充分Lipschitz常数的条件Transformer扰动界和首token决策边际推论。

Result: 给出上述理论结果与反例，说明仅凭浅层观察无法推出无条件未来输出保证；系统分析区分查询头并集、物理页分配和KV传输负载，并给出剪枝的算术盈亏平衡条件。

Conclusion: 本文为理论性工作，定义流程、假设与形式化极限，不报告实测加速或任务精度保持，实验留待后续验证假设、近似紧性和端到端资源权衡。

Abstract: Mid-prefill pruning can reduce the sequence processed by deeper transformer layers, but attention concentration alone does not certify that discarded context is dispensable. We formulate EntroPrefill as a Renyi-guided proposal mechanism coupled to explicit constraints on discarded attention mass. Sink-isolated, regularized head pooling respects grouped-query attention while exposing a quantitative trade-off between specialization and worst-head coverage. We derive a mixture-to-head deletion envelope, a computable upper bound on feasible token removal, and a finite-sample observer guarantee that remains valid when the pruning layer is selected adaptively. We then establish a conditional transformer perturbation bound with explicit sufficient Lipschitz constants and a first-token decision-margin corollary. A counterexample shows why shallow observations alone cannot imply an unconditional future-output guarantee. The systems analysis distinguishes query-head unions, physical page allocation, and KV-transfer payload, and gives an arithmetic break-even condition for pruning. This manuscript is theoretical in scope: it defines the procedure, its assumptions, and its formal limits, but does not report measured acceleration or task-accuracy preservation. Experiments are reserved for subsequent validation of the assumptions, approximation tightness, and end-to-end resource trade-offs.

</details>


### [96] [Unrolled Flow Models for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09759)
*Faissal Izermine,Hanru Bai,Oscar Davis,T. Konstantin Rusch*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文从理论与实证两方面探讨了流匹配模型在推理任务中增加积分步数能否提升性能，并提出了训练与稳定化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配语言模型虽能少步生成，但增加积分步数对推理任务的增益不明确，作者旨在揭示其理论能力与实际限制。

Method: 证明两层Transformer参数化的流可解图可达性，所需步数随目标距根距离增加；提出在[0,1]随机子区间上对模型自身潜在轨迹进行端到端训练，并将潜在状态投影到球面以稳定长轨迹动力学，同时采用无参数选择分数从多次采样中选择。

Result: 在ProsQA上准确率达97%，性能随积分步数增加而提升；在Sudoku和Maze等长轨迹任务上，球面稳定化带来显著增益，超越参数量三倍以上的基线；多次采样结合无参数选择分数可进一步提升性能，但长答案的可靠选择仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了流模型用于推理的理论基础，并表明轨迹训练、稳定潜在动力学和轨迹选择有助于在实际中实现该能力。

Abstract: Flow matching enables language generation in few steps, but whether additional integration steps improve reasoning remains unclear. We prove that a flow parameterized by a two-layer Transformer can solve graph reachability, with the required number of integration steps increasing with the target's distance from the root. Yet, standard flow language models can fail to benefit from additional steps on reasoning tasks. We attribute this limitation to objectives that supervise each time point independently, without explicitly training successive steps to build on one another. To address this, we instead train through the model's own latent rollout over a randomly sampled subinterval of [0, 1], decoding only at the endpoint. On ProsQA, this raises accuracy to 97% and enables performance to improve with additional integration steps. For the longer rollouts required by reasoning tasks such as Sudoku and Maze, retracting the latent state onto a sphere stabilizes the dynamics and yields substantial gains over baselines with more than three times as many parameters. Sampling multiple rollouts further improves performance when paired with a parameter-free selection score, although reliable selection remains challenging for longer answers. Together, these results establish a theoretical basis for reasoning with flows and show how rollout training, stable latent dynamics, and rollout selection help realize this capacity in practice.

</details>


### [97] [Leaner Transformers Can Easily Learn to Cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09760)
*Charlotte Park,Kenneth L. Clarkson,Lior Horesh,Takuya Ito,Parikshit Ram*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文改进了Transformer执行Lloyd's k-means算法的嵌入尺寸，并研究了学习该算法的收敛性、泛化及泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作表明Transformer能以嵌入尺寸d+k精确执行Lloyd算法，但需要(d+k)^2的注意力投影矩阵。本文旨在构建更小嵌入尺寸的Transformer，并探究学习聚类算法的理论性质与泛化能力。

Method: 首先设计嵌入尺寸为d+⌈log2 k⌉的等表达力Transformer执行Lloyd算法；然后在聚类任务分布上训练这些Transformer，理论刻画并实验验证影响随机梯度学习算法收敛和分布内泛化的因素；最后探测学习到的Transformer的泛化聚类能力。

Result: 提出了更小嵌入尺寸的Transformer；理论刻画并实验验证了影响收敛和分布内泛化的因素；揭示了学习算法在哪些情况下成功或失败。

Conclusion: Transformer可以以更紧凑的嵌入尺寸执行Lloyd算法，且基于随机梯度的学习算法的收敛与泛化受特定因素影响，其泛化聚类能力存在成功与失败的边界。

Abstract: Transformers have in-context learning capabilities, where some known learning algorithms can be executed in the forward pass through the model. Recent work shows that transformers can exactly perform Lloyd's algorithm for $k$-means clustering with $n$ points in $d$ dimensions with an embedding size $d_{\textsf{emb}} = d+k$ (thus, requiring attention projection matrices of size $(d+k)^2$). In this work, we build upon this result in the following ways: First, we present an equally expressive but smaller transformer that executes Lloyd's algorithm with embedding size $d_{\textsf{emb}} = (d + \lceil \log_2 k \rceil)$. Next, we train these transformers to learn the clustering algorithms given a distribution of clustering tasks, and theoretically characterize and empirically validate the factors affecting the convergence and in-distribution generalization of learning algorithms based on stochastic gradients. Finally, we probe the general clustering abilities of these learned algorithms (in the form of transformers), and try to understand situations where they succeed and fail.

</details>


### [98] [BoT-GRPO: Efficient Process-Reward RL for Reasoning via Bag-of-Token Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09804)
*Yingxiang Yang,Weihang Xiao,Zhunxuan Wang,Joshua Flashner,Niresh Agarwal*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出BoT-GRPO，将GRPO扩展到token级奖励模型，通过长度不变'词袋'聚合实现无价值网络的过程监督，加速收敛并提升最终质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO中rollout的每个token获得相同优势，过程监督效率低；希望在无价值网络成本下加速收敛并提升最终质量。

Method: BoT-GRPO收集所有rollout的token级奖励，按源序列长度的倒数加权，并相对加权组统计量计算逐token优势；无需critic，可在有token级奖励时直接替代GRPO。

Result: React前端代码生成：编译率达80%，比GRPO快至多1.9倍，收敛快于GSPO、DAPO、PURE，最终编译率和VLM判定胜率更高；AIME数学推理：Pass@k绝对增益最高8.1%，步数减半。还比较了Qwen2.5-3B、SmolLM3-3B、Phi-4-mini-reasoning等推理与非推理基座。

Conclusion: 奖励稳定性比丰富性更重要：干净、有界、稳定的细粒度信号能持续加速学习，而噪声较大的替代方案会停滞。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is now central to eliciting reasoning in large language models, while in the popular algorithm Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) every token in a rollout receives the same advantage. We ask how to make process supervision efficient: accelerating convergence and improving final quality without the cost of value networks. We propose Bag-of-Tokens Group Relative Policy Optimization (BoT-GRPO), which extends GRPO to token-level reward models through a length-invariant "bag of tokens" aggregation: it collects all token-level rewards across rollouts, weights each by the inverse of its source sequence length, and computes per-token advantages relative to weighted group statistics. BoT-GRPO is critic-free, and is a drop-in replacement wherever GRPO is used when token-level reward is available. On React front-end code generation, BoT-GRPO reaches $80\%$ compile rate up to $1.9\times$ faster than GRPO and converges faster than modern GRPO variants (GSPO, DAPO, PURE) while reaching higher final compile and VLM-judged win rates. On a second task, AIME mathematical reasoning, BoT-GRPO delivers absolute Pass@$k$ gains up to $8.1\%$ over GRPO in half the steps. For both tasks we compare the algorithm's performance on reasoning vs. non-reasoning base-model families (Qwen2.5-3B, SmolLM3-3B, Phi-4-mini-reasoning). Our experiments also yield a practical recipe for the reward model itself: reward stability matters more than richness: clean, bounded, stable fine-grained signals consistently accelerate learning where noisier alternatives stall.

</details>


### [99] [Dual-QK: Sharp Queries and Flat Keys for Prunable 2-bit KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09827)
*Sunjoo Whang,Jungjun Oh,Minsung Kim,Dongho Seo,Jisu Shin,Gregory Kielian,Hoi-Jun Yoo,Sangjin Kim*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Dual-QK，通过配对非正交查询和键变换，同时实现低比特KV缓存量化和查询通道剪枝，在长上下文下保持精度并大幅压缩和加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长输入和长生成增加KV缓存存储和访问成本。低比特量化降低存储和内存流量，查询通道剪枝减少键缓存读取。但基于旋转的量化为了保持查询-键点积不变，需对查询施加相同正交变换，这会分散查询能量，削弱可剪枝小分量与需保留大分量的分离。

Method: Dual-QK使用配对的非正交查询与键变换，基于校准的查询和键统计，结合部分键白化与查询对齐基，平衡键尺度以支持INT2量化并集中查询能量以进行动态通道剪枝。还引入通道0保护和桶相对RoPE，以在长上下文中保持低比特精度。

Result: 在四个模型、五个生成基准和长上下文检索任务上，40%查询通道稀疏度下，大多数任务精度优于OSCAR。128K上下文时，KV缓存压缩6.8倍，KV读取量估计减少8.3倍（相对未剪枝BF16）。SGLang实现解码吞吐量最高达未剪枝BF16的3.75倍。

Conclusion: Dual-QK有效解决了旋转量化与查询剪枝之间的冲突，在长上下文下实现了高压缩比、高吞吐量且精度优于基线。

Abstract: Long inputs and extended generation increase the storage and access costs of the key-value (KV) cache. Low-bit quantization reduces storage and memory traffic, while query-channel pruning can further reduce key-cache reads. Rotation-based quantization redistributes the energy of key outliers across channels. To maintain computational invariance, the same orthogonal transform must be applied to queries, preserving query-key dot products. However, this rotation can disperse query energy, weakening the separation between a few large components to retain and many small ones to prune. We introduce Dual-QK, which uses paired non-orthogonal query and key transforms to address this conflict. Using calibrated query and key statistics, Dual-QK combines partial key whitening with a query-aligned basis to balance key scales for INT2 quantization and concentrate query energy for dynamic channel pruning. Channel-0 protection and bucket-relative RoPE support low-bit accuracy over long contexts. Experiments on four models across five generative benchmarks and long-context retrieval tasks show improved accuracy over OSCAR on most tasks at 40% query-channel sparsity. At a 128K context, Dual-QK provides $6.8\times$ KV-cache compression and an estimated $8.3\times$ reduction in KV read volume relative to unpruned BF16. Under the evaluated configurations, our SGLang implementation achieves up to $3.75\times$ the decoding throughput of unpruned BF16.

</details>


### [100] [Fully Interpretable Minimal Transformers: From Geometry to Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09838)
*Raneem Mahajne,Toviah Moldwin*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一个构建和解释极简Transformer模型的框架，通过将嵌入维度和头大小限制为2，实现内部表示的全二维可视化，并展示模型在特定任务上的逐步算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer如何利用信息几何实现下一词预测，并提供一个教学和实验平台以直观理解其内部计算机制。

Method: 将Transformer的嵌入维度和注意力头大小限制为2，训练模型完成一项简单任务：在数字序列中出现'+'运算符时输出最近观察到的偶数。训练后，通过二维可视化逐步骤展示嵌入、Q/K/V变换、注意力矩阵形成、残差流及决策边界，并引入一套可解释性可视化工具。

Result: 成功可视化了模型内部表示的全过程：令牌及其位置嵌入、Q/K/V矩阵变换、Q与K点积形成注意力矩阵、注意力选择值移动表示到输出层区域以正确预测下一词。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解Transformer如何利用信息几何进行下一词预测提供了教学和实验测试平台，表明学习到的几何结构隐含了算法步骤。

Abstract: We present a framework for building and interpreting minimal transformer models. By constraining a transformer's embedding dimension and head size to 2, we enable full two-dimensional visualization of its internal representations. Embeddings, query/key/value transforms, attention outputs, residual streams, and decision boundaries can all be seen directly. Our central claim is that the learned geometry implies an algorithm; the arrangement of points and boundaries in R^2 can be read as a step-by-step procedure. We train a transformer on a simple task where it must produce the most recently observed even number whenever the '+' operator appears in a sequence of digits. Once trained, we visually walk through every step of the transformer's computation. We show how the model embeds the tokens and their respective positions in the sequence, transforms them via the Q, K, and V matrices, uses the dot product between the Q and K representations to form the attention matrix, and uses the attention matrix to select values that move the representation of each input token to the region of the domain of the output layer that will correctly predict the next token. We introduce a suite of interpretability visualizations that make the algorithmic interpretation of this procedure explicit. Our framework offers a pedagogical and experimental testbed to explore how transformers use informational geometry to implement next-token prediction.

</details>


### [101] [For Those Who Believe in Faithfulness: Optimizing the Area Under Insertion and Deletion Curves for Ranking Relative Feature Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09844)
*Bjørn Leth Møller,Bulat Ibragimov,Christian Igel*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于忠实性目标函数的归因方法Ra-NEM，通过随机化损失近似梯度，结合神经解释掩码框架，实现高效、准确的模型解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在社会相关任务中需要有效的可解释AI方法，特别是归因方法，而现有归因质量评估依赖插入/删除曲线下的面积，需要一种直接优化忠实性的方法。

Method: 从忠实性概念推导目标函数并近似其梯度；建立插入曲线与top-k特征选择的联系，得到衡量归因质量的损失函数；通过随机化损失高效近似梯度；将该损失与神经解释掩码框架结合，形成Ra-NEM，适用于任何可微模型且不影响模型性能。

Result: 实验表明，Ra-NEM能稳健且高效地提供准确归因；与其他算法相比，归因不仅忠实性更高，在其他XAI指标上也表现良好；推理速度快，适合在线应用。

Conclusion: Ra-NEM是一种有效、高效且通用的归因方法，通过优化忠实性损失提升解释质量，并具备在线部署潜力。

Abstract: The adoption of machine learning for socially relevant tasks requires effective explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) methods to better understand the behavior of machine learning models. Attribution methods are a popular XAI approach in which input-output relationships are characterized by heat maps that reflect the relative importance of input features for a particular prediction. The quality of such maps is often assessed by measuring faithfulness based on the area under insertion and deletion curves, which measures changes in the model output as features are added and removed. In this study, we derive an objective function from this notion of faithfulness and a way to approximate its gradient. We establish the connection between insertion curves and top-$k$ feature selection, which leads to a loss function measuring the quality of attributions. Randomization of the loss allows us to efficiently approximate its gradient. To show the effectiveness of the general approach, we combine the loss function with the neural explanation mask framework. The resulting method, termed Ra-NEM, can be used with any differentiable model without affecting the model's performance. Experiments demonstrate that Ra-NEM provides accurate attributions robustly and efficiently. Compared to other algorithms, the attributions have not only higher faithfulness but also perform well in terms of other XAI metrics. The high inference speed of Ra-NEM makes the method suitable for online applications. The code is available online: https://github.com/baerminator/Ra_Nem

</details>


### [102] [MUNITE: Unified Multimodal Latent Inference for Any-to-Any Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09866)
*Kyeongmin Yeo,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: MUNITE 是一个统一潜在变量框架，将编码与潜在生成视为同一推断问题在不同观测证据量下的表现，实现灵活的多模态任意到任意生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态生成方法难以统一处理任意模态子集输入与缺失模态下的生成，编码与生成通常被割裂，且不完整训练样本难以学习条件分布。

Method: 基于单一条件流模型，对完整观测的潜在表示建模条件分布；完整观测退化为确定性编码，无观测对应潜在边缘分布，中间子集为条件潜在推断。共享潜在样本保证跨目标一致性，模态专用解码器建模剩余不确定性。通过自蒸馏扩展条件流匹配：在相同中间潜在状态下，用富证据预测监督小证据子集的条件预测，当富证据轨迹满足精确条件流时，学习信号等价于全目标去噪。

Result: 在 PolyMNIST-D-Q、FFHQ64 与 image-text-audio 上取得有竞争力或更优的生成质量与源目标对齐，联合生成一致性更高；在所有一对多与无条件 image-text-audio 比较中取得最高一致性。

Conclusion: 统一潜在推断能有效应对多样多模态设置，验证了 MUNITE 在任意到任意多模态生成中的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce MUNITE, a latent-variable framework for flexible any-to-any multimodal generation that treats encoding and latent generation as the same inference problem under different amounts of observed evidence. Given any subset of modalities, MUNITE models the conditional distribution over the latent representation associated with the complete observation. Full observation recovers deterministic encoding, no observation recovers the latent marginal, and intermediate subsets define conditional latent inference, all within a single conditional flow model. A shared latent sample captures variation that must remain consistent across generated targets, while modality-specific generative decoders model the remaining uncertainty independently. To learn these conditional distributions from incomplete training examples, we extend conditional flow matching through self-distillation: predictions conditioned on richer available observations supervise the same model conditioned on smaller subsets at the same intermediate latent state. When the richer-evidence trajectory follows the exact conditional flow, this provides the same expected learning signal as full-target denoising. Across PolyMNIST-D-Q, FFHQ64, and image-text-audio, MUNITE achieves competitive or better generation quality and source-target alignment, with higher joint-generation coherence. In particular, it attains the highest coherence in all one-to-many and unconditional image-text-audio comparisons, showing the effectiveness of unified latent inference across diverse multimodal settings.

</details>


### [103] [RollVerify: Bridging Efficiency and Accuracy in Long-Tail Rollout Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09914)
*Yongqiang Yao,Jinru Tan,Kaihuan Liang,Zixin Yin,Yazhe Niu,Ruihao Gong,Dahua Lin,Ningyi Xu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出RollVerify框架，通过主动验证和修复部分展开轨迹中的离策略偏差，在保持异步/部分展开效率的同时达到接近在策略训练的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习依赖大量展开，长尾展开导致GPU气泡；异步或部分展开虽提升吞吐但引入陈旧离策略样本，现有重加权方法仍有性能差距。

Method: 定义离策略偏移度量OPS量化部分生成轨迹的偏差，以OPS约束指导序列级和词元级验证，识别并截断无效后缀，从而在训练前主动修复样本。

Result: 在数学和工具辅助数学推理实验中，RollVerify精度与在策略训练相当，同时降低训练成本；代码生成结果提供初步证据。

Conclusion: 主动验证修复部分展开样本可兼顾效率与精度，为可扩展强化学习提供轻量方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is crucial for improving large language models' reasoning and generalization. It relies on massive rollouts whose lengths become increasingly long-tailed as context windows grow. In on-policy training, these long-tail rollouts can result in GPU bubbles, reducing system utilization and limiting RL scalability. Asynchronous or partial-rollout methods improve throughput by relaxing synchronization, but inevitably introduce stale off-policy samples (trajectories) that may hurt final accuracy. Existing approaches mainly mitigate this off-policy issue by reweighting off-policy samples during training, yet they can still leave a performance gap compared to fully on-policy training. In this work, rather than passively reweighting samples during training, we propose RollVerify, a lightweight RL framework built on partial rollout that actively verifies and repairs samples before they enter training. Specifically, it introduces an off-policy shift metric OPS, to quantify the off-policy deviation of partially generated trajectories. Guided by the OPS constraint, RollVerify performs both sequence-level and token-level verification to identify and truncate invalid suffixes of trajectories. This yields high-quality samples that protect the models' accuracy while preserving the efficiency gains of partial rollout. Experiments on mathematical and tool-assisted mathematical reasoning show that RollVerify achieves accuracy comparable to on-policy training while reducing training cost. Additional code-generation results provide preliminary evidence beyond mathematics.

</details>


### [104] [NeuralZip: Reusable Setup for Fast Lossless Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09916)
*Martín Bravo,Samuel Horváth,Gonzalo Navarro,Andrés Abeliuk*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出NeuralZip无损压缩方法，通过一次性预训练并复用指数统计结构，实现快速且可迁移的模型权重压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无损压缩可减少模型权重的存储和传输，但重复的统计分析和编码构造带来计算开销。研究是否可一次性准备指数统计结构并复用。

Method: NeuralZip将具有相似指数分布的块分组，共享Huffman码，并选择性地用打包指数表示重复指数元组。在后续编码前进行设置选择表示，编码时仍处理当前张量值。设置可预计算并从兼容架构迁移。

Result: 在浮点模型检查点中，设置后压缩比基线快1.81-21.33倍，实现精确逐位重建。迁移设置保持相似压缩率，避免摊销设置成本。训练检查点随权重演化持续复用。GPU实验减少活跃内存达27.5%，并精确复现logits。

Conclusion: 压缩适应具有可迁移性和可复用性，NeuralZip能高效实现无损压缩并降低内存使用。

Abstract: Lossless compression can reduce the storage and movement of model weights without changing their floating-point values, but repeated statistical analysis and code construction add computational overhead. We study whether the statistical structure of exponents can be prepared once and reused. For this, we introduce NeuralZip, which groups chunks with similar exponent distributions, shares Huffman codes, and selectively represents recurring exponent tuples using packed exponents, thereby achieving additional moderate compression ratios. A setup chooses these representations before subsequent encodings, while every encoding still processes the current tensor values. In floating-point model checkpoints, post-setup compression is 1.81-21.33$\times$ faster than the baselines and achieves exact bit-to-bit reconstruction. We show that this setup can be precomputed and transferred from another compatible architecture, preserving similar compression ratios and avoiding the need to amortize setup costs. Therefore, compression adaptation is transferable and reusable. Training checkpoints demonstrate continued reuse as the weights evolve. Finally, GPU experiments reduce active memory usage by up to 27.5$\%$ while reproducing the logits exactly.

</details>


### [105] [Learning Traffic Flow Dynamics with Stochastic Physics-Informed Neural Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09946)
*Federica Bragone,Matthieu Barreau*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出物理信息神经细胞自动机PI-NCA用于数据驱动交通流建模，保证车辆守恒并扩展至随机动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通流建模需理解车辆集体动力学，细胞自动机框架简单可解释但学习局部转移规则并保持物理约束仍具挑战，尤其随机模型。

Method: 基于标准神经细胞自动机设计物理一致的神经架构，与道路拓扑一致并保证车辆总数守恒，约束学习规则；进一步参数化概率转移规则扩展至随机动力学，保持物理约束。

Result: 在Nagel-Schreckenberg和Kerner-Klenov-Wolf细胞自动机生成的多个交通场景中评估，PI-NCA成功学习两种交通模型动力学，持续优于标准NCA，随机扩展捕获概率转移规则且不破坏物理约束。

Conclusion: PI-NCA能有效学习交通流动力学并保持物理约束，随机扩展可行，优于标准NCA。

Abstract: Traffic flow modeling is essential for understanding and predicting the collective dynamics of vehicles on road networks. Cellular automata provide a simple, interpretable yet powerful framework for representing these dynamics via local interaction rules, while retaining the ability to reproduce complex macroscopic traffic phenomena. However, learning local transition rules from data while preserving physically meaningful constraints remains challenging, particularly for stochastic models. In this work, we propose a physics-informed neural cellular automaton (PI-NCA) for data-driven traffic flow modeling. Building on the standard neural cellular automaton (NCA), we design a neural architecture that is physically consistent with the road topology and guarantees conservation of the total number of vehicles, thereby constraining the learned transition rules to physically admissible dynamics. We further extend this framework to stochastic dynamics by parameterizing probabilistic transition rules while preserving the same physics-informed constraints. We evaluate the proposed models on multiple traffic scenarios generated by the well-established Nagel-Schreckenberg and Kerner-Klenov-Wolf cellular automata. The results demonstrate that the PI-NCA successfully learns the dynamics of both traffic models and consistently outperforms a standard NCA, while the stochastic extension captures probabilistic transition rules without compromising the imposed physical constraints.

</details>


### [106] [TR-PTQ: High-Accuracy Integer-Only Transformer Post Training Quantization via Taylor Region Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09969)
*Eliyahu Levy,Adam Teman,Yoni Pugachov*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文揭示Transformer量化精度损失主要源于归一化层学习缩放参数和GELU复合近似，而非数值精度不足，并提出统一整数化PTQ方法TR-PTQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法将精度损失归因于数值精度不足，常需回退到浮点运算，但Transformer非线性层量化仍具挑战，因此需重新识别真正的结构误差源。

Method: 提出TR-PTQ，利用共享Taylor Region指数与对数原语，使除法、平方根等昂贵运算完全在整数对数域执行；结合免校准、离群值感知的LayerNorm参数优化，消除非线性对浮点硬件单元的需求。

Result: 在视觉和语言基准上实现小于1.5%的绝对精度下降，验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 归一化层缩放参数与GELU复合近似是主要误差贡献者，SoftMax对激进量化天然鲁棒；TR-PTQ通过整数化公式与优化策略，可有效推进Transformer的PTQ部署。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) enables efficient deployment, yet transformer architectures remain challenging to quantize due to nonlinear layers.
