While existing methods attribute accuracy loss to insufficient numerical precision, often necessitating floating-point fallbacks, we demonstrate that degradation is actually driven by specific structural error sources. We find that learned scale parameters in normalization layers and compounded approximations in GELU are the primary error contributors, whereas SoftMax remains inherently robust to aggressive quantization. To address these bottlenecks, we introduce TR-PTQ, a unified integer-only formulation using shared Taylor Region (TR) exponential and logarithm primitives. This approach allows computationally expensive operations, including division and square roots, to be performed entirely in the log-domain via standard integer arithmetic. Combined with a calibration-free, outlier-aware optimization for LayerNorm parameters, our method eliminates the need for floating-point hardware units for nonlinearities, achieving less than 1.5\% absolute accuracy degradation across vision and language benchmarks.

</details>


### [107] [Efficient Patch-Based Anomaly Detection Fused with Diffusion Driven Generative Modeling for Semiconductor Wafer Bin Map Open Set Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09993)
*Limon Bin Hossain,Md Sadib Rahman Ananta*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种混合单类框架，将基于图像块的师生检测器EfficientAD与去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）的部分扩散重建相结合，通过固定凸组合融合其百分位校准分数，用于晶圆图空间缺陷检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督分类器只能识别训练中见过的缺陷类型，而基于单一机制的单类检测器通常只能捕获局部结构偏差或全局分布违规，难以兼顾两者。

Method: 耦合EfficientAD与DDPM，DDPM用于部分扩散重建；对两模块分数进行百分位校准，再以固定凸组合融合。仅用WM-38K混合型数据集中的700片正常晶圆训练，在18,658片留出晶圆上评估。

Result: 融合检测器AUROC达0.9985，误分类从DDPM的852和EfficientAD的1,412降至618，所有两两差异p < 0.001显著。增益源于两模块错误弱重叠，但固定权重融合仅获得oracle选择器可用校正的40-70%。在倒置类别平衡下，平均精度和F1饱和，而MCC和阴性预测值暴露正常预测不可靠；像素级图显示强图像级可分性不意味着空间定位能力，扩散模块作为局部密度先验而非全局几何推理起作用。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持对混合晶圆异常检测器采用样本自适应融合和面向不平衡的评估。

Abstract: Spatial defect signatures on wafer bin maps (WBMs) trace yield loss to specific process faults, yet supervised classifiers recognize only the defect types seen during training, and one-class detectors built on a single mechanism tend to capture either local structural deviations or global distributional violations, but rarely both. This work proposes a hybrid one-class framework that couples a patch-based student-teacher detector (EfficientAD) with a denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM) used for partial-diffusion reconstruction, and fuses their percentile-calibrated scores through a fixed convex combination. Trained on only 700 normal wafers from the WM-38K mixed-type dataset and evaluated on 18,658 held-out wafers, the fused detector reached an AUROC of 0.9985 and reduced misclassifications from 852 (DDPM) and 1,412 (EfficientAD) to 618, with all pairwise differences significant at p < 0.001. Beyond aggregate accuracy, the analysis shows that the gain arises from weakly overlapping errors between the two modules, yet fixed-weight fusion recovers only 40-70% of the correction available to an oracle selector. Under the benchmark's inverted class balance, average precision and F1 saturate, while the Matthews correlation coefficient and negative predictive value expose unreliable normal predictions. Pixel-level maps further show that strong image-level separability does not imply spatial localization, and the diffusion module succeeds as a local density prior rather than through global geometric reasoning. These findings motivate sample-adaptive fusion and imbalance-aware evaluation of hybrid wafer anomaly detectors.

</details>


### [108] [A Drosophila Whole-Connectome Network Can Learn Human-Designed Cognitive Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10014)
*Joonghui Cho,Minchan Kang,Daeshik Kim*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 利用雄性果蝇全脑连接组作为固定循环拓扑，训练人工网络完成有界加法和接地关系语言任务，证明其可提供可复用的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生物接线图能否作为有用的计算基底，超越其进化所适应的行为。

Method: 以MaleCNS v1.0连接组为固定循环拓扑，每条解剖边学习一个标量；分别训练有界加法和基于固定100词词典的接地关系语言任务；并与保持度分布的有向重连网络及21个固定图比较。

Result: 有界加法：92.77% vs 67.93%（重连）；严格配对语言端点：61.59% vs 44.17%（重连），在21图比较中排名第一；在48组干预子集上，打乱任务定义的感觉特征使得分从60.94%降至19.27%。

Conclusion: 高阶MaleCNS接线为有界加法和接地关系语言提供了可复用的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Can a biological wiring diagram serve as a useful computational substrate beyond the behaviors for which it evolved? We use the publicly released MaleCNS v1.0 connectome, reconstructed from a single adult male Drosophila specimen, as the fixed recurrent topology of an artificial network. We train separate models for bounded addition and for a controlled grounded relational language task built from a fixed 100-word lexicon. In both models, one scalar is learned per anatomical edge. The anatomical graph reaches 92.77% mean accuracy on held-out addition, compared with 67.93% for directed degree-preserving rewires. On the strict paired language endpoint, which matches original and order-reversed scenes to their corresponding descriptions, it reaches 61.59% across four fixed interfaces, compared with 44.17% for matched rewires. At the canonical interface, it ranks first in a fixed 21-graph comparison. On the matched 48-group intervention subset, shuffling task-defined sensory features reduces its score from 60.94% to 19.27%. Together, these results show that higher-order MaleCNS wiring provides a reusable inductive bias for bounded addition and grounded relational language.

</details>


### [109] [Matching of signal, noise and hardware timescales for filtering and forecasting of correlated noise signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10037)
*Joshua Donald,Alex Gabbitas,Arthur G. T. Coveney,Sergey Savel'ev,Pavel Borisov*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该研究利用纳米多孔氧化铌物理储备池，揭示噪声相关时间、储备池记忆与预测时域之间的关系决定相关噪声是被过滤还是被预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储备池计算虽能高效处理时序数据，但其内在响应时间尺度固定，而真实信号包含多时间尺度的确定性与随机成分，需理解其处理随机噪声的机制。

Method: 采用纳米多孔氧化铌储备池，对合成含噪信号和加密货币价格波动数据进行实验，分析噪声相关时间、储备池记忆与预测时域三者关系，并提出储备池记忆时域和预测机制指数。

Result: 快于相关储备池记忆和预测时域的噪声被储备池平均化，而慢变噪声的时间结构足以用于算法预测；两个新指标可区分过滤与预测两种操作机制。

Conclusion: 时间尺度匹配可指导输入时间序列编码及物理储备池架构设计，使其能在不同时间尺度上过滤、分析和预测随机信号成分。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing exploits the nonlinear dynamics of physical systems to process time-dependent data with greater energy efficiency than conventional machine learning approaches. However, physical reservoirs have fixed intrinsic response timescales, whereas real-world signals combine deterministic and stochastic components across multiple timescales. Here we show, using a nanoporous niobium oxide reservoir, synthetic noisy signals and cryptocurrency-price volatility, that the relationship among noise correlation time, reservoir memory and forecast horizon determines whether correlated noise is filtered or predicted. Noise varying faster than the relevant reservoir memory and forecast horizon is averaged by the reservoir, whereas the temporal structure of slower-varying noise is sufficient for algorithmic forecasting. We introduce the reservoir memory horizon and forecasting regime index to distinguish these operating regimes. These contributions demonstrate that timescale matching can guide the encoding of input time series and development of physical reservoir architectures that filter, analyse and predict stochastic signal components across distinct temporal scales.

</details>


### [110] [Multi-Agent Coordination via Support-Preserving Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10087)
*Sangmin Lee,Youngju Na,Chanmi Lee,Sung-eui Yoon*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文针对离线多智能体强化学习中基于生成式策略的教师训练失败模式，提出MoSDOT方法，通过半离散最优传输将噪声样本分配到单一模式，以提升教师样本质量和路由一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流式教师模型将噪声与回放目标独立配对，导致邻近噪声被路由到冲突的协调模式，产生无效模式间样本，并通过蒸馏损失传播到学生策略。

Method: 提出Mode-Support Semi-Discrete Optimal Transport (MoSDOT)，将多模态回放总结为有限模式支持并预设容量，用条件半离散最优传输在教师训练前为每个噪声样本分配单一模式；并研究共享随机性变体。

Result: 在受控诊断和离线MARL基准上，MoSDOT提高了端点质量和路由一致性，尤其在表现出多模态联合行为的数据集上。

Conclusion: MoSDOT有效消除了教师侧伪影，改善了离线MARL中生成式策略的蒸馏效果，并揭示了严格乘积执行下的残余差距。

Abstract: Offline MARL increasingly relies on generative policies to model multimodal joint behavior, typically by distilling a centralized teacher into decentralized one-step actors under the CTDE. We identify a failure mode at the teacher training stage: standard flow-based teachers pair noise with replay targets independently, so nearby noise samples can be routed toward conflicting coordination modes. The teacher then produces samples between valid modes, and because the distillation loss regresses each local actor onto the conditional mean of the teacher's output given local input, this error is not absorbed but propagated to the student. To remove this teacher-side artifact, we propose Mode-Support Semi-Discrete Optimal Transport (MoSDOT), which summarizes multimodal replay into a finite mode support with prescribed capacities and uses conditional semi-discrete optimal transport to assign each noise sample to a single mode before teacher training. We additionally study a shared-randomness variant that uses a shared noise component at execution to expose the residual gap intrinsic to strict-product execution. On controlled diagnostics and offline MARL benchmarks, MoSDOT improves endpoint quality and routing consistency, particularly on datasets exhibiting multimodal joint behavior.

</details>


### [111] [YANchor-4B: Effective Long-Horizon Reasoning in O(N) Time with O(1) Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10118)
*Huishan Ji,Hua Xu,Weiming Zhang,Qirui Ye*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出YANchor-4B通用递归模型，通过锚点机制保留关键记忆，实现O(N)时间与O(1)内存的长程推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程推理需访问早期信息且控制生成成本：全历史注意力存储与计算随长度增长，递归压缩易丢失精确细节。

Method: YANchor-4B采用递归架构，将关键记忆保存为锚点供后续推理检索，并设计多维记忆机制以支持有效长程推理。

Result: 在AIME 2024--2026上达82.93%平均pass@1，HMMT上达63.64%，显著优于线性时间、恒定状态同类模型及更大模型；在H100上批量长生成吞吐量较Transformer和混合基线提升数倍；数十个基准测试显示其通用能力优势。

Conclusion: YANchor在保持O(N)时间与O(1)内存的同时，兼顾长程推理精度与通用性能，并具备更高生成效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning demands access to earlier information at a manageable generation cost. Full-history attention incurs growing storage and computation, while recurrent compression can lose precise details. Therefore, we present YANchor-4B, a general-purpose recurrent model that preserves crucial memory as ANchors for retrieval during subsequent reasoning. Beyond $O(N)$-time generation and $O(1)$ memory, YANchor enables effective long-horizon reasoning through its multidimensional memory mechanism. For example, on challenging math problems, it achieves 82.93% mean pass@1 on AIME 2024--2026 and 63.64% on HMMT, substantially outperforming linear-time, constant-state counterparts, including larger models. It also delivers several-fold higher batched long-generation throughput than Transformer and hybrid baselines on H100. Furthermore, evaluations across dozens of benchmarks demonstrate YANchor's superiority in general-purpose capabilities.

</details>


### [112] [What Can a Gaussian Process Design Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10122)
*Ivan De Boi,Marnix Van Soom*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该研究提出用Gale对偶和电路从设计角度分析高斯过程模型，预测模型在给定输入下能产生何种矛盾，从而区分模型正确与数据无法揭示错误的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程模型与数据一致可能源于假设正确，也可能源于所选输入无法暴露错误；需要在观测响应前从设计上区分这两种情况。

Method: 对于有限特征GP，核矩阵的零空间给出无噪声响应必须满足的代数关系，Gale对偶将每个观测映射为向量，最小暴露错误的观测组为电路；对于一般核，关系变为软约束。采用结合结构证据（违反关系随噪声减小而无限增长）和先验证据的标准检验，并在模拟中用预测功效选择下一个输入。

Result: 模拟中预测功效与实际拒绝率匹配；按预测功效选择下一个输入，对局部差异的功效从0.48提升到0.72，而按预测方差选择为0.51；二维网格包含精确可加性检验，而拉丁超立方缺失。

Conclusion: 检验本身经典，贡献在于前瞻性解读：在观测响应前，设计已决定其能产生何种矛盾。

Abstract: A Gaussian process (GP) model can agree with the data for two reasons: its assumptions are right, or the chosen inputs could never have shown that they are wrong. The distinction can be checked from the design before any responses are observed. Every model implies relations that its noiseless responses must satisfy at the chosen inputs, such as the middle value lies on the line through its two neighbours. For GPs built from finitely many features, these relations are exactly the null space of the kernel matrix. Gale duality gives them a geometric interpretation, in which each observation has a vector and the smallest groups of observations that can expose an error are the circuits. For other kernels the relations become soft: response patterns may be improbable under the prior rather than algebraically impossible. A standard test then combines two kinds of evidence. Structural evidence comes from a violated relation and grows without limit as the noise falls. Prior-based evidence only says that a departure is improbable under the prior. With all inputs at the two ends of an interval, for example, a GP can reject a straight line against a large curvature, but only because the implied intercept is improbable, never because curvature was seen. In simulations the predicted power matched the observed rejection rates. Choosing the next input by predicted power raised the power against a localised discrepancy from 0.48 to 0.72, against 0.51 when choosing by predictive variance, and a grid in two dimensions contained exact tests of additivity that a Latin hypercube lacked. The test itself is classical. The contribution is the prospective reading of that test: before observing the responses, the design already determines what kind of contradiction it can produce.

</details>


### [113] [Edge Accuracy Is Not Enough: Why Dynamics-Learned Structure Fails to Transfer to Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10213)
*Nicholas Tan Jerome,Fangnian Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文揭示从动力学预测中学习的关系结构（如NRI）在稀缺标签的逆问题中迁移会系统性失败，即使满足理论上的边误差条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆问题中标注数据稀缺，常用策略是迁移从丰富的正向模拟数据中学到的关系结构，但该策略是否普遍有效尚不清楚。

Method: 理论分析近似结构对估计误差的益处条件（Δ < n² - kn）；在180个CFD模拟氢泄漏和180个声学场景的源定位任务上，比较NRI学习结构、任务优化注意力基线及物理先验（格林函数）的性能；进行四项独立证据检验（增加18倍数据、边阈值敏感性、图重叠度、其他结构估计器）；提出基于Jaccard相似度的轻量迁移性测试。

Result: NRI结构使性能分别下降116%和201%，而物理先验仅下降69-72%；NRI增加18倍数据仅提升0.5%（基线提升16.6%，p<0.001）；性能对边阈值不敏感；学习图与任务最优图仅6%边重叠；其他结构估计器无益甚至更差；迁移性测试能区分所有四对域/结构的成功与失败迁移，计算时间<1小时，使用目标域数据15-20%。

Conclusion: 满足边精度条件的结构仍可能因近似误差而迁移失败，该误差无法由边精度控制；提出的启发式迁移性测试可有效预警失败迁移，但需更多案例验证其通用性。

Abstract: A natural strategy for inverse problems with scarce labelled data is to transfer relational structure learned from abundant forward-simulation data. We show this strategy fails systematically, even when it satisfies the standard theoretical justification for why structure should help. We prove that approximate structure provides estimation-error benefits whenever the edge error satisfies $Δ< n^2 - kn$, reducing sample complexity from $O(n^2)$ to $O(kn+Δ)$. Structure learned via Neural Relational Inference (NRI) from dynamics prediction satisfies this condition, yet on a source-localisation task across 180 CFD-simulated hydrogen-leak scenarios and 180 acoustic scenarios, it degrades performance by 116% and 201% relative to a flexible, task-optimised attention baseline, while a physics-based prior (Green's function) degrades by only 69-72%. Four independent lines of evidence show this is not a tuning failure: NRI improves only 0.5% when given 18x more training data (versus 16.6% for the task-optimised baseline, $p<0.001$); performance is insensitive to the NRI edge threshold across a wide range; the dynamics-learned graph overlaps the task-optimal graph on only 6% of edges; and two further dynamics-derived structure estimators (correlation- and mutual-information-based) show no measurable benefit over a structure-free baseline, with the correlation-based estimator performing markedly worse. We formalise this gap as a statement about approximation error that the edge-accuracy condition cannot control, and we provide a lightweight transferability test (Jaccard similarity against a partially-observed target-task graph) that separates successful from failed transfer in all four domain/structure pairs we evaluate, using under an hour of computation and 15-20% of target-domain data; we present this as a heuristic calibrated on few cases, not a validated general threshold.

</details>


### [114] [From Prompts to Trees: Effective LLM-Guided Tree Generation for Few-Shot Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10227)
*Yue Qiu,Zekang Du,Yiqun Diao,Bingsheng He,Qinbin Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一个三阶段框架，将大语言模型知识蒸馏为可解释决策树，用于小样本表格数据分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理成本高且可解释性差，决策树虽快且透明但在小样本下表现不佳，因此需要结合两者优势。

Method: 不直接让LLM生成完整树，而是通过三阶段范式提示LLM生成规则，再将规则组织成决策树。

Result: 在多个真实表格数据集上，方法在准确率和可解释性上优于基线，且提示开销显著降低。

Conclusion: 该框架有效桥接LLM与决策树，在小样本表格分类中实现高性能与可解释性，并减少提示成本。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) possess rich world knowledge and impressive generalization capabilities, their direct application to tabular data classification is hindered by high inference costs and limited interpretability. In contrast, decision trees are fast and transparent but often underperform in low-data regimes. In this work, we propose a novel framework that bridges these paradigms by distilling LLM knowledge into interpretable decision trees under a few-shot learning setting. Instead of directly prompting the LLM to generate full trees, which is often unstable and inefficient, we develop a three-stage paradigm that prompts the LLM to generate rules and organize the rules into a tree. Experiments on multiple real-world tabular datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior accuracy and interpretability with significantly lower prompting overhead compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [115] [ProtocolMatch: Protocol-Dependent Model Selection for Scientific Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10239)
*Lu Wei,Yufeng Wang,Haibin Ling*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 论文提出ProtocolMatch评估框架，主张科学动力学预测的模型选择应依赖协议而非仅架构，需同时报告准确率、物理有效性和分布偏移可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学动力学预测常被简化为架构选择问题，但实际部署还受观测历史、闭环反馈、计算预算、物理目标和测试分布影响，缺乏协议依赖的模型选择与评估方法。

Method: 提出ProtocolMatch——计算匹配、验证选择、保留失败的评估框架；在驱动量子自旋动力学上，用三个独立生成数据集比较循环网络、分块注意力、因果注意力和低秩线性预测器，并考察观察历史限制、状态刷新、物理惩罚和驱动频率偏移。

Result: 因果注意力与循环网络的排序随训练集增大而反转；六自旋局部观测中线性预测器平均误差最低；限制观察历史恶化刷新历史视图但改善闭环视图；最新状态MLP在状态刷新下误差低于持久性但闭环排名随系统变化且出现爆炸误差；物理惩罚改善目标一致性但不稳定提升预测误差；分布内区间在驱动频率偏移后覆盖大幅下降。

Conclusion: 科学模型选择应返回预测器及其协议，并分别报告准确率、物理有效性和偏移分布可靠性。

Abstract: Scientific dynamics forecasting is often framed as an architecture choice, although deployment is also determined by observed history, rollout feedback, compute budget, physical objective, and test distribution. We formulate protocol-dependent model selection and introduce ProtocolMatch, a compute-matched, validation-selected, and failure-preserving evaluation framework. On driven quantum-spin dynamics, we compare recurrent, patched-attention, causal-attention, and low-rank linear predictors across three independently generated datasets. The causal-attention--recurrence ordering reverses as the training set grows within a fixed two-spin task, while a linear predictor has the lowest mean error in the six-spin local-observable comparison. Restricting observed history worsens every refreshed-history view but improves every closed-loop view in the four-spin study. A latest-state MLP has lower error than persistence on every dataset under state refresh across all five cells, yet its closed-loop rank varies by system and includes finite explosive errors. Physical penalties improve targeted consistency without reliably improving prediction error, and in-distribution intervals lose most coverage after a driving-frequency shift. Thus scientific model selection should return a predictor with its protocol and report accuracy, physical validity, and shifted-distribution reliability separately.

</details>


### [116] [Sparse Planning in Visual World Models via Cost Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10274)
*Yingchen Xu,Edward Grefenstette*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练、目标条件化的空间令牌选择器COSTGRAD，依据规划代价对输入令牌的梯度范数排序，实现稀疏世界模型规划加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于令牌的世界模型虽支持细粒度潜在规划，但反复处理大规模空间令牌网格导致动作搜索计算昂贵。

Method: COSTGRAD从下游控制目标的规划代价梯度中推导令牌重要性，对空间令牌排序并保留高重要性令牌，在AdaLN条件预测器上以50%稀疏度进行规划，并可结合缩减的CEM搜索。

Result: 在四个连续控制基准中的三个上，COSTGRAD匹配或超越全令牌规划，每环境规划步获得2.6倍实际加速；结合令牌稀疏与缩减CEM搜索后总加速约5倍且仍超过全令牌基线。同时发现架构依赖的失效模式：在AdaLN与concat对比中，纯COSTGRAD在concat上失去相对随机选择的优势，该差异与动作通路漂移相关。

Conclusion: 选择器与架构的兼容性是稀疏世界模型规划的一个设计维度，COSTGRAD在AdaLN架构下有效，但在concat架构下需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Token-based world models enable fine-grained latent planning, but repeatedly processing large spatial token grids makes action search expensive. We introduce COSTGRAD, a training-free, goal-conditioned selector that ranks spatial tokens by the gradient norm of the planning cost with respect to each input token. By deriving importance from the downstream control objective, COSTGRAD targets tokens that matter for planning rather than merely for prediction. On AdaLN-conditioned predictors at $50\%$ sparsity, COSTGRAD matches or exceeds full-token planning on three of four continuous-control benchmarks, while giving a measured $2.6\times$ wall-clock speedup per environment planning step. Combining token sparsity with reduced CEM search increases this to a $\sim 5\times$ total speedup while still exceeding the full-token baseline. We also identify an architecture-dependent failure mode: in a matched AdaLN-vs-concat comparison, concat maintains comparable full-token performance but pure COSTGRAD loses its advantage over random selection. This difference tracks action-pathway drift: gradient-selected removal produces less drift than random removal on AdaLN, but more on concat. These results highlight selector-architecture compatibility as a design axis for sparse world-model planning. Project page and demos: https://ycxuyingchen.github.io/costgrad/

</details>


### [117] [Continual Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10302)
*Tommaso Marzi,Ahmed Hendawy,Jan Peters,Carlo D'Eramo,Andrea Cini,Cesare Alippi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 针对任务结构变化的持续多智能体强化学习问题，提出将任务序列映射为属性图的CGMARL框架，并给出基准GRAFO与基于冻结图编码器FROG的方法以缓解遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续多智能体强化学习方法缺乏利用任务间结构差异的专门机制，难以促进迁移并缓解对旧任务的遗忘；许多应用中任务差异体现在底层结构上（如电网拓扑、编队构型）。

Method: 提出CGMARL框架，将任务序列映射为一系列属性图，每张图决定环境动态（下一状态和/或奖励）及对应任务的智能体数量；构建首个CGMARL基准GRAFO；提出FROG方法，使用冻结图骨干在基于图的CMARL策略中保留过去的结构信息，并与现有持续学习方法结合。

Result: 在GRAFO上的实验表明，将FROG与现有持续学习方法配对后，在多个CGMARL场景中性能显著提升。

Conclusion: 利用任务结构图信息并冻结图编码器可有效提升持续多智能体强化学习在新任务适应与旧任务保持之间的平衡，缓解灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: In Continual Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (CMARL), agents learn cooperative policies across sequences of tasks, aiming to adapt effectively to new tasks while preserving the ability to solve previously encountered ones. In many applications, tasks differ in their underlying structure, which can represent, for example, distinct operational conditions or target configurations (e.g., different network topologies in power grids or arrangements in formation control). Existing CMARL methods lack dedicated mechanisms to leverage this structural information when learning new tasks, failing to promote transfer and mitigate forgetting. To fill this gap, we propose Continual Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (CGMARL), a novel framework for CMARL problems in which task sequences are mapped into a series of attributed graphs, each modeling a task-specific structure. In CGMARL, each graph determines the environment dynamics (next states and/or rewards) and the number of agents for the corresponding task. Then, we present Graph-based Formation (GRAFO), the first CGMARL benchmark, and show how forgetting arises in this setting. Finally, to address this limitation, we propose Frozen Graph Encoder (FROG), a method that relies on a frozen graph backbone to preserve past structural information in graph-based CMARL policies. Experiments on GRAFO show that pairing FROG with existing CL methods substantially improves performance on multiple CGMARL scenarios.

</details>


### [118] [SemanticFold: Latent Sequence Compression SeparatesLanguage Modeling, Decodability, and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10304)
*Mingyan Liu,Min Huang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文研究提示前缀的潜在序列压缩是否保留大语言模型推理能力，提出SemanticFold压缩方案并多维度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究潜在序列压缩对LLM推理能力的影响，并检验是否存在单一压缩阈值或标量证书来衡量压缩保真度。

Method: 提出SemanticFold，在学习边界处折叠前缀隐藏状态；在五种模型规模上采用固定目标协议，对比原生与压缩前缀，考察负对数似然、有限标签推理准确率、线性探针可访问性、开放式生成及系统级内存与延迟五类端点。

Result: 压缩对各端点影响非单调且阈值不共享；Qwen3-1.7B在R=1.7时压缩后NLL降低0.135，SmolLM2在R=1.2时升高0.013，Pythia基本不变；NLL分解表明Qwen的有利似然主要源于残差自适应而非序列缩短；线性探针准确率与宏AUC变化绝对值小于0.03且置信区间跨零。

Conclusion: 潜在压缩下的能力保持没有单一标量证书：语言模型拟合、可解码性与推理行为回答不同问题，在同一压缩操作下可能朝不同方向变化。

Abstract: We study whether latent sequence compression of prompt prefixes preserves the capabilities that large language models rely on during inference. We introduce SemanticFold, a compression scheme that folds prefix hidden states at learned boundaries, and evaluate it across five model scales: Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-8B, SmolLM2-1.7B, Pythia-1.4B, and Pythia-6.9B. We use a fixed-target protocol: a frozen prefix is executed natively or compressed, and both arms teacher-force identical continuation tokens. This design rules out target-selection explanations for likelihood changes. We examine five endpoint families: fixed-target negative log-likelihood, finite-label reasoning accuracy, linear probe accessibility, open-ended generation, and systems-level memory and latency. We find that compression moves these endpoints non-monotonically and that they do not share a single compression threshold. On Qwen3-1.7B at compression ratio R=1.7, compressed-minus-native mean NLL decreases by 0.135 under paired bootstrap with 10000 draws. On SmolLM2 at R=1.2, the mean change is 0.013 higher than native. On both Pythia checkpoints, NLL is effectively unchanged. An NLL decomposition separating sequence shortening from the learned residual transform shows that the favorable Qwen likelihood is attributable primarily to residual adaptation rather than to shortening alone. MLP-only, which applies the transform without shortening, achieves 0.082 lower NLL than Full SemanticFold. Linear probe accuracy and macro AUC change by less than 0.03 in absolute value across conditions, with confidence intervals crossing zero. We conclude that preservation under latent compression has no single scalar certificate: language-model fit, decodability, and reasoning behavior answer different questions and can move in different directions under the same compression operation.

</details>


### [119] [How Do Transformers Learn to Represent Symmetries?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10305)
*Eduardo Santos-Escriche,Valerie Engelmayer,Ya-Wei Eileen Lin,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 研究普通Transformer通过有限数据增强学习点云数据中不同对称群的能力，并分析其外推行为与可解释机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管有限数据增强训练Transformer取得经验成功，但架构、对称不变性与增强预算之间的相互作用尚未充分探索。

Method: 使用普通Transformer，通过有限数据增强在点云数据集上学习不同对称群，并对基础保角子群进行外推实验与结构分析。

Result: 发现可学习性递增顺序：非保角对称、保角对称、基础保角子群（平移、旋转、缩放）；识别出诱导不变性的可解释机制，并证明该机制可用于学习等变性。

Conclusion: 普通Transformer可通过有限增强学习多种对称性，其近似不变性机制可作为学习等变性的关键构建模块。

Abstract: Training Transformer-based architectures with finite data augmentation has become an increasingly popular approach in geometric machine learning. Despite its empirical success, the interplay between the Transformer architecture, invariance to different symmetries, and augmentation budgets remains underexplored. In this paper, we study the ability of a vanilla Transformer to learn various symmetries through finite data augmentation for point cloud datasets. We identify an ordering of increasing learnability across the following symmetry groups: (i) non-angle-preserving symmetries, (ii) angle-preserving symmetries, and (iii) base angle-preserving subgroups, such as translation, rotation, and scale. For the base angle-preserving groups, we further investigate the Transformer's extrapolation behavior and conduct a structural analysis of the trained models, allowing us to identify interpretable mechanisms that induce invariance. Finally, we extend our analysis to equivariant functions and show that the detected mechanisms for approximate invariance can also provide a key building block for learned equivariance. Our project page is available at https://transformers-learn-symmetries.github.io/

</details>


### [120] [RSIGym: A Flexible Environment for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10310)
*Fanqing Meng,Lingxiao Du,Haocheng Lu,Qiguang Chen,Ziqi Zhao,Zijian Wu,Jiayuan Zhuo,Mengkang Hu,Michael Qizhe Shieh*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出RSIGym，一个基于EaaS的智能体原生研究环境，支持智能体在统一环境中联合优化数据、训练设置与执行工具，并用RSI-Index评估自我改进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进需将已接受的改变带入后续改进周期，而研究智能体提出的改变也需要大量研究基础设施；现有设定常让智能体重建常规基础设施或仅能探索单个组件。

Method: RSIGym通过可复用服务暴露训练、推理、rollout、评估和沙箱执行，具备共享预算与权限控制，支持数据、工具及联合改进轨道；定义RSI-Index为五个基准上剩余性能差距平均关闭比例。

Result: 在独立联合运行中，Opus 5取得最高RSI-Index 0.4809，每基准运行预算500美元；其系统提升所有五个基准，SWE-bench Verified从17.67%升至50.33%，AIME从31.67%升至97.78%；额外实验涵盖工具精炼、预算变化与受限网络。

Conclusion: RSIGym使智能体能诊断失败并选择候选，实现跨组件联合自我改进；开源代码与结果以支持可复现性和进一步研究。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement requires carrying accepted changes into later improvement cycles, while studying agent-proposed changes also requires substantial research infrastructure. Existing settings often leave agents to rebuild routine infrastructure or restrict exploration to individual components. We introduce RSIGym, an agent-native research environment based on Everything as a Service (EaaS). RSIGym exposes training, inference, rollout, evaluation, and sandbox execution through reusable services, with shared budget and permission controls supporting Data, Harness, and Joint improvement tracks. This design enables agents to investigate individual interventions and jointly optimize data, training settings, and execution harnesses within the same environment. We define RSI-Index as the mean fraction of the remaining performance gap closed across five benchmarks covering software engineering, terminal interaction, mathematics, scientific reasoning, and skill-based tasks. Comparing six frontier research models in independent Joint runs, Opus 5 achieves the highest RSI-Index of 0.4809 under a $500 platform-service budget per benchmark run. Its selected systems improve all five benchmarks, raising SWE-bench Verified from 17.67% to 50.33% and AIME from 31.67% to 97.78%. Additional experiments examine DSH-harness refinement, budget variation, and restricted network access, while recorded trajectories reveal how agents diagnose failures and select candidates. We open-source the full RSIGym codebase and results to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [121] [Fault-tolerant foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10311)
*Trevor McCourt,Ila R. Fiete,Isaac L. Chuang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 研究表明，通过训练，大语言模型能够容忍不可靠硬件，且其错误弹性随规模增大而增强，暗示存在形式化容错可能，为低能耗故障硬件上运行AI推理提供节能路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴计算机硬件常以可靠性换取能效；作者希望探索LLM是否能被训练以容忍这种不可靠性，从而在低能耗、有故障的硬件上运行AI推理以实现大幅节能。

Method: 在模拟的故障数字硬件上进行总计40,000 GPU小时的训练运行，并据此推导修改后的神经缩放定律，量化模型错误弹性随规模变化的趋势。

Result: 模型不仅未因硬件不可靠而性能下降，其错误弹性反而随模型增大而增加；缩放定律表明模型学会在“好”的纠错码内计算，且相对开销随模型增大保持有限。

Conclusion: 作者猜想经适当训练的LLM可能具有形式化容错性；若成立，在低能耗、有故障硬件上运行AI推理可能成为大幅节能的途径。

Abstract: Emerging computer hardware often trades reliability for energy efficiency; here we show that large-language models (LLMs) can be trained to tolerate this unreliability, and that rather than degrading, their error resilience actually increases as they grow. Modified neural scaling laws inferred from 40,000 GPU-hours of training runs on simulated faulty digital hardware quantify this trend and suggest that models learn to compute within "good" error-correcting codes, whose relative overhead remains finite no matter how large the model gets. This finding leads us to conjecture that appropriately trained LLMs may be formally fault-tolerant; if true, running AI inference on low energy, faulty hardware may be a path to substantial energy savings over the status quo.

</details>


### [122] [Thinking in Depth: Retrospective Inference for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10317)
*Hao-Run Cai,Si-Yang Liu,Zi-Jian Cheng,Kun-Yang Yu,Jin-Hao Sheng,Guo Yu,Chonghan Liu,Zhi Zhou,Jun-Peng Jiang,Lan-Zhe Guo,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出表格基础模型Retro，通过回顾式推理让深层网络显式重访并重组中间层信息，在多个基准上达到前沿水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（TFMs）虽用堆叠Transformer层做上下文预测，但追踪发现预测细化在深度上高度不均，常集中在最后几层，中间表示未被充分利用。

Method: Retro基于回顾式推理，包含两个互补操作：Attention Residuals自适应重加权不同深度的贡献以决定重访哪些中间信息；query-conditioned Gated Attention按表示维度逐元素调制注意力输出，为每个查询塑造上下文更新。

Result: 分析显示Retro将预测细化提前并更广泛地分布到各深度，不同阶段修订不同查询子集，呈现多视图细化模式；在TabArena、TALENT和RelArena上排名前三并位于Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 直接重用中间表示是更有效利用TFMs深度信息的实用方法。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) are pretrained across diverse tabular tasks and make predictions on a new table at inference time using its labeled examples as context. Most recent TFMs perform such in-context prediction with stacked Transformer layers, repeatedly transforming how examples are represented and compared. By tracing individual queries through several strong TFMs, we find that predictive refinement is highly uneven across depth and is often concentrated in later layers. This uneven refinement motivates us to reconsider how intermediate representations are constructed and reused throughout the network. We introduce Retro, a tabular foundation model based on retrospective inference, where later stages can explicitly revisit and recombine intermediate information produced earlier in the network. Retro organizes this process around two complementary operations: which intermediate information to revisit, and how the resulting contextual update should be shaped for each query. Attention Residuals address the former by adaptively reweighting contributions from different depths, while query-conditioned Gated Attention addresses the latter by modulating the attention output element-wise across representation dimensions. Our analysis shows that Retro shifts predictive refinement earlier and more broadly across depth, with different stages revising different subsets of queries in a pattern suggestive of multi-view refinement. Across TabArena, TALENT, and RelArena, Retro ranks among the top three and lies on the Pareto frontier. These results indicate that directly reusing intermediate representations provides a practical way to better exploit depth in TFMs.

</details>


### [123] [Training Parallel Speculative Draft Models by Directly Minimizing Expected Decoding Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10411)
*Yunxiao Zhao,Changxiao Cai*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文将推测解码建模为马尔可夫奖励过程，提出EDR目标以精确优化全局解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行与半自回归草稿模型忽略跨轮耦合，现有块局部代理目标无法直接优化全局解码效率。

Method: 构建马尔可夫奖励过程框架，导出EDR目标与精确时序差分梯度，并设计离线评估器。

Result: 微调DSpark和DFly后，在数学推理、代码生成和聊天等九个基准上持续提升平均接受长度，优于现有训练目标。

Conclusion: EDR为并行草稿模型提供无辅助超参数的精确优化与评估框架，有效提升推测解码效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by using a low-cost draft model to propose tokens that the full-size target model verifies in parallel. Parallel and semi-autoregressive (semi- AR) drafters improve drafting efficiency by proposing an entire block in a single forward pass, but training them raises a new difficulty: the draft distribution for a given position depends on where the decoding round starts, and where rounds start depends on how many tokens earlier rounds accepted. Existing training objectives typically rely on block-local surrogates that ignore this cross-round coupling, and therefore do not directly optimize the global decoding efficiency. In this work, we develop a theoretical framework for training and evaluating these drafters by representing speculative decoding as a Markov reward process. This formulation yields the Expected Decoding Rounds (EDR) objective, which weights local rejection costs by state occupancies and exactly equals the expected number of decoding rounds. Unlike prior surrogate objectives, EDR introduces no auxiliary hyperparameters. We then derive an exact temporal-difference gradient that supports unbiased stochastic optimization from target-model rollouts. The same framework also yields an exact offline evaluator for round counts, enabling paired drafter comparisons on shared target rollouts without running speculative decoding. Finetuning two state-of-the- art drafters, DSpark and DFly, with EDR consistently improves mean accepted length and outperforms existing training objectives across nine benchmarks spanning math reasoning, code generation, and chat.

</details>


### [124] [HAN-Mamba: Hierarchical Selective State Space Networks for Multi-Scale Financial Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10323)
*Mihai Bogdan Deaconu,Ioan Daniel Pop*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出HAN-Mamba混合架构，用选择性状态空间(Mamba)编码器替换Transformer编码器，在保持注意力融合器的同时实现更优的已实现波动率预测，参数更少且支持长上下文与流式更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期已实现波动率预测需融合从秒级订单簿到周级制度漂移的多时间尺度市场信息，而现有HAN-T中的二次注意力编码器参数多、复杂度高，且高频上下文长度受限。

Method: 保留HAN-T的分层多流与注意力融合器结构，将短、中、长三个尺度流的Transformer编码器替换为选择性状态空间(Mamba)编码器，利用输入依赖门控建模波动率的持久衰减记忆与突变制度切换；融合器仍对三个token做置换不变注意力。

Result: 在Optiver已实现波动率预测基准的时间感知五折交叉验证下，HAN-Mamba平均RMSPE为0.1942，优于HAN-T的0.1965，参数量减少33%；线性时间编码器将高频上下文从60扩展到240个bucket，误差降至0.1927，而注意力变体在该处饱和；支持推理时恒定时间流式更新。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明性能提升源于编码器替换，分层先验可跨序列模型族迁移，且置换不变注意力融合器仍是跨尺度整合的正确机制。

Abstract: Short-horizon realized volatility forecasting requires the integration of market information that evolves at incompatible temporal resolutions, from second-level order book dynamics to weekly regime drift. Our conference work introduced HAN-T, a hierarchical architecture in which scale-specific Transformer encoders process short, mid, and long-horizon streams and a learned attention fuser weighs their contributions. This article replaces the quadratic attention encoders with selective state space (Mamba) encoders while retaining attention only in the fuser, where the input is a three-token set rather than a long sequence. The resulting hybrid, HAN-Mamba, summarizes each stream through a recurrent state whose input-dependent gating matches two structural properties of volatility: persistent but decaying memory and abrupt regime shifts. On the Optiver Realized Volatility Prediction benchmark under time-aware five-fold cross-validation, HAN-Mamba improves mean RMSPE over HAN-T (0.1942 vs. 0.1965) with 33% fewer parameters. Its linear-time encoders further allow the high-frequency context to be extended from 60 to 240 buckets, reducing error to 0.1927 where the attention variant saturates, and support constant-time streaming updates at inference. Ablations attribute the gains to the encoder swap, confirm that the hierarchical prior transfers across sequence-model families, and show that the permutation-invariant attention fuser remains the correct mechanism for cross-scale integration.

</details>


### [125] [A Good Self-Teacher Meets the Student Where They Are: Joint On-Policy Learning and Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10447)
*Randy Ardywibowo,Arnav Dalal,Jiantao Jiao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 论文针对特权自蒸馏中教师指导可能不匹配学生能力而损害学生性能的问题，推导教师局部蒸馏更新成为学生奖励梯度正倍数的充要条件，据此提出JOLT方法，将单一策略联合训练为KL正则化的特权教师和无特权学生，在数学推理、代码、工具使用和终端使用任务上提升训练效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习依赖稀疏的结果奖励，在困难长时程任务中成功轨迹稀少且昂贵；On-Policy Distillation可提供密集token级监督，自蒸馏进一步免除独立教师模型，但仅靠特权条件并不能保证蒸馏更新一定有利于学生——特权信息可能让教师走捷径，产生与学生当前行为不匹配的监督，导致即使教师性能更高也可能使学生退化。

Method: 分析特权教师选择如何影响学生更新，推导教师局部蒸馏更新为学生奖励梯度正倍数的充要条件，表明教师不仅需任务表现好，还需提供适合学生当前能力的指导；据此设计结合结果奖励与面向学生的token级KL正则的教师训练代理目标，提出Joint On-Policy Learning and Teaching (JOLT)，联合训练单一策略的两种角色：使用KL正则目标训练的特权教师，以及使用密集on-policy蒸馏训练的无特权学生。

Result: 在数学推理、编码、工具使用和终端使用任务上，JOLT提升了训练效率和性能，且引入学生奖励后可获得进一步增益。

Conclusion: 合适的特权教师应兼具任务能力与学生适配性，通过KL正则约束教师偏离学生并配合on-policy蒸馏，可在无需独立教师模型的情况下实现高效稳定的自蒸馏训练，为稀疏奖励长时程任务提供有效方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) from outcome rewards suffers from sparse supervision, particularly on difficult, long-horizon tasks where successful trajectories are rare and costly to generate. On-Policy Distillation (OPD) offers an attractive alternative by providing dense token-level supervision from a stronger teacher along the student's own generations. Self-distillation methods further remove the need for a separate teacher model by conditioning the same policy on privileged information to serve as its own teacher. However, privileged conditioning alone does not guarantee that the resulting distillation update improves the student. Indeed, privileged information can lead the teacher to solve tasks through shortcuts unavailable to the student, producing supervision poorly matched to the student's current behavior. Consequently, even a higher-performing teacher can provide guidance that degrades student performance. To address this, we analyze how the choice of privileged teacher affects the student's update. We derive a necessary and sufficient condition for the teacher's local distillation update to be a positive multiple of the student's reward gradient. Our analysis suggests that the teacher should not only perform well on the task, but also provide guidance suited to the student's current capabilities. This characterization motivates a practical teacher-training surrogate that combines outcome rewards with token-level Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization toward the student. Based on this result, we propose Joint On-Policy Learning and Teaching (JOLT), which jointly trains a single policy in two roles: a privileged teacher using a KL-regularized objective, and an unprivileged student using dense on-policy distillation. Across mathematical reasoning, coding, tool use, and terminal use, JOLT improves training efficiency and performance, with further gains from student rewards.

</details>


### [126] [Decoupling Exploration from Optimization in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10536)
*Saif Punjwani,Micah Goldblum*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出探索-蒸馏（ExpDis）框架，将探索与优化解耦，以在RLVR中安全地激励新颖性并提升推理多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR虽有望发现新推理策略，但直接加入强新颖性奖励效果有限且会降低模型质量；且可验证奖励仅监督知识行为的狭窄部分，导致质量退化难以恢复。

Method: 训练一个或多个带新颖性奖励的探索者策略，对其轨迹进行正确性与质量过滤，再蒸馏到单独的学生策略；学生策略不使用新颖性奖励训练。交替进行多轮探索与优化。

Result: 在七个数学推理基准和两个模型族上，相同墙钟预算下ExpDis优于DAPO，并观察到更好的pass@k扩展，表明生成更丰富的正确解。

Conclusion: 解耦探索与优化可激进扩展探索而不损害学生策略，从而提升推理多样性和性能。

Abstract: Modern language models undergo reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) on top of already-trained checkpoints. A key promise of RLVR is the discovery of new reasoning strategies. In principle, a model can sample novel ideas absent from its prior training data. In practice, however, augmenting RLVR with strong novelty incentives has seen limited success and can degrade model quality. Because verifiable rewards supervise only a narrow slice of the model's knowledge and behavior, such degradations are difficult to recover from. Instead, we decouple exploration from optimization in a framework we call Exploration-Distillation (ExpDis). We train one or more explorer policies with a novelty bonus in the reward, filter their trajectories for correctness and quality, and distill them into a separate student policy. The student policy is then trained without a novelty bonus. We repeat the above procedure for several rounds, alternating between exploration and optimization. This decoupling allows us to aggressively scale exploration without degrading the student policy. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks and two model families, ExpDis outperforms DAPO at the same wall-clock budget. Moreover, we observe improved pass@$k$ scaling, indicating that ExpDis produces models that generate more diverse correct solutions.

</details>


### [127] [Temporally Interpretable Differentiable Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10367)
*Eisuke Hirota,Aarav Sane,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文引入时间维度可解释性（temporal interpretability），通过动作分块（action chunking）提升可微决策树（DDTs）在序贯决策任务中的可解释性，并提出两种策略梯度算法与信息论树重构算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可微决策树虽能提供离散树可视化，但其单时间步行为与人类多时间步规划存在固有不匹配，不适合序贯决策任务，因此需要引入时间维度的可解释性。

Method: 提出两种结合动作分块的新型策略梯度算法；开发信息论树重构算法以在训练中修改树，保持参数高效；并在四个仿真环境中，从蒸馏的动作分块策略热启动动作分块DDTs。

Result: 热启动动作分块DDTs在四个仿真环境中的三个里达到与神经网络策略相当的性能，同时参数量减少高达80%。

Conclusion: 从蒸馏的动作分块策略热启动动作分块DDTs是获得时间可解释树的最有效方式，能实现参数高效且性能接近神经网络策略。

Abstract: Interpretability offers a solution to safe autonomy by providing transparency into an agent's underlying decision-making model. Within sequential-decision making tasks, differentiable decision trees (DDTs) are one approach to such interpretability, maintaining automatic-differentiable policies while providing humans with a discrete tree-based visualization. Nonetheless, current implementations of DDTs are not well-suited for sequential-decision making domains, as there exists an inherent mismatch between a tree's single-timestep behavior and a human's multi-timestep planning. Our work thus introduces time as a new dimension of interpretability, coined as temporal interpretability, and demonstrates how temporal abstractions via action chunking improve it. We achieve this by first introducing two novel policy gradient algorithms that incorporate action chunking. Additionally, to maintain parameter-efficient trees, we develop an information-theoretic tree restructuring algorithm that modifies the tree during training. Across four simulation environments, we find that warm-starting action chunked DDTs from a distilled action chunked policy is the most effective way to obtain temporally interpretable trees: they match neural network policies in three of the four domains while using up to 80$\%$ fewer parameters. Our code is available at https://github.com/ei5uke/temp-interp.

</details>


### [128] [Input-Blind Controls Produce Substantial Oracle Headroom for Layer Programs in Multiple-Choice Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10368)
*Yibei Guo,Rui Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该研究通过对照实验检验层程序选择带来的增益是否源于所选层计算的特定优势，发现大量剩余空间可由与输入无关的扰动产生，从而质疑选择增益的归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应计算中，oracle评估用已知答案估计选择潜力，但选择带来的增益本身无法解释所选程序为何有效，需区分选择增益与特定层计算的好处。

Method: 在Qwen3-4B-Base和Llama-3.1-8B上，对4413道多选题评估32种层跳过与重复程序；比较其相对固定动作的增益与同位置输入无关扰动的增益；在另一提示上重评估选择；进行KL校准比较、字母偏移、旋转选项及生成答案测试。

Result: 共享选项顺序时，对照扰动在Qwen3-4B-Base和Llama-3.1-8B上分别有10.2-11.8和15.6-19.4个百分点的剩余空间，超过真实程序的9.0和10.1；仅答案变化率匹配，排序依赖菜单；旋转选项后两家族剩余空间锐减，但真实减对照仍为正（1.4-2.3和3.7-4.5点）；生成答案测试中搜索选择程序在改写后保持26.0点优势，但无安慰剂对照。

Conclusion: 在共享选项顺序下，大量剩余空间可跨提示持续存在，但无法确立所选层计算的特定优势；与这些对照的排序均不能识别该优势。

Abstract: Adaptive computation aims to improve language-model inference by tailoring execution to each input. For layer programs, oracle evaluations use known answers to estimate the potential gain from this flexibility, before a practical selector is available. However, a gain from selection does not by itself explain why the chosen programs help. This study examines this distinction using 32 layer-skipping and repetition programs on two models and 4,413 multiple-choice items. The analysis compares their gains over a fixed action selected without the evaluation prompt with those of input-blind perturbations at the same sites, re-evaluating selections on another prompt. With shared option order, the controls give 10.2-11.8 and 15.6-19.4 percentage points of headroom on Qwen3-4B-Base and Llama-3.1-8B, exceeding the real programs' 9.0 and 10.1 in all three random-direction draws per model. They match answer-change rate only, and the ordering depends on the menu: in post hoc comparisons, real programs lead on Llama's repeat-only menu in every draw. A smaller KL-calibrated comparison, including an input-dependent control, favours real programs in point estimate, with inconclusive corrected tests. Fixed letter offsets produce headroom of similar scale. Rotating options sharply reduces both families' headroom, while leaving positive real-minus-control differences of 1.4-2.3 and 3.7-4.5 points; their magnitudes and statistical support depend on further adjustments and the reference. A supplementary generated-answer test finds that search-selected programs keep a 26.0-point advantage over programs selected for other problems after rewording, without a placebo comparison. These results show that substantial headroom can persist across prompts with shared option order without establishing a benefit specific to the selected layer computation; neither ordering against these controls identifies that benefit.

</details>


### [129] [Continual Learning without Continual Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10379)
*Nikita Narayanan,Ritham Majumdarr,Sonali Parbhoo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Latent Concept PFN，将持续学习从持续训练转为持续推理，通过扩展上下文证据集适应新类别与领域，冻结参数以避免遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法依赖持续优化（正则化、回放、参数扩展）来防止灾难性遗忘，但参数更新仍会导致旧知识被覆盖；本文希望用持续推理替代持续训练。

Method: 基于PFN（Prior-Fitted Network）的元训练模型，元训练后冻结；维护潜在概念空间进行上下文贝叶斯推理，以捕捉跨领域和类别的语义结构。新领域或类别到来时，将示例加入记忆，适应表现为潜在概念后验更新而非梯度更新。概念标注仅在元训练中使用，作为潜在空间的软锚点；结合概念标签与原始输入证据处理噪声、模糊或不完整标注，并发现预定义概念集之外的区分。

Result: 在类别增量和领域增量持续学习数据集上取得有竞争力的持续学习性能，同时学习到可解释的潜在概念。

Conclusion: 持续推理范式可在不改变参数的情况下减少遗忘，统一处理领域与类别增量持续学习且无需任务标识，并支持可解释概念学习。

Abstract: Continual learning requires models to adapt to new domains and new classes while retaining prior knowledge. Many existing methods rely on continued optimization, using regularization, replay, or parameter expansion to prevent new updates from overwriting previously learned knowledge. Instead, we propose replacing continual training with continual inference: a PFN-based model that is meta-trained, and then frozen, adapting to new classes only by extending an in-context evidence set. Our model, Latent Concept PFN, performs in-context Bayesian inference over a latent concept space that captures semantic structure shared across domains and classes. As each new domain or class arrives, exemplars are added to the memory; adaptation reflects updated posterior beliefs over latent concepts rather than gradient updates. No parameters are changed, reducing forgetting. The same method handles both domain and class incremental continual learning without task identity. Concept annotations are only used during meta-training, acting as a soft anchor on the latent space rather than a fixed bottleneck. Unlike fixed-vocabulary concept methods, the model also handles noisy, ambiguous, or incomplete annotations by combining concept labels with raw input evidence to discover distinctions beyond the predefined concept set. Experiments on class and domain incremental learning datasets demonstrate competitive continual learning performance while learning interpretable latent concepts.

</details>


### [130] [ResidualQuant: KV Cache Quantization for Looped Transformers with 2-Bit Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10381)
*Heejun Kim,Junyoung Lee,SangLyul Cho,Dongsu Han,Insu Han,Sehoon Kim*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ResidualQuant，利用循环Transformer中跨循环KV状态高度相似的特点，以末次循环KV为参考对残差进行低精度量化，实现INT2高精度压缩并大幅降低KV缓存内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过重复共享块提升参数效率，但KV缓存随循环次数线性增长成为内存瓶颈；现有KV量化在极低精度下精度损失严重。

Method: 观察到跨循环KV状态高度相似，以最终循环KV状态为参考，对剩余循环用低精度残差表示；结合最小二乘缩放、旋转以及逐循环混合精度，实现低至INT2的精确量化与高效重建。

Result: 在多个循环Transformer模型及数学推理和代码生成基准上，ResidualQuant持续优于基于旋转的SOTA KV量化；混合精度下精度接近BF16，理论KV存储减少80.7%，相同内存预算下精度最高提升13.0%；RTX 5090上固定批量解码吞吐最高提升2.73倍，批量可扩大2倍，峰值吞吐最高提升4.15倍。

Conclusion: ResidualQuant有效缓解循环Transformer的KV缓存内存瓶颈，在极低精度量化下保持高精度，并显著提升推理吞吐和批量规模。

Abstract: Looped Transformers improve parameter efficiency by repeatedly applying shared Transformer blocks over multiple recurrent loops, increasing computational depth without increasing the parameter count. However, KV cache memory still scales with the number of loops, becoming a key memory bottleneck that limits batch size and inference throughput. KV cache quantization can alleviate this bottleneck, but existing methods often suffer substantial accuracy degradation at aggressive low-precision regimes. We observe that looped Transformers offer a unique opportunity: KV states across loops are highly similar. Based on this observation, we propose ResidualQuant, which uses the final-loop KV states as a reference and represents the remaining loops with low-precision residuals. Our method further combines least-square scaling and rotations applied to the residuals, as well as loop-wise mixed precision, to enable accurate quantization down to INT2 while retaining efficient reconstruction. Across multiple looped Transformer models and mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, ResidualQuant consistently improves the accuracy-memory tradeoff over state-of-the-art rotation-based KV quantization. In particular, our method retains accuracy close to BF16 under mixed-precision settings while reducing theoretical KV storage by 80.7%, achieving up to 13.0% higher accuracy than the rotation-based baseline at the same memory budget. On an RTX 5090, the reduced KV memory traffic improves fixed-batch decode throughput by up to 2.73x, while the smaller memory footprint enables up to 2x larger batches, improving peak throughput by up to 4.15x.

</details>


### [131] [NeuralBES: A Differentiable, Control-Aware Emulator for Scalable Building Energy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10459)
*Ting-Yu Dai,Takuya Kurihana,Wing Yee Au,Hon Yung Wong*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出NeuralBES，一种可微分建筑能耗仿真器，通过共享神经编码器将建筑元数据映射为物理约束的RC模型参数，经对数空间并行扫描求解并配合预测-校正回路处理恒温器非线性，在ResStock上实现跨气候区的精确且物理有效的全年能耗预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求侧灵活性（预测、转移、削减住宅能耗负荷）依赖可跨数百万异质建筑信任的热模型。现有工具存在硬性权衡：高保真物理仿真器（如EnergyPlus）准确但串行且需逐建筑校准；纯数据驱动序列模型可扩展但放弃物理结构，导致预测可信度低。

Method: NeuralBES是一个可微分仿真器，用共享神经编码器参数化基于电阻-电容（RC）的热模型：将静态建筑元数据（建筑面积、建造年代、HVAC类型等）映射为物理有界的电容、电导和设备系数，这些系数构成标量线性递推，通过对数空间并行扫描求解；预测-校正回路闭合恒温器-温度非线性，同时保留全时域梯度流。在ResStock数据集三个气候区上训练。

Result: NeuralBES在单个训练编码器内处理异质建筑原型、年代和气候区，而黑盒基线产生统计上合理但物理不一致的轨迹。在全年逐时滚动预测中，NeuralBES是唯一同时满足物理有效且与最强原始误差基线MAPE差距在4个百分点以内的数据条件模型，参数量比Transformer和循环基线少约一个数量级；在同等参数量下，其MAPE比灰盒RC替代方案降低一半以上。

Conclusion: NeuralBES通过将物理结构嵌入神经参数化，解决了物理仿真器与数据驱动模型之间的权衡，在异质建筑和大规模部署场景下实现了可扩展、可信且准确的建筑能耗仿真。

Abstract: Demand-side flexibility i.e. forecasting, shifting, and curtailing residential energy loads, depends on thermal models trusted across millions of heterogeneous buildings. Existing tools force a hard tradeoff: high-fidelity physics simulators such as EnergyPlus are accurate but sequential and require per-building calibration, while purely data-driven sequence models scale but abandon the physical structure that makes their predictions trustworthy.
  We introduce NeuralBES (Building Energy Simulation), a differentiable emulator that resolves this tradeoff by parameterizing a resistance--capacitance (RC) based thermal model with a shared neural encoder: static building metadata such as floor area, vintage, and HVAC type is mapped to physically bounded capacitances, conductances, and equipment coefficients, which become the coefficients of a scalar linear recurrence solved via a log-space parallel scan, and a predictor--corrector loop closes the thermostat--temperature nonlinearity while preserving full-horizon gradient flow. Trained on the ResStock dataset across three climate zones, NeuralBES handles heterogeneous building archetypes, vintages, and climate zones within a single trained encoder, while black-box baselines produce statistically plausible but physically inconsistent trajectories. On the annual full-year rollout, NeuralBES is the only data-conditioned model that is simultaneously physics-valid and accurate to within 4 MAPE points of the strongest raw-error baseline, while operating at roughly an order of magnitude fewer parameters than the transformer and recurrent baselines; among physics-valid baselines at parameter parity it more than halves the MAPE of the grey-box RC alternative.

</details>


### [132] [Distilling Graph Geometry: Knowledge Gap from GNNs to MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10520)
*Zhewei Chen,Hao Zhu,Jiaojiao Jiang,Ahad N. Zehmakan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文揭示GNN-to-MLP蒸馏中学生表示空间存在谱欠拟合与谱过拟合两种失败模式，并提出基于Ollivier-Ricci曲率的图几何感知蒸馏框架G^2MLP，在训练时分配预测级与表示级对齐监督，推理时仍为无图MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN-to-MLP蒸馏方法主要迁移节点级预测或采用基于置信度的重加权，但未明确学生应在何处保留教师由图结构诱导的几何信息，导致稀疏图上谱欠拟合、稠密图上谱过拟合。

Method: 以能量加权的教师-学生对齐目标为动机，利用Ollivier-Ricci曲率识别两类谱误差集中区域，并据此在预测级对齐与表示级对齐之间分配监督，提出G^2MLP训练时蒸馏框架。

Result: 在节点分类基准上，G^2MLP持续优于无图蒸馏基线，在两种图密度情形下均缩小教师-学生秩差距，并可无需改变架构迁移到Graph Transformer教师与链接预测任务。

Conclusion: 显式建模并保留教师图诱导的几何结构是提升GNN-to-MLP蒸馏的关键，曲率引导的监督分配能有效缓解谱欠拟合与谱过拟合，同时保持推理阶段无图MLP的部署优势。

Abstract: GNN-to-MLP distillation aims to retain the predictive accuracy of a message-passing teacher while deploying a graph-free MLP at inference. Existing methods mainly transfer node-wise predictions or use confidence-based reweighting, but they do not specify where the student should preserve the teacher's graph-induced geometry. We show that this omission leads to two spectral failure modes in the student's representation space. On sparse graphs, the student suffers from spectral underfit, missing high-energy teacher directions concentrated near boundary regions. On dense graphs, it suffers from spectral overfit, retaining spurious directions that the teacher has collapsed through aggregation. Motivated by an energy-weighted teacher-student alignment objective, we propose Graph Geometry-aware MLP (G^2MLP), a training-time distillation framework guided by Ollivier-Ricci curvature. Curvature identifies where the two spectral errors concentrate and is used to allocate supervision between prediction-level and representation-level alignment. The deployed model remains a standard MLP and requires no graph access at inference. Across node-classification benchmarks, G^2MLP consistently improves over graph-free distillation baselines, reduces the teacher-student rank gap in both regimes, and transfers without architectural changes to Graph Transformer teachers and link prediction.

</details>


<div id='Model'></div>

# Model [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Accelerating Floating-Point Satisfiability Solving via Gradient Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08808)
*Yuanzhuo Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出GradSAT框架，将SMT子句视为多任务学习任务，通过动态梯度归一化平衡梯度，解决基于梯度的SMT求解中的梯度支配问题，并结合GPU连续优化与位精确局部搜索实现高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于优化的SMT求解器（如针对QF_FP理论）应用梯度下降到逻辑公式的连续松弛，但受限于梯度支配现象：少数困难子句主导优化轨迹，导致求解器陷入局部极小，无法满足整体公式。

Method: GradSAT将每个SMT子句视为独立的多任务学习任务，应用GradNorm动态梯度归一化，在运行时平衡各子句梯度幅度，惩罚主导梯度并加速滞后子句。实现采用两阶段混合流水线：第一阶段使用GPU加速的PyTorch后端（符号编译与算子融合）导航连续松弛至高质盆地；第二阶段将候选赋值交给位精确局部搜索引擎快速求解精确赋值。

Result: 通过稳定连续搜索动态，GradSAT缓解了先前基于梯度求解器的脆弱性，为复杂约束求解提供了鲁棒且高度可并行化的架构。

Conclusion: GradSAT桥接了基于优化的SMT求解与多任务学习，有效克服梯度支配，提升求解鲁棒性和并行性，适用于软件验证、程序分析和编译器测试等领域的QF_FP约束求解。

Abstract: Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) solvers are foundational to software verification, program analysis, and compiler testing, particularly over the theory of Quantifier-Free Floating-Point (QF_FP). While recent optimization-based SMT solvers have successfully applied gradient descent to continuous relaxations of logical formulas, they are fundamentally bottlenecked by gradient domination, a phenomenon where a small subset of difficult clauses hijacks the optimization trajectory, preventing the solver from satisfying the broader formula and trapping it in local minima.
  To overcome this, we present GradSAT, a novel framework that bridges optimization-based SMT solving with Multi-Task Learning (MTL). GradSAT reformulates the constraint satisfaction process by treating each SMT clause as an independent MTL task. By applying dynamic gradient normalization (GradNorm), GradSAT actively balances the gradient magnitudes across all clauses at runtime, systematically penalizing dominant gradients and accelerating lagging clauses to ensure uniform convergence. GradSAT implements this through a highly optimized, two-stage hybrid pipeline. First, a GPU-accelerated PyTorch backend leveraging symbolic compilation and operator fusion navigates the continuous relaxation to a high-quality basin. Second, the candidate assignment is handed off to a bit-precise local search engine to rapidly resolve the exact, rigorous assignment. By stabilizing the continuous search dynamics, GradSAT mitigates the brittleness of prior gradient-based solvers and provides a robust, highly parallelizable architecture for complex constraint solving.

</details>


### [134] [Can AI Agents Make Open-Ended Scientific Discovery? Evidence from Station](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08927)
*Wenyu Du,Stephen Chung*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该研究探究AI在开放式科学发现中的自主能力，提出Station环境并加入监督与元反思机制，显著提升了对ICLR论文中研究发现的重现率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统在明确指标的科学发现任务中进展迅速，但在开放式科学发现中的自主性尚不明确。

Method: 构建Station开放世界环境模拟科学生态系统，加入Supervisor机制与周期性Meta Reflection以鼓励持续探索；基于三篇ICLR口头论文构建开放式任务，向智能体提供研究问题但隐去结果并禁用网络；将原始发现拆分为独立标准来评估重现比例，并进行消融与行为分析。

Result: Station平均重新发现62.7%的标准，而Codex Multiagent-v2为15.4%，AI Scientist-v2为14.4-20.6%；消融和行为分析表明两种机制同时加入可改善研究覆盖率和连续性；在两个无参考答案的开放式任务上，智能体的部分发现与知识截止日期后研究人员报告的发现高度一致。

Conclusion: 合适的环境能使智能体在开放式科学发现中自主取得有意义的进展。

Abstract: Recent AI systems have made rapid progress in scientific discovery when given well-defined metrics, but whether they can autonomously undertake open-ended scientific discovery remains unclear. We investigate AI's ability to tackle open-ended tasks in Station, an open-world environment in which multiple agents simulate a scientific ecosystem. To tackle challenges specific to open-ended tasks, we propose augmenting Station with two mechanisms: a Supervisor mechanism and periodic Meta Reflection, which encourage persistent exploration even when intermediate metrics are lacking. We construct open-ended tasks from three recent oral papers presented at ICLR. We give agents the main research question studied in each paper while withholding the paper's results and disabling web access. We then measure how many of the original findings-partitioned into individual criteria-agents rediscover. We find that Station rediscovers 62.7% of the criteria on average, compared with 15.4% for Codex Multiagent-v2 and 14.4-20.6% for AI Scientist-v2. Ablation and behavioral analyses indicate that adding the two mechanisms together improves research coverage and continuity. We further evaluate Station on two open-ended tasks without oracle papers and find that some of the discoveries made by the agents closely match discoveries reported by researchers after the knowledge cutoff date. Together, these results indicate that a suitable environment can enable agents to autonomously make meaningful progress in open-ended scientific discovery.

</details>


### [135] [FinVector-Market-4B: A Controlled Study of LoRA Adaptation for Structured Financial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08882)
*Alina Khaybullina*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: FinVector-Market-4B通过LoRA微调Qwen3.5-4B，在金融任务上取得显著提升，但增益受任务分布和提示合约限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究紧凑金融领域适配能否在匹配提示下带来超越输出格式学习的任务特定增益。

Method: 对Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B进行rank-16 LoRA适配，使用22,000例语料，在600例基准上以隐式和显式JSON模式合约评估基座与适配模型。

Result: 仅提供模式使基座JSON有效性从0%升至91.3%；显式提示下FinQA精确匹配从14.7%到40.0%，计算表达式正确率从48.0%到82.7%，场景分支标签一致从20.1%到89.5%，蕴含方向一致从52.4%到87.2%；策略评分审计显示宏观F1下降因标签集变化，同三类目标下基座77.4%、适配器83.1%。

Conclusion: 紧凑金融领域适配在匹配提示下可产生显著任务特定增益，但受评估任务分布和提示合约约束，且基准泛化声明受文件重叠和计算目标不一致影响。

Abstract: FinVector-Market-4B adapts Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B with rank-16 LoRA on a 22,000-example corpus for structured financial tasks. We evaluate the base and adapted models on the same 600-example benchmark under implicit and explicit JSON-schema contracts. Supplying the schema alone raises base-model JSON validity from 0% to 91.3%. Under matched explicit prompting, the frozen scores improve from 14.7% to 40.0% for FinQA answer exact match, from 48.0% to 82.7% for calculator-expression correctness, from 20.1% to 89.5% for scenario branch-label agreement, and from 52.4% to 87.2% for implication-direction agreement. A post-hoc policy-scoring audit shows that the reported macro-F1 decline reflects a changing label set; using the same three target classes gives 77.4% for the base and 83.1% for the adapter. Filing overlap and calculator-target inconsistencies qualify the benchmark's generalization claims. The results show that compact financial domain adaptation can produce substantial task-specific gains beyond output-format learning under matched prompting, with gains bounded by the evaluated task distribution and prompt contract.

</details>


### [136] [Work While They Sleep: Exploiting Evaluation Latency for Fully Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08969)
*Gustavo Sutter,Alejandro Comas-Leon,David Holzmüller,Hao Wang,Luis Ricardez-Sandoval,Pascal Poupart,Agustinus Kristiadi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出ELF-BO算法，利用目标函数评估延迟进行全贝叶斯优化，在保持决策延迟与标准BO相当的同时达到全贝叶斯方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒优化中目标评估耗时且昂贵，标准贝叶斯优化仅用点估计拟合超参数，全贝叶斯方法虽能更好处理不确定性但计算成本过高，难以实际应用。

Method: ELF-BO在目标函数评估期间从超参数后验中采样，待观测到目标值后仅对样本重新加权，从而提前计算下一次建议，实现全贝叶斯优化而不显著增加决策时间。

Result: 在合成函数和真实应用中，ELF-BO性能与全贝叶斯方法相当，决策延迟与标准BO相当或更优。

Conclusion: ELF-BO使全贝叶斯优化在实际应用中变得可行。

Abstract: Black-box optimization problems are ubiquitous across science and engineering, often dealing with expensive objective functions. This objective latency has two consequences during optimization: (i) the objective evaluation dominates execution time, and (ii) sample-efficient algorithms are crucial to accelerate development and avoid wasting resources. Bayesian optimization (BO) methods are the \textit{de facto} choice of planners for suggesting the next point to try. Standard BO fits the surrogate model's hyperparameters with a point estimate. Alternatively, a fully Bayesian approach uses model averaging to account for uncertainty over the hyperparameters, leading to better uncertainty estimates---useful in the low-data regime that is pervasive in BO. However, it is often prohibitively expensive and thus rarely used. In this work, we propose ELF-BO, an algorithm that uses the objective evaluation latency to headstart the computation of the next suggestion, allowing for fully Bayesian optimization without incurring substantial decision-time costs. This is done by sampling from the hyperparameter posterior \emph{while} the objective is being evaluated, only requiring reweighting of the samples once the objective value is observed. Across synthetic functions and real-world applications, we show that ELF-BO matches the performance of fully Bayesian methods while only incurring decision latency on par with or better than standard BO. Thus, ELF-BO makes fully Bayesian optimization practical in real-world use cases.

</details>


### [137] [When the Governor Becomes the Disturbance: Control-Generated Disturbance and Cost-Aware Backoff in Governed Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09037)
*Veronique Ziegler*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 在受控的文件恢复环境中，监督者干扰工具使用智能体，通过自适应退避规则可减少持续阻塞并提高任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究监督者（governor）对受监管工具使用智能体的干扰可能性，特别是监管强度增加导致工具故障，以及成本无差别监督者如何将这些故障转化为持续阻塞。

Method: 构建受控文件恢复环境，实验性施加工具故障，比较成本无差别监督者与基于已知诱发事件移动平均降低干预概率的退避规则。使用手工编码的随机策略智能体，测试结果替换和损坏工具参数执行两种故障模式；并进行Gemini 2.5 Flash实验，涵盖6个任务共576个回合。

Result: 在随机策略智能体上，故障模式在两种条件下均出现；对于持续策略，自适应退避比干预频率近似匹配的固定弱监督者提高了完成率。Gemini 2.5 Flash实验中，退避减少了阻塞并提高了完成率；在测试设置中，中等退避强度取得最高观察到的总体成功率。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了干预成本与持续动作阻塞之间的交互作用，并提供了一种可能的缓解措施。成本机制是施加的，其诱发事件对退避规则直接可观察；超出此受控环境的适用性仍是实证问题。

Abstract: Supervisory governors can interfere with the tool-using agents they regulate. We study this possibility in a controlled file-recovery environment where increases in regulatory intensity trigger experimentally imposed tool failures. A cost-blind governor can turn these failures into persistent blocking that prevents task completion. We compare this governor with a backoff rule that reduces intervention probability using a moving average of known induced events. On a hand-coded stochastic-policy agent, the failure pattern appears under both result replacement and execution of corrupted tool arguments. For the persistent policy, adaptive backoff improves completion relative to a fixed weak governor with approximately matched intervention frequency. A Gemini 2.5 Flash experiment comprising 576 episodes across 6 tasks also shows reduced blocking and improved completion under backoff; among the tested settings, intermediate backoff strength achieves the highest observed aggregate success. These results identify an interaction between intervention cost and persistent action blocking, together with a possible mitigation. The cost mechanisms are imposed and their induced events are directly observable to the backoff rule; applicability beyond this controlled environment remains an empirical question.

</details>


### [138] [Neighborhood Smoothing for Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09020)
*Idan Horowitz,Avigdor Gal*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文提出将图平滑作为训练时校准的一般原则，通过惩罚表示空间中相邻样本预测分布的JS散度来缓解神经网络过度自信问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练时校准方法主要修改任务损失或校准惩罚，未充分利用学习表示中的邻域结构，导致校准效果有限。

Method: 引入图平滑原则，理论分析其效果并推导边界，提出基于图的训练时正则化器，惩罚相邻样本预测分布间的Jensen-Shannon散度。

Result: 在标准校准基准上，该方法提升预测质量，且与后处理校准互补；在八个图像和表格设置中，经温度缩放后，有七个设置取得所有评估训练时方法中最低的NLL。

Conclusion: 在学习表示上进行图平滑对神经网络校准具有重要价值，能有效改善校准性能。

Abstract: Modern neural networks are often miscalibrated, with a tendency to overconfidence. Existing train-time calibration methods largely modify task losses or calibration penalties, leaving neighborhood structure in learned representations underexploited. We introduce graph smoothing as a general principle for train-time calibration, which encourages similar predictive distributions across neighboring samples in representation space. We analyze the effects of graph smoothing, deriving bounds that connect predictive divergence between neighboring samples to local confidence variation and to the propagation of pointwise calibration error, and characterize the conditions under which smoothing can or cannot improve calibration. In light of this analysis, we propose \modelNoSpace, a graph-based train-time regularizer that penalizes the Jensen--Shannon divergence between predictive distributions of neighboring samples. We present a thorough empirical analysis, showing that across standard calibration benchmarks, \model improves predictive quality, and the improvement is complementary to post-hoc calibration: after temperature scaling, \model attains the lowest NLL of all evaluated train-time methods in seven of the eight image and tabular settings. These findings demonstrate the value of graph smoothing over learned representations for neural network calibration.

</details>


### [139] [Justice After Identity: Large Language Models and the View from Everywhere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09053)
*W. Russell Neuman*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了利用大语言模型（LLMs）近似罗尔斯“原初状态”的可能性，并比较了人类、基础模型与前沿/微调模型在道德困境判断中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类对正义与公平的分歧源于社会地位、个人利益、文化传承和历史环境等自利因素；罗尔斯的“原初状态”试图通过无知之幕克服这一局限，但批评者质疑人们能否真正悬置社会身份。LLMs没有单一阶级、种族、性别、国籍或传记，却编码了广泛人类身份与道德传统的语言表征，可能实现一种“来自各处的视角”的整合性原初状态。

Method: 呈现数据比较人类、基础模型和前沿/微调模型对经典道德困境的判断，并系统变化身份关系以及罗尔斯式身份约束条件。

Result: 摘要未报告具体结果，但通过比较不同模型与人类在身份关系及罗尔斯约束下的道德判断，检验LLMs是否能近似整合性原初状态。

Conclusion: 作者推测：若先进AI系统提供深思熟虑的建议，人类是否真的可能接受；并认为人类不太可能完全将集体分配与程序过程的代理控制权交给计算系统，但AI可在与个体和集体人类判断互动中发挥积极作用，尤其在面对日益极化的公平正义观点时。

Abstract: The search for a common view of justice and fairness has challenged human collective activity, as our diverging judgments are unavoidably shaped by the self-interests of social position, personal benefit, cultural inheritance, and historical circumstance. John Rawls famously attempted to overcome this limitation through popularizing a philosophical tradition known by the phrase "the original position" - a thought experiment by which people select principles of justice without knowing the identities or advantages they will possess. Critics, however, have long questioned whether people can meaningfully suspend their social identities and suppress morally relevant forms of lived experience. Artificial intelligence engaged to calculate algorithmic and agentic fairness introduces a novel possibility. LLMs have no singular class, race, gender, nationality, or biography, yet their parameters encode linguistic representations of a vast range of human identities and moral traditions. Perhaps the ethical judgments of LLMs could approximate an integrative original position - a "view from everywhere" generated not by excluding social identities but by computationally incorporating their diversity.It is unlikely that humankind will "hand over the keys" to computational systems by simply delegating complete agentic control of distributive and procedural collective processes. But AI may play a role, perhaps a positive one, interacting with individual and collective human judgment as we often confront increasingly polarized views on what is fair and just. We present data comparing human, base model, and frontier/fine-tuned model judgments about classic moral dilemmas while systematically varying identity relationships and Rawlsian constraints on identity. We conclude by speculating whether, if advanced AI systems provide humans with thoughtful advice, humans would actually be likely to accept it.

</details>


### [140] [RippleCP: Measuring Counterfactual Checkpoint Advantage in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09088)
*Mayur Akewar,Ravi Ranjan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出反事实检查点优势，衡量检查点候选是否值得物化；在SWE-bench试点上发现收益分化为两个数量级差异的区间，且经典已用工作量规则会错误定价第二个检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体检查点系统只决定恢复相关状态、快照方式和回滚是否可接受，但不决定哪些安全边界值得物化；恢复成本高昂，需要量化检查点的实际收益以指导放置策略。

Method: 定义反事实检查点优势为物化候选检查点相对于跳过它所带来的未来恢复成本降低；通过驱动CP分支和SKIP分支到同一逻辑失败，并在匹配的模型、工具、验证器和停止条件下恢复两者来测量；在12个SWE-bench Verified任务和106个真实恢复分支的冻结试点上评估。

Result: 检查点平均每任务节省49.4秒，但收益分为两个相差两个数量级的区间：首个检查点在156.5秒受保护工作上返回100.0秒，转化率0.64；稍后一步的第二个检查点在41.8秒上返回-1.1秒，转化率-0.03。恢复是重新推导而非重放，因此保留的工作量不能很好指示节省的工作量；经典已用工作量规则按全额名义成本错误定价第二个检查点。

Conclusion: 识别了检查点放置可能带来收益的位置，并设定了放置策略必须达到的门槛；恢复成本结构要求超越经典已用工作量规则来评估检查点价值。

Abstract: Agent checkpoint systems decide what state is recovery-relevant, how to snapshot it, and whether rollback is admissible. None decides which of the safe boundaries they expose are worth materializing. We formulate this as counterfactual checkpoint advantage, the reduction in future recovery cost obtained by checkpointing a candidate rather than skipping it, and measure it by driving a CP branch and a SKIP branch to the same logical failure and recovering both under matched model, tool, verifier, and stopping conditions. On a frozen pilot of 12 SWE-bench Verified tasks and 106 real recovery branches, checkpointing saves 49.4 s per task, and that figure resolves into two regimes two orders of magnitude apart. The first checkpoint returns 100.0 s on 156.5 s of protected work, a conversion of 0.64; a second one step later returns-1.1 s on 41.8 s, a conversion of -0.03. Recovery is a re-derivation rather than a replay, so preserved work is a poor guide to saved work, and the classical elapsed-work rule misprices the second checkpoint by its full nominal cost. We identify where placement can pay, and set the bar a placement policy must clear.

</details>


### [141] [Defining Purpose-Limited Secrets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10010)
*Bhumika Mittal,Aalok Thakkar*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文提出将密码学秘密的“用途”作为秘密本身的属性，并定义了三种执行机制：限制、调解和问责。以支付令牌为例，展示了如何在一个秘密上结合三种机制，并给出了安全性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统中，秘密的用途仅在策略中声明，而实际执行仅限制能力，导致能力通常大于用途，例如用于聚合的解密密钥可读取所有记录，支付单张发票的令牌可耗尽账户。因此需要将用途作为秘密的属性来强制执行。

Method: 定义可允许操作集A(P)来代表声明的预期用途I，并针对同一I定义三种机制：限制（预期用途外的操作不可行）、调解（可信组件拒绝此类操作）、问责（超出声明范围的使用可行但可归因于持有者）。以支付令牌为例，结合三种机制，并基于无随机预言机的不可伪造签名和抗碰撞哈希证明衰减不可伪造性，在随机预言机模型下基于离散对数和不可伪造签名证明可追溯性和不可陷害性。

Result: 限制性博弈（不可预测性和不可区分性）与功能签名不可伪造性和约束PRF伪随机性的密钥查询形式一致，并对应于仿真安全功能加密在其可行性边界内；针对I，它们还记录了一个允许过多的谓词。支付令牌可支付一笔有上限的费用给一个商户，可缩小但不可扩大，由商户检查，若重复花费则识别提款账户。衰减不可伪造性归约到无随机预言机的不可伪造签名和抗碰撞哈希；可追溯性和不可陷害性在随机预言机模型下基于离散对数和不可伪造签名成立。

Conclusion: 该框架没有提出新的原语、困难假设或通用组合定理，而是作为一个通用规范，用于陈述和比较此类原语。

Abstract: A cryptographic secret is issued for a purpose but grants a capability, and the capability is usually larger: a decryption key meant for computing aggregates can read every record, and a token meant to pay one invoice can drain the account. Deployed systems state the purpose in policy and enforce only the capability. We make the purpose a property of the secret. A mechanism sees operations, not reasons, so a scheme enforces an admissible set $A(P)$ of operations that stands in for a declared intended use $I$; whether $I$ captures the human purpose is a modeling obligation that marks where policy takes over. Against the same $I$ we define three regimes: under confinement an operation outside the intended use is infeasible, under mediation a trusted component refuses it, and under accountability use beyond a stated bound is possible but attributed to the holder. The confinement games, for unpredictability and indistinguishability, coincide with the key-query forms of functional-signature unforgeability and constrained-PRF pseudorandomness and correspond to simulation-secure functional encryption within its feasibility boundary; stated against $I$, they also register a predicate that permits too much. A payment token uses all three regimes on one secret: it pays one capped charge at one merchant, can be narrowed but not widened, is checked by the merchant, and identifies the withdrawing account if spent twice. Attenuation unforgeability reduces to unforgeable signatures and a collision-resistant hash without random oracles; traceability and non-frameability hold under discrete log and unforgeable signatures in the random-oracle model. We claim no new primitive, hardness assumption, or general composition theorem; the framework is a common specification against which such primitives are stated and compared.

</details>


### [142] [BetweenCut: Private Heavy-Node Classification with Doubly Logarithmic Error in Tree Height](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10075)
*Ergute Bao,Graham Cormode,Xiaokui Xiao,Ting Yu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出BetweenCut算法，在树上寻找计数超过阈值的重节点时，实现记录级差分隐私，并将加性误差界从Ω(log h)或Ω(√log h)改进为O(log log h)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树上重节点查找是结构化数据分析与学习的基础操作。现有方法因每条记录沿根到叶路径贡献计数，隐私成本随树高累积，且需多次阈值比较，导致加性误差较大，亟需在不牺牲精度的前提下实现记录级差分隐私。

Method: 提出(ε,δ)-差分隐私算法BetweenCut，通过优化隐私预算分配或阈值比较策略，降低沿路径累积的隐私成本，从而将加性误差控制在对数对数级别。

Result: BetweenCut对树高h的加性误差界为O_{ε,δ}(log log h)，优于现有Ω_{ε,δ}(log h)和Ω_{ε,δ}(√log h)的界；该误差同时适用于所有节点，且与输入数据库大小无关。

Conclusion: BetweenCut显著改进了深树场景下重节点查找的差分隐私误差界，为结构化数据上的隐私保护分析提供了更高效的基础工具。

Abstract: Finding heavy nodes in a tree---those whose counts exceed a given threshold---is a building block for analysis and learning over structured data. Achieving record-level differential privacy (DP) without sacrificing accuracy is challenging because each record contributes to counts along an entire root-to-leaf path, allowing privacy costs to accumulate across levels. Existing methods account for the multiple threshold comparisons for each record incur additive error margins of $Ω_{\varepsilon,δ}(\log h)$ or $Ω_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ for tree height $h$. We introduce \textsc{BetweenCut}, an $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP algorithm with an additive error margin of $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\log\log h)$, improving the existing bounds for deep trees. This error holds simultaneously for all nodes and is independent of the input database size.

</details>


### [143] [Which Buildings Are Artificial Intelligence-Ready? A Measurement-Based Assessment Framework for AI Question Answering and Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09119)
*Wooyoung Jung*

Main category: Model
