TL;DR: 该研究提出一个量化建筑对代理式AI就绪度的框架，通过知识图谱设定回答与执行两个上限，并用参考AI代理检验实际上限实现程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式AI正成为建筑交互与控制接口，但缺乏系统评估建筑对其就绪度的方法。

Method: 构建建筑知识图谱以计算可回答就绪度上限（数据可回答的运维问题比例）和执行就绪度上限（暴露的控制动作比例）；再用参考AI代理在固定问题集上的准确率衡量上限实现程度。

Result: 模拟办公室中，代理实现0.62的可回答就绪度（上限0.64），说明限制来自缺失数据而非AI，但故障原因命名除外；37个真实建筑图谱中位可回答上限为0.16，45个中有15个因未链接传感器而降低。

Conclusion: 该框架将“建筑是否AI就绪”转化为可审计、可排序的改造问题。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) systems are becoming the interface to buildings, answering questions and controlling operations, but a building's readiness for them has not been systematically assessed. This study proposes a framework to quantify it. First, a building's knowledge graph sets two ceilings. The answerable-readiness ceiling is the share of operational questions its data could answer, and the actuation-readiness ceiling is the share of control actions it exposes. Second, a reference AI agent's accuracy on a fixed set of these questions shows how much of the ceilings is realized. On a simulated office, the agent realizes 0.62 of a 0.64 answerable ceiling, so missing data, not the AI, limit readiness, except in naming a fault's cause. Across 37 public real-building graphs, the median answerable ceiling is 0.16, and in 15 of 45, unlinked sensors lower it. The framework turns "is this building AI-ready?" into an auditable, ranked retrofit question.

</details>


### [144] [Learning Cross-Model Activation Alignments with Explicit Many-to-Many Layer Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09058)
*Alina Sudakov,Guy Bar-Shalom,Fabrizio Frasca,Haggai Maron*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出MATCHA方法，学习LLM间逐层激活对齐，将映射分解为可提取的层映射矩阵和层共享特征映射，联合优化并验证其跨模型迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM快速迭代发布，需理解两个独立训练模型间的对应关系：哪些层对应、特征如何变换。已有方法预先固定层对应（按相对深度配对），缺乏从数据中学习层对应关系的能力。

Method: MATCHA将源模型逐层激活到目标模型的映射分解为两个因子：(1) 层映射，输出显式的target-by-source矩阵，可提取和检视；(2) 层共享的特征映射，作用于隐藏空间。两个因子通过提示（prompts）联合学习，不预先固定层对应。

Result: 在7个模型、3个模型族组成的42对模型组合上，MATCHA比先前方法更忠实地重建目标激活，并显著提升检索类指标。恢复的映射整体随深度单调，但呈现一致的多对多关系：每个目标层对应源模型的一个层带。

Conclusion: MATCHA实现了无需预定义层对应的激活对齐，支持激活空间干预迁移，使为某一模型开发的引导向量和探针可迁移到另一模型。

Abstract: LLMs are released at a rapid pace, raising a natural question: how do two independently trained models relate, both in which layers correspond and in how features transform between them? We study this by learning an activation alignment, a map from a source model's layerwise activations to a target's. Our method, MATCHA, factors this map into a layer map, whose output is an explicit target-by-source matrix that can be extracted and inspected, and a layer-shared feature map between hidden spaces. Most of prior work fixes the layer correspondence in advance, pairing layers at roughly the same relative depth; in contrast, we learn both factors jointly from prompts. Across 42 pairs of seven models spanning three different families, MATCHA reconstructs the target's activations more faithfully and improves retrieval-based metrics substantially, w.r.t. previous approaches. The recovered maps are broadly monotone in depth but, in contrast with most previous approaches, are consistently many-to-many: each target layer draws on a band of source layers. Our alignments also enable transfer of activation-space interventions, allowing steering vectors and probes developed for one model to transfer to another.

</details>


### [145] [Tucker Bottleneck Attention for Multi-Dimensional Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09090)
*Ryan Solgi,Parsa Madinei,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Tucker瓶颈注意力(TuBA)，利用低秩张量结构实现高效全局令牌混合，在视频预测和天气预测中取得更优精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力的二次计算成本限制了其从多维数据扩展到长序列的能力。

Method: TuBA将隐藏张量投影到紧凑的Tucker核心，在核心上执行多头自注意力和线性投影，并将更新写回原空间，实现亚二次计算；其自回归扩展在核心内结合双向交互、核心间采用因果注意力。

Result: 在视频预测和全球天气预测上，TuBA相比标准自注意力分别降低误差和计算量达24.7%/66.6%和37.1%/85.1%，加速比最高4.27倍，优于标准/高效注意力和任务专用模型。

Conclusion: 低秩Tucker核心和多帧生成分别优于全秩注意力和逐帧生成，验证了TuBA的有效性。

Abstract: The quadratic cost of self-attention limits scalability to long sequences from multidimensional data. We introduce Tucker bottleneck attention (TuBA), which exploits low-rank tensor structure for efficient global token mixing. TuBA projects hidden tensors into compact Tucker cores, performs multi-head self-attention and linear projections on the cores, and writes updates back to the ambient space, enabling subquadratic computation. Its autoregressive extension combines bidirectional interactions within cores with causal attention across cores. On video prediction and global weather forecasting, TuBA achieves favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over standard and efficient attention and task-specific models. Compared to standard self-attention, TuBA reduces error and computation by up to 24.7% and 66.6% for video prediction and 37.1% and 85.1% for autoregressive weather forecasting, with speedups up to 4.27 times. Low-rank Tucker cores and multi-frame generation also outperform full-rank attention and frame-by-frame generation, respectively.

</details>


### [146] [From Probabilities to Decisions: Search and Multi-Teacher Distillation with Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09188)
*Mohamad Yazan Sadoun,Sarah Sharif,Yaser Mike Banad*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 研究仅输出概率的模型Jev在严格约束决策任务中的应用，通过蒸馏使其可用于搜索，并探讨与LLM合作的最佳标注预算分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率-only模型（System One）能快速返回固定选择的校准概率，但需验证其在需前瞻的决策任务中的价值，以及如何与LLM教师合作高效利用标注预算。

Method: 在子弹棋中，将Jev判断嵌入Stockfish搜索，并通过蒸馏成紧凑评估器实现每步使用；在文本重排序中，比较Jev与Qwen3-32B标注训练的模型，并测试在固定预算下平均两者标注的效果。

Result: 子弹棋bot达到Lichess 2200+；平均Jev和Qwen标注比仅用Qwen提升9.6 Elo（95% CI 4.3-14.9），且可复现；同一评委的第二答案无增益；Jev是Qwen最佳搭档。重排序中，Jev单独训练与Qwen相当，但仅需21分钟API调用（对比5.1 GPU小时），加入Qwen增益仅千分之几。

Conclusion: 搜索提供前瞻，蒸馏使判断廉价可用，LLM搭档在棋类中有效；在重排序中Jev高效且LLM增益有限。

Abstract: Probability-only models, which TypeSafe calls System One models, return calibrated probabilities for fixed choices in milliseconds and generate no text. We study one such model, Jev, through two tasks that require decisions under tight constraints. In bullet chess, a bot that places Jev's judgment inside Stockfish search alongside an opening book and endgame tablebases climbs above a 2200 Lichess bullet rating against other bots. Live model calls are too slow for search, so we distill pairwise judgments into a compact evaluator that runs at every position. We then ask how best to spend a fixed labeling budget when an LLM, Qwen3-32B, is available as a second teacher. In chess, averaging both judges' labels beats spending the whole budget on Qwen alone by 9.6 Elo (95% interval 4.3 to 14.9), and the gain replicates on fresh openings; a second answer from the same judge is no substitute, and Jev is the strongest partner for Qwen among the models tested. In passage reranking, Jev's labels alone train a reranker that scores as high as Qwen's, from 21 minutes of API calls instead of 5.1 GPU-hours, and adding Qwen gains at most a few thousandths in ranking quality. Search supplies the lookahead, distillation makes the judgment cheap enough to use at every position, and an LLM partner pays off in chess.

</details>


### [147] [FreeEvolve: Learning to Evolve Beyond Fixed Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09197)
*Lecheng Kong,Like Hui,Nikos Kanakaris,Prithwish Jana,Sahika Genc,Narayanan Sadagopan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出FREEEVOLVE，自动化语言模型智能体的进化优化过程，通过学习进化技能而非手工设计搜索循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体进化器虽能自动设计提示、技能和工作流，但其优化过程本身仍由手工设计的固定搜索循环控制，缺乏自主性。

Method: 环境给定目标、目标智能体、评估器、数据和资源限制；进化器在限制内自主决定测试内容、证据量、候选保留与停止时机，这些决策遵循可编辑的进化技能，并通过元进化在生成的新目标智能体上评分来改进技能。

Result: 在tau3-bench、ARC-AGI-2、ARC-AGI-3和Terminal-Bench 2.1上，FREEEVOLVE自主控制进化过程，平均提升主要留出指标13.6分，匹配或超越手工设计的进化器；元进化技能比种子技能在新目标智能体上额外提升6.9分。

Conclusion: 优化过程成为从经验中习得的能力，而非预先设计的循环，且习得过程随经验持续改进，展示出跨环境的可迁移性。

Abstract: Agent evolvers automate the design of the prompts, skills and workflows around language model agents, yet the optimization process they follow is still designed by hand: a fixed search loop decides how candidates are evaluated, which are kept and when the search stops. We propose FREEEVOLVE, which automates this process as well. An environment specifies the goal, target agent, evaluator, data and resource limits; within these limits, the evolver itself decides what to test, how much evidence to collect, which candidates to pursue and when to stop. These decisions follow an editable evolution skill, which we improve through meta-evolution by scoring each candidate skill on the fresh target agent it produces. The optimization process thus becomes a capability learned from experience rather than a loop engineered in advance. On tau3-bench, ARC-AGI-2, ARC-AGI-3 and Terminal-Bench 2.1, FREEEVOLVE controls the evolution campaign by itself, yet improves the primary held-out metric by 13.6 points on average and matches or exceeds hand-designed evolvers. The learned process keeps improving with experience: meta-evolved skills add 6.9 points over the seed skill on fresh target agents, demonstrating transferability across environments.

</details>


### [148] [Domain-informed Adaptive Sampling for Generalizable PINNs in Metal Additive Manufacturing via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09126)
*Hyeonsu Lee,Jihoon Jeong*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向金属增材制造热建模的物理信息神经网络自适应采样策略，通过条件流匹配学习高残差分布，显著提升了泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINNs在金属增材制造中多采用手工设计、静态的配点采样策略，缺乏理论依据且难以跨工艺条件扩展，限制了泛化能力。

Method: 基于经验风险最小化的理论分析，证明工艺条件感知的自适应采样优于静态采样；提出两阶段框架：(1) 条件流匹配模型学习不同工艺条件下的近似高残差分布；(2) 混合采样策略，将该分布与领域知识基础分布结合，生成自适应配点以精炼PINN预测器。

Result: 在金属增材制造数值基准上，相同配点预算下，相对L2误差平均降低62.1%，持续优于最先进的PINN基线，并能捕捉文献中常被忽略的工艺相关散热区域。

Conclusion: 这是首个用于金属增材制造中PINNs的自适应采样策略，有助于提升泛化能力和更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Accurate thermal modeling is essential in metal additive manufacturing (AM) for understanding the process-structure-property chain. Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) offer effective surrogate thermal modeling by minimizing physics-based residual losses at collocation points. However, prior works typically rely on manually-crafted, static collocation sampling strategies, which are neither principled nor scalable across process conditions, hindering their generalization capability. In this work, we provide theoretical analysis through empirical risk minimization, showing that process condition-aware adaptive sampling is strictly more favorable than conventional static sampling for generalization. Building on this insight, we propose an adaptive sampling strategy within a two-stage framework: (1) a conditional Flow Matching model that learns approximate high-residual distributions across different process conditions, and (2) a mixed sampling strategy combining this distribution with a domain-informed base distribution to generate adaptive collocation points for refining the PINN predictor. Experiments on metal AM numerical benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art PINN baselines, achieving an average 62.1\% reduction in relative $L_2$ error under an identical collocation budget, by capturing process-dependent heat dissipation regions often overlooked in the literature. To the authors' knowledge, this is the first adaptive sampling strategy for PINNs in metal AM, contributing to the enhanced generalization and broader applicability.

</details>


### [149] [A Cognitive-Aware QML-CRL Framework for Detecting Affinity and Romance-Investment Fraud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09141)
*Bibhas Adhikari,Ramya Srinivasan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种混合量子-经典框架，通过建模操纵性对话中的认知偏差来检测情感与恋爱投资诈骗，并在合成对话上评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感与恋爱投资诈骗依赖操纵性对话中的认知偏差，传统方法难以有效捕捉这些偏差及其时间顺序和共现关系，因此需要新的检测框架。

Method: 将关键认知偏差编码到结构化参数化量子电路的专用量子比特中，并设置帧量子比特以敏感于操纵性重构的时间顺序，叙事量子比特通过可训练纠缠层聚合共现；电路参数与经典强化学习智能体联合训练，该智能体将逐轮是否标记对话建模为最优停止问题。

Result: 在包含困难负样本、合法但紧急以及合法但强势推销对话的合成对话数据集上评估了模型性能。

Conclusion: 该混合量子-经典框架能够结合认知偏差建模与强化学习决策，为检测情感与恋爱投资诈骗提供了一种可行方案，并在具有挑战性的合成对话上验证了其性能。

Abstract: We present a hybrid quantum-classical framework that detects affinity and romance-investment fraud by modelling the cognitive biases in a manipulative conversation. In our proposed framework, cognitive biases central to this fraud class are carried by dedicated qubits in a structured parameterized quantum circuit, together with a frame qubit makes the encoding sensitive to the temporal order of manipulative reframing, and a narrative qubit that aggregates co-occurrence through a trainable entanglement layer. The circuit parameters are trained jointly with a classical reinforcement-learning agent that decides, turn by turn, whether to flag the conversation, modeled as an optimal stopping problem. We evaluate the model's performance on synthetic conversations that include hard negatives, legitimate but urgent, and legitimate but pushy sales conversations.

</details>


### [150] [We Query, Therefore We Compute: On Oracle Computation beyond the Machine, with an Application to Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09243)
*Kefan Liu,Fengning Ou,Yelin Luo,Jingdi Lei*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个抽象机器，将LLM视为Oracle，扩展双栈下推自动机，以统一Agentic系统中Workflows和Agents的视图，并提供操作系统式的抽象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic系统从操作系统零散借用调度、缓存、隔离等抽象，缺乏统一基础，且对Workflows和Agents两种形式的共同视图有限。

Method: 将LLM视为Oracle，扩展双栈下推自动机，新增一条指令：将整个栈作为查询交给Oracle，并将答案追加到同一栈。机器执行两种计算：Oracle的计算和图灵完备的Priestess计算。通过存储对称性破缺S和转换对称性破缺T，使Priestess程序成为Oracle运行程序的操作系统，并产生Agent和Workflow作为任务程序的两种放置。针对内部自回归Oracle，在特定条件下每次答案结束时两种计算同步，借此建模缓存并分析调度。构造V将机器适配到冯·诺依曼计算机，并提出ArchNights（扩展RISC-V ISA和Linux风格操作系统）作为实现。

Result: 对于内部自回归Oracle，两种计算在特定条件下于每次答案结束时同步，通过同步可建模缓存和分析调度；无保证能固定两种计算间传递的内容，但能固定边界本身。ArchNights-SE在gem5上作为计算机系统运行，当运行LLM作为Oracle时成为Agentic系统，并将开源。

Conclusion: Agentic系统可像计算机系统一样设计。凭借已建立的基础和统一视图，未来工作可以共享不变量和边界，每个都有其条件。

Abstract: Agentic systems use large language models (LLMs) to carry out concrete tasks. Prior work often borrows abstractions such as scheduling, caching or isolation piecemeal from operating systems, so the mechanisms it builds share little common ground, and the shared view of the two forms of agentic system, Workflows and Agents, is limited. We construct an abstract machine that provides both.
  We treat the LLM as an Oracle and extend a two-stack pushdown automaton with one instruction, which hands the Oracle a whole stack as its query and appends the answer to that same stack. The machine thus performs two computations, the Oracle's and a Turing-complete one that we call the Priestess. A stack that the program only appends to grows autoregressively, as an agent's context does. Two symmetry breakings, S in storage and T in transitions, make a Priestess program the operating system of the programs the Oracle runs, and produce the Agent and the Workflow as the two placements of a task's program.
  For internally autoregressive Oracles, the two computations synchronize at the end of every answer under certain conditions, and through that synchronization we model caching and analyse scheduling. No guarantee that holds for every Oracle can fix which content crosses between the two computations, but such a guarantee does fix the boundary itself.
  The construction V fits the machine to a von Neumann computer. To show that it is realizable, we propose ArchNights, an extended RISC-V ISA and a Linux-style operating system implementing the machine by design. ArchNights-SE runs on gem5 as a computer system, becomes an agentic system when it runs an LLM as the Oracle, and will be open source.
  Agentic systems can then be designed as computer systems are. With a foundation built and a unified view, future work can share invariants and bounds, each with its conditions.

</details>


### [151] [An Accuracy--Information Tradeoff for Loss-Difference Conditional Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09206)
*Hazar Yueksel*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文研究了损失差条件互信息（ld-CMI）与准确率之间的关系，证明在特定线性预测器和正则化条件下，准确率会迫使ld-CMI达到Ω(n)比特，并给出了高斯噪声下的精确阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确率已知会迫使信息进入模型，但数据处理不等式不能将下界传递到损失上，因此需要探究准确率是否也迫使ld-CMI。

Method: 通过界定损失差的三阶矩，针对线性预测器、光滑凸损失（如logistic损失）和幂次r≥2的正则化器，在缩放符号立方体上的乘积分布中分析。

Result: 在最优样本量n≍ε^{-2+2/r}下，每个期望超额风险≤ε的正常学习器的最坏情况ld-CMI为Ω(n)比特；高斯噪声下为Θ(n/(1+(τ/ε)^2))比特；无正则化时n≍ε^{-2}。

Conclusion: 范围缩放的ld-CMI界在这些分布上不能消失，尽管每个正常学习器的泛化差距为O(n^{-1/2})；模型级信息不能决定噪声损失差信息，且增长、斜率和维度条件（维度条件至多差一个对数）是必要的。

Abstract: Loss-difference conditional mutual information (ld-CMI) uses the smallest of the standard observations in the supersample hierarchy of generalization bounds: it measures what a learner's loss differences reveal about which candidate of each pair it was trained on. Accuracy is known to force information into the model; data processing does not carry such lower bounds to losses. We show, by bounding three moments of the loss differences, that accuracy also forces ld-CMI. For linear predictors with a smooth convex loss of nonzero slope at zero, such as the logistic loss, plus a regularizer whose curvature and growth are both of power $r\ge2$, on product distributions over a scaled sign cube in dimension at least linear in $n$, every proper learner with expected excess risk at most $\varepsilon$ on these distributions at the optimal sample size $n\asymp\varepsilon^{-2+2/r}$ has worst-case ld-CMI of order $n$ bits, and $Θ(n/(1+(τ/\varepsilon)^2))$ bits under Gaussian noise of standard deviation $τ$ on the loss differences. The same holds without a regularizer, at $n\asymp\varepsilon^{-2}$. Consequently, range-scaled ld-CMI bounds cannot vanish on these distributions, although every proper learner's generalization gap is $O(n^{-1/2})$. We also show that model-level information does not determine noisy loss-difference information, and that the growth, slope and dimension conditions are needed, the last up to a logarithm.

</details>


### [152] [From Plausible Hierarchies to Useful Taxonomies: Evaluating Agentic Harnesses on Customer Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09377)
*Prabhath Chellingi,Raviraja G,Viraj Bagal*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 生成式分类树虽能通过常规命名与结构检查，但在整体树层面存在严重质量缺陷，需引入全树度量进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类树质量检查仅针对单个节点（名称与描述匹配、父子关系正确、兄弟节点区分），缺乏对整棵树作为生产分类体系是否实用的操作性评估。

Method: 在两个专有反馈语料库（1,940和5,000条记录）上构建六个分类树：每个语料库包含一个生产参考树和两次相同输入的生成器重复运行；所有树均通过常规检查，并接受产品覆盖率检查；提出两类全树度量——结构判别器（检验树形是否从数据学习而非生成器强加）和团队可分区性（检验分支能否将反馈划分为团队可拥有的组）。

Result: 所有生成树均通过常规检查，产品覆盖率检查甚至偏好生成树；但生成树中至少97.7%的叶节点名称仅重复祖先名称（参考树为13.9%和2.9%），其中一棵树中四分之三记录落入多个顶级类别；常规检查视为同等正确的树在跨分支泄漏上相差27个百分点，且仅有一棵优于随机划分。

Conclusion: 表面合理性不足以衡量分类树质量；评估必须同时测量整棵树和每个节点。

Abstract: Taxonomies are the symbolic representations through which AI systems organize evidence, aggregate patterns, and answer questions over large document collections. Over customer feedback, the category tree decides how every record is counted and routed, which problems get seen, and which team owns them. Agentic harnesses now make it easy to generate a plausible-looking hierarchy, and such trees are checked today with generic, individually scoped checks: each name fits its description, sits under the right parent, and stays distinct from its siblings. We ask a more operational question: when is a generated hierarchy actually useful as a production taxonomy? We build six taxonomies over two proprietary feedback corpora (1,940 and 5,000 records): for each corpus, a production reference and two repeated runs of the same harness under identical inputs. All six pass every generic naming and structure check, and a deeper product-coverage check even prefers the generated trees. Yet in every generated tree at least 97.7% of leaf names merely restate an ancestor's name (13.9% and 2.9% in the references), and in one, three of every four records fall under multiple top-level categories. We introduce two families of whole-tree metrics: structural discriminators test whether a tree's shape was learned from the data or imposed by its generator; team partitionability tests whether branches split feedback into groups teams can own. Trees the generic checks rate as equally correct differ by 27 percentage points in cross-branch leakage, and only one of the two beats a random split. Surface plausibility is an insufficient measure of taxonomy quality: evaluation must measure the whole tree as well as each node.

</details>


### [153] [Reliability of LLM Judges for Evaluating Entity Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09554)
*Vaibhava Lakshmi Ravideshik,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统评估LLM作为评判者在实体对齐（EA）任务中的可靠性，发现标签暴露引发的锚定偏差会严重削弱评判能力，并提出无标签协议可恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估实体对齐系统依赖昂贵的专家标注，LLM-as-judge提供可扩展替代方案，但其在结构化预测任务（如EA）中的可靠性尚未被研究。

Method: 对三个前沿模型、三个数据集和四个EA系统进行基准测试，采用扰动偏差诊断、覆盖所有数据集-评判者-提示组合的元评估以及反事实标签翻转测试。

Result: 发现锚定偏差：可见系统决策标签时评判者区分能力反转。标签暴露导致J-ROC-AUC降至0.12-0.87；无标签协议在独特名称数据集上恢复至0.93-1.00，生物医学对上恢复至0.93-0.95。反事实实验确认因果性（FSR 53-99%），并揭示前沿模型悖论：更强的评判者对标签更敏感。双标注者盲评（102对，Cohen's kappa=0.902）直接证实该机制。

Conclusion: 发布首个生物医学EA基准（MeSH-SNOMED CT，15K对）及可复现的LLM评判可靠性审计框架，代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Entity Alignment (EA) identifies equivalent entities across knowledge graphs and is critical for knowledge base integration and ontology merging. Evaluating EA systems at scale requires expensive expert annotation, making systematic assessment across diverse domains practically infeasible. LLM-as-judge evaluation offers a potentially scalable alternative, yet its reliability for structured prediction tasks like EA remains unstudied. We present the first systematic benchmarking study across three frontier models, three datasets, and four EA systems, using perturbation bias diagnostics, meta-evaluation across all dataset-judge-prompt combinations, and counterfactual label-flip tests. We identify anchor bias, a failure mode in which judges invert discrimination when the system's decision label is visible. Label exposure causally collapses judge discrimination (J-ROC-AUC 0.12-0.87), while a label-free protocol recovers near-ceiling capability on distinctive-name datasets (0.93-1.00) and significant recovery on biomedical pairs (0.93-0.95). Counterfactual experiments confirm causality (FSR 53-99%) and reveal a frontier model paradox: stronger judges exhibit greater label sensitivity, not less. A blinded two-annotator human evaluation (102 pairs, Cohen's kappa=0.902) confirms this mechanism directly. We release the first biomedical EA benchmark (MeSH-SNOMED CT, 15K pairs) and a reproducible auditing framework for LLM judge reliability in EA. Code and data are available at https://github.com/vaibhavalakshmiravideshik/llm-as-a-judge-entity-alignment.

</details>


### [154] [Node-level Graph Neural Architecture Search Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09297)
*Lintao Yanga,Sirui Lia,Yaqing Wang,Pietro Liò,Xu Shen,Baisong Liu,Chengbin Peng*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出节点级图神经架构搜索算法N-GNAS，为不同节点子集自动选择合适网络架构，并引入对比学习损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNN对所有节点施加统一卷积操作，忽略节点结构与特征差异，导致性能下降及层数增加时的过平滑问题。

Method: N-GNAS在更新节点特征时，为每个节点子集自动选择合适网络架构；同时引入对比学习损失，使不同类别样本特征分离、同类样本特征聚集。

Result: 在八个数据集的节点分类和图分类任务上，N-GNAS优于当前主流GNAS方法及人工设计的GNN；例如在CiteSeer数据集上达到78.26%的准确率。

Conclusion: 节点级架构搜索可有效缓解统一卷积带来的局限与过平滑问题，提升图神经网络在多种任务上的性能。

Abstract: In recent years, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and architecture search frameworks have gained extensive application in non-Euclidean data processing, attributable to their superior capacity in managing unstructured data. Nevertheless, traditional approaches typically apply uniform convolution operations to all nodes, regardless of their varying structural and feature characteristics, which can undermine model performance and result in over-smoothing issues as the number of layers increases. To overcome this limitation, in this work, we propose a \textbf{N}ode-Level \textbf{G}raph \textbf{N}eural \textbf{A}rchitecture \textbf{S}earch (N-GNAS) algorithm. It can automatically choose an appropriate network architecture for each subset of nodes when updating node features. N-GNAS also introduces a contrastive learning loss to separate sample features from different categories and vice versa. In experiments conducted on eight datasets for node and graph classification, our methodology outperforms current leading GNAS techniques and traditional human-designed GNNs. For example, it achieves an accuracy rate of 78.26\% on the CiteSeer dataset.

</details>


### [155] [Automatically Building and Updating a Knowledge Graph of MLIP Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09644)
*Alexis Beer,Liudmyla Klochko,Mathieu d'Aquin*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种基于大语言模型的多步骤流程，自动构建机器学习原子间势（MLIP）领域的知识图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料科学中概念、实体等的语义表示已有诸多努力，但快速演变的机器学习预测材料性质领域仍缺乏结构化知识表示，尤其是MLIP方向。

Method: 基于LLM的流程，包括从文档和文章中抽取信息，并利用SHACL约束进行验证循环以检测和纠正错误；以逐模型方式构建，强调表示一致性，支持迭代式新增模型。

Result: 在Matbench Discovery排行榜所列模型上构建了知识图谱，并展示了可查询的几个有趣方面。

Conclusion: 该流程能够自动、迭代地构建MLIP领域知识图谱，便于新模型的加入，并支持后续知识查询。

Abstract: Complementing the many efforts in providing semantic representations of concepts, notions, and entities in materials science, we report and illustrate a process by which we can automatically build a knowledge graph of the fast evolving field of machine learning applied to the prediction of material properties, focusing on MLIP (Machine Learning Interatomic Potential). This LLM-based process relies on multiple steps, from information extraction in documents and articles to a validation loop using SHACL constraints to detect and correct errors. It is carried out on a model-by-model basis, focusing on the consistency of representation, therefore enabling an iterative construction where the addition of new models is facilitated. We illustrate the process by showing a few interesting aspects that can be queried from a knowledge graph built from the models listed in the Matbench Discovery leaderboard.

</details>


### [156] [Cost-Efficient Theorem Proving via Agent Orchestration in Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09681)
*Shuangjie Yao,Nikolaus Holzer,Mark Paul Santolucito,Baishakhi Ray,Suman Jana,Dongdong She*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 论文提出CoCo-Prover，将程序验证形式化为代价约束下的元级决策问题，基于两层证明图（声明内AND/OR超图+跨声明引理依赖图）进行目标选择与动作购买，在Lean 4五个基准上取得更优的成功率-成本前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码流畅但缺乏正确性保证，程序验证可提供机器可检查的证明；然而现有证明器只追求通过率、忽视成功与成本的权衡，而真实软件包含数百个相互依赖的证明义务，规模化场景下关键在于能否经济地证明尽可能多的定理。

Method: 以两层证明图为基础：每个声明内的AND/OR证明超图与跨声明的引理依赖图相连。每一步回答两个问题——选择哪些开放目标、在这些目标上购买哪些动作。目标选择保持符号化，作为证明图上的拓扑遍历；动作选择采用智能体编排的元级决策，由智能体路由器将每次有界专家调用视为单独定价的尽力计算，在证据积累下演化路由规则，将异构专家智能体与配置匹配。

Result: 在Lean 4的五个程序验证基准（函数级CLEVER、VERINA、AlgoVeri，仓库级NTP4VC、Vero）上，CoCo-Prover的成功率-成本前沿优于前沿编码智能体和最先进的LLM证明器等基线：在每个基准上取得最佳求解率，其中两个基准达100%；相比使用最强LLM的最强基线，成本最多降低30.9%。

Conclusion: 将成本高效的程序证明形式化为代价约束下的元级决策，并借助两层证明图与智能体路由编排，能够在不牺牲甚至提升求解率的同时显著降低成本，为规模化LLM生成代码的验证提供了更经济可行的路径。

Abstract: Program verification establishes software correctness through machine-checkable proofs constructed in theorem provers. It's a guarantee especially valuable for code generated by large language models (LLMs), which is fluent but carries no assurance of correctness. Almost all existing provers, however, pursue pass rates alone at whatever sampling or search budget it takes, and overlook the success-vs-cost frontier; yet real software often carries hundreds of interdependent proof obligations, so what matters at scale is not whether one theorem can be proved, but how many can be proved economically. We introduce CoCo-Prover, which formalizes cost-efficient program proving as metalevel decision-making under cost, grounded on two-level proof graphs: an AND/OR proof hypergraph within each declaration is joined to a lemma-dependency graph across declarations; and at each step, it answers two questions: which open goals to select, and which actions to purchase on these goals. Selection stays symbolic as a topological pass over the proof graphs. Action choice is agent orchestration via metalevel decision-making: an agentic router treats every bounded specialist invocation as a separately priced, best-effort computation, matching heterogeneous specialist agents together with configurations, under evolved routing rules as evidence accumulates. On five program verification benchmarks in Lean 4 including function-level CLEVER, VERINA, and AlgoVeri, and repository-level NTP4VC and Vero, we show that CoCo-Prover achieves a better success-vs-cost frontier than baselines including frontier coding agents and state-of-the-art LLM-based provers: it achieves the best solve rate on every benchmark and up to 100% on two benchmarks. It also reduces cost by up to 30.9% compared to the strongest baseline with the strongest LLM in our evaluation.

</details>


### [157] [A Tale of Two Error Categories: Exploring Concealed Trade-Offs in the Errors of Automated Judges in Evaluation of Uncertainty Quantifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09693)
*Evgenia Ilia,Wilker Aziz*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文分析了自动判断错误对不确定性量化评估可靠性的影响，发现判断性能只能粗略预测影响，且错误倾向于最严重地扭曲有信息量的UQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM广泛应用需要不确定性量化来避免错误和幻觉，但NLG中缺乏人工干预时自动正确性判断不可靠，且现有评估常未验证自动判断。

Method: 对已发表工作进行元分析，并在问答任务上用4个LLM、人工与自动判断及7种流行UQ进行实验。

Result: ①判断性能只能粗略预测其错误对UQ评估可靠性的实际影响；②判断错误往往最严重地误现有信息量的UQ；③这些现象与置信度和判断错误类别之间的相关模式有关。

Conclusion: 自动判断在UQ评估中普遍且未充分验证，其错误会系统性扭曲评估，尤其损害有信息量的UQ，需谨慎对待并加强人工验证。

Abstract: The wide adoption of LLMs across broad NLG applications heightens the importance of providing users with the means to avert errors and hallucinations. Uncertainty quantification is poised to fill that gap; with low uncertainty (high confidence), as a proxy for correctness, allowing users to be selective (e.g., reject low-confidence, likely incorrect responses). Correlation between confidence and correctness then serves as a useful criterion for evaluation of uncertainty quantifiers (UQs). But in NLG, where diverse responses can be adequate to a prompt, obtaining reliable correctness judgements is not simple, especially without human intervention. Errors in automated judgement are hardly avoidable and known to diminish the reliability of evaluation protocols (Santilli et al., 2025; Ielanskyi et al., 2025). In a meta-analysis of published work, we show that automated judgement is the present norm. Besides, automated judgements are rarely validated against human ones, and the validation of the UQ evaluation they automate is even rarer. With experiments in question answering, using 4 LLMs, human and automated judgements and 7 popular UQs, we find that i) a judge's performance can only coarsely predict the observed impact of its errors on the reliability of UQ evaluation, and that ii) judgement errors tend to misrepresent informative UQs most. We link these observations to patterns of correlation between confidence and categories of judgement error.

</details>


### [158] [From Expert-Guided Proof Search to Automated Open-Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09769)
*Adrián Zámečník,Matěj Kripner,Martin Koutecký,Martin Balko,Jan Grebík,Pavel Hubáček,Robert Šámal,Václav Rozhoň*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: Bolzano是一种多智能体开源系统，通过并行证明智能体与验证智能体协作，维护人类可读的研究状态，用于数学证明搜索和验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在数学研究中日益重要，但进展常依赖于高效证明搜索、增量改进和仔细验证，需要一种能整合这些要素的系统。

Method: 采用多智能体架构，包含并行证明智能体和验证智能体，保持人类可读的研究状态；先在专家选定的问题上手动使用，后从四组论文中提取约3800个开放问题，进行无特定人工指导的自动运行。

Result: 初始手动使用在专家选定问题上获得8个由领域专家验证的证明；自动运行解决了约200个开放问题；在STOC 2026论文实验中回答了论文提出的四个问题，并得到作者确认。

Conclusion: Bolzano系统能有效辅助数学研究，在无特定人工指导下解决大量开放问题，并在理论计算机科学顶级会议论文中验证了其发现能力。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly contributing to mathematical research, where progress often depends on efficient proof search, incremental improvements and careful verification. We describe Bolzano, a multi-agent open-source system that uses parallel prover agents with a verifier agent and maintains a human-readable research state. Initial manual use on expert-selected problems yielded 8 results whose proofs were checked by domain experts. Motivated by these case studies, we ran Bolzano without problem-specific human guidance on about 3,800 open problems extracted from four sets of papers, solving about 200 open problems. One experiment used papers accepted to STOC 2026, a top conference in theoretical computer science. There, we answered four questions raised in the papers, as confirmed by their authors.

</details>


### [159] [Shared Geometry As A Rosetta Stone: Cross-Modal Alignment Without Paired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09411)
*Dominik Schnaus,Thomas Dagès,Daniel Cremers,Xi Wang,Phillip Isola*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文发现独立训练的多模态模型无需配对样本即可实现粗略跨模态对齐，并提出Wasserstein Procrustes方法，在极少配对样本下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态对齐通常依赖大量配对数据，但Platonic Representation Hypothesis暗示不同模态模型可能自发收敛到共享表示几何，因此探究是否无需配对样本即可对齐。

Method: 提出Wasserstein Procrustes方法，利用粗略几何初始化，在无配对样本条件下估计单一正交映射，对齐两个不相交的嵌入集合。

Result: 在多个数据集、模态和单模态模型上，无需配对即可一致地对齐独立训练表示，标准几何对齐指标能准确预测可行性；极少配对时显著优于现有方法，与更多配对方法竞争；对齐结果可实现无配对文本到图像生成。

Conclusion: 独立训练的模型通常共享足够的几何结构，仅需极少或无配对数据即可建立跨模态对应关系。

Abstract: Multimodal representations enable zero-shot classification and retrieval, but aligning independently trained models usually requires large amounts of paired data. Yet, the Platonic Representation Hypothesis suggests that models trained on different modalities may converge spontaneously toward a shared representation geometry. But then, do we even need paired examples for cross-modal alignment? Remarkably, we show that paired examples are unnecessary for coarse cross-modal alignment. Our simple Wasserstein Procrustes method with a coarse geometric initialization aligns two disjoint embedding sets by estimating a single orthogonal map without seeing any pairs. Across datasets, modalities, and unimodal models, we show that we can consistently align independently trained representations without pairs, and standard geometric alignment metrics accurately predict when this is possible. Nevertheless, we can naturally benefit from paired examples. In the very few-pair regime, our method substantially outperforms existing ones, while staying competitive with pair-based methods with more added examples. Finally, we demonstrate that the resulting alignments can enable text-to-image generation without paired examples. These results show that independently trained models often share enough geometry to establish cross-modal correspondence with little or no paired data.

</details>


### [160] [What a Reporting Convention Hides: A Matched-Budget Audit of Quantum Natural Gradient with an Exactly Computed Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09425)
*Lu Wei,Yufeng Wang,Haibin Ling*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文比较了Adam、SPSA和QNG三种变分量子优化器的评估成本，指出不同比较惯例（是否只统计达标运行或在单一目标下判定）会改变结论，并建议将未达标运行计入预算且报告多个目标下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有比较常只计时达到目标损失的运行，或在单一目标处读取结论；这两种惯例可能掩盖优化器昂贵步骤的收益，因此需要量化惯例对结论的影响。

Method: 在从超参数选择中留出的初始化上，对Adam、SPSA和QNG进行测量；计算QNG预条件所需的精确度量，将每步成本折算为电路评估次数，并给所有方法相同预算。

Result: 在跨电路宽度、成本族和含常见未达标设置的扫描中，SPSA达到宽松目标所需评估次数是Adam的两倍以上；剔除未达标运行会隐藏此差距。在全局成本族固定严格目标设置时，QNG通常先于Adam达到严格目标但晚于其达到宽松目标；当用参数数量二次的硬件计数替代线性模拟器价格时，QNG的严格目标领先消失。

Conclusion: 建议将每次未达标运行按预算计费，并报告多个目标下的判定结果，以避免比较惯例改变优化器优劣结论。

Abstract: Several published comparisons of variational quantum optimizers time only runs that reach a target loss, or read the verdict at a single target. Either convention could decide whether an optimizer's costlier steps pay off. We measure how much each convention changes verdicts among Adam, simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation (SPSA) and quantum natural gradient (QNG), on initializations held out from the selection of settings. We compute the exact metric that preconditions QNG, price every step in circuit evaluations and give every method the same budget. In a median pooled over circuit widths, cost families and a sweep of settings with common misses, SPSA needs more than twice Adam's evaluations to reach a loose target. Dropping the censored runs that miss the target hides this gap. On the global-cost family we hold fixed the settings selected for a strict target. QNG then usually reaches the loose target after Adam but the strict target first. QNG's strict-target lead disappears when the metric's simulator price, linear in the number of parameters, is replaced by an assumed hardware count quadratic in that number. We recommend charging every miss the budget and reporting verdicts across targets.

</details>


### [161] [Outperformance Inverse Optimization: Learning Objective Functions that Outperform Agent Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09890)
*Akira Kitaoka*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的逆优化方法——outperformance逆优化，旨在为混合整数线性规划（MILP）寻找一组权重，使每个状态下最优解在所有分量上均优于观测动作，并给出可仅用前向问题预言机评估的损失函数及梯度与DC优化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MILP逆优化方法以复现观测为最优解为目标，当观测次优时只能学习折中权重，无法保证生成严格优于观测的解。

Method: 定义outperformance逆优化问题：寻找权重使每个状态下的最优解在每一分量上超过观测动作；设计仅依赖前向问题预言机的损失函数，并采用梯度法和DC优化算法最小化该损失。

Result: 对于在所有观测上诱导唯一outperformance最优解的权重，证明其在新状态上失败的概率（泛化误差）以与观测数成反比的量上界，且该界在观测数上（至多对数因子）是紧的；在合成与真实数据实验中，所提方法在预测优于动作的解方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: outperformance逆优化为MILP提供了一种可扩展的框架，能够学习产生严格优于观测动作的权重，并具有理论保证的泛化性能。

Abstract: Inverse optimization estimates the weights of an objective function that explain observed decisions as optimal solutions, and is used in a variety of fields. For mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs), existing methods aim to reproduce the observations as optimal solutions, and thus learn compromise weights when the observations are suboptimal. We propose outperformance inverse optimization, which instead seeks weights that induce, at each state, an optimal solution outperforming the observed action in every component. We give a loss function that can be evaluated with forward-problem oracles alone and is thus applicable to MILPs, together with gradient-based and DC optimization algorithms for minimizing it. For weights inducing a unique outperforming optimal solution at all observations, we prove that the probability of failing to induce such a solution at a new state (the generalization error) is bounded by a quantity inversely proportional to the number of observations, and that this bound is tight in the number of observations up to logarithmic factors. In experiments on synthetic and real data, the proposed methods improve the prediction of solutions outperforming the actions over existing methods.

</details>


### [162] [NL2Hull: A Natural Language-Driven Constrained Ship Design Decision Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09896)
*Wenhua Huo,Fenglei Han,Wangyuan Zhao,Jialin Wu,Jiayi Han*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出自然语言到船体框架（NL2Hull），将船舶型线编辑形式化为带类型离散决策问题，连接语言决策与数值几何，并构建数据集与基准，提出约束船舶设计决策模型Chip。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 船舶型线设计需兼顾光滑几何表示、局部形状编辑与船体约束，现有方法缺乏可复现的语言驱动决策接口及对几何与连续控制组件的评估。

Method: NL2Hull框架包含约束自由变形引擎（CFFD），以NURBS表示水线，对控制点应用FFD，重建船体并检查几何约束；构建船舶设计决策数据集（SDD，134,558条清洗记录）及其子集基准SDDBench（5,000条记录，43,496个带类型问题）；提出约束船舶设计决策模型Chip处理自然语言请求。

Result: Chip达到95.90%问题准确率、99.32% FFD精确匹配，负对数似然0.0951，期望校准误差0.0032，Brier分数0.0551。

Conclusion: NL2Hull框架为基于语言的船舶型线决策提供了可复现评估接口，同时识别出仍需进一步开发的几何与连续控制组件；代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Ship-form design combines smooth geometric representation, local shape editing, and constraints on the resulting hull. We present the Natural-Language-to-Hull Framework (NL2Hull Framework), which formulates ship-form editing as a typed discrete decision problem and connects language decisions to numerical geometry. Its Constrained Free-Form Deformation Engine (CFFD Engine) represents hull waterlines with non-uniform rational B-splines (NURBS), applies free-form deformation (FFD) to their control points, reconstructs the hull, and checks geometric constraints. We construct the Ship Design Decision Dataset (SDD Dataset) with 134,558 cleaned records and evaluate compared models on its subset Ship Design Decision Benchmark (SDDBench), containing 5,000 records and 43,496 typed questions. We propose Chip, a constrained ship-design decision model for processing natural-language requests. Chip reaches 95.90\% question accuracy and 99.32\% FFD exact match, with a negative log-likelihood of 0.0951, an expected calibration error of 0.0032, and a Brier score of 0.0551. The NL2Hull Framework provides a reproducible interface for evaluating language-based ship-form decisions while identifying the geometry and continuous-control components that require further development. Our code and dataset is available at https://github.com/wenhuahuo/NL2Hull.

</details>


### [163] [AgentTime: Can Agents Estimate and Control Their Own Runtime?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09944)
*Michael Ofengenden,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出 AgentTime 基准，评估 AI 智能体按时长工作、预测运行时间与估计已用时间的能力，发现时长遵循与时间感知仍是独立短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体需具备时间意识以管理运行时并控制自身行为，但现有工作多关注时间感知，原生智能体框架中的时长遵循与控制尚属空白。

Method: 构建含 222 个任务、覆盖 18 个来源的基准，涉及编码、计算机使用、智能体工作与自动化研究；通过追加指定工作时长的单条指令进行时长遵循实验，并开展运行时预测与回溯估计实验。

Result: 时长遵循准确率差异显著，Fable 5.1 偏差约 2.9 倍而 GPT-6 Astra 仅 1.2 倍；158 条可分类 Astra 运行中有 14 条在看似完成后显式休眠；预测普遍高估自然运行时长；移除时间信息使 Sol 与 Astra 偏差翻倍以上、Fable 接近翻倍。

Conclusion: 完成任务的能力并不等同于掌控自身时间或全程按需工作时长，长时程可靠、安全、自主运行需同时评估任务完成与时间控制能力。

Abstract: An essential control of AI agents is their ability to manage runtime. This ability requires a sense of time-awareness, to predict and estimate wall-clock time and to control their own actions. Prior work has focused on time-awareness, but duration-following and control in native agent harnesses remain unexplored. We present AgentTime, a benchmark for testing whether agents can work for a requested duration, predict their runtime, and estimate elapsed time afterward. It comprises 222 tasks from 18 sources spanning coding, computer use, agentic work, and automated research. Duration-following experiments append a single instruction specifying how long to work, with requests ranging from about a minute to multiple days. Accuracy on these instructions varies substantially: Fable 5.1 in Claude Code deviates from requested runtimes by a typical factor of 2.9$\times$, compared with only 1.2$\times$ for GPT-6 Astra in Codex. However, matching the requested runtime does not, by itself, establish continued work on the task. Among 158 reviewed Astra runs with classifiable transcripts, 14 explicitly slept after appearing to finish. In forecasting experiments, predictions tend to overestimate natural runtimes. In retrospective experiments, removing temporal information more than doubles deviation for Sol and Astra and nearly doubles it for Fable. An agent's ability to complete a task does not guarantee that it can control its own time or work for the whole requested duration. For agents to run reliably, safely, and autonomously over long horizons, we require the evaluation of both.

</details>


### [164] [Online Resource Allocation with an Endogenous Markov State: Fewer LP Solves Earn More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09577)
*Zhaohua Chen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 研究具有内生马尔可夫状态的有限时域在线资源分配问题，分析频繁与不频繁重求解策略在不同最优性条件下的遗憾性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线资源分配中行动影响状态转移，需理解重求解频率与最优性条件（非退化、不可约性）对遗憾的渐近影响，并处理请求先验未知情形。

Method: 建立瞬态流体LP上界与平稳LP随机控制基准；假设平稳LP唯一最优，引入原始非退化与最优诱导核不可约性；分别分析已知先验下频繁/不频繁重求解，以及未知先验下三阶段U形不频繁重求解策略（O(log log T)次LP求解）。

Result: 已知先验：非退化且不可约时两类重求解均达O(1)遗憾；退化最优时，不可约下不频繁重求解最坏Θ(√T)，频繁重求解可达Ω(T)。未知先验：给定最优目标状态类与常成本进入策略时，非退化O(1)、退化O(√T)；无目标类信息则线性极小极大遗憾不可避免。数值实验显示逐轮重求解不稳定，阈值化缓解损失，不频繁方案在已知与估计先验下均占优。

Conclusion: 重求解频率并非越高越好，频繁优化可能渐近更差；非退化与不可约性是关键正则条件；不频繁重求解在未知先验下结合学习与库存校正可达到近优遗憾。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon online resource allocation with i.i.d. requests and an endogenous Markov state on a finite state space: each action affects the transition of the state that governs future rewards and resource consumption. In this problem, a transient fluid LP benchmark upper bounds the expected reward of every nonanticipating policy, while a stationary LP supplies randomized state-dependent controls. We assume that the stationary LP has a unique optimum and identify primal nondegeneracy and irreducibility of the optimal induced kernel as important regularity conditions in this framework. With a known request prior, we show that, under nondegeneracy and irreducibility, both frequent and infrequent re-solving attain $O(1)$ regret. However, under a degenerate optimum, irreducibility yields the sharp worst-case $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ rate for infrequent re-solving, while frequent re-solving can incur $Ω(T)$ regret. Thus, more frequent optimization can perform asymptotically worse. With an unknown request prior, we develop a three-phase U-shaped infrequent re-solving policy that coordinates learning and inventory correction with $O(\log\log T)$ LP solves. When the optimal induced kernel is irreducible and the algorithm is given the optimal target state class and a constant-cost entrance policy, it attains $O(1)$ regret under nondegeneracy and $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret under degeneracy. Without the target-class information, linear minimax regret is unavoidable. Numerical experiments further illustrate the instability of round-by-round re-solving relative to epoch-wise infrequent re-solving, show that thresholding greatly mitigates its loss, and find that infrequent schemes remain dominant under both known and estimated priors.

</details>


### [165] [The Identifiability and Observability of Deep Normalized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09620)
*Pranav Venkata Konda*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究深度、无掩码、单头注意力中哪些参数由其输入-输出函数决定，证明Henry--Marchetti--Kohn猜想在实解析情形（含softmax）成立，并对异常纤维和局部可辨识性进行分类与量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解注意力机制的可辨识性：输入-输出函数在多大程度上唯一确定内部参数（有效分数与组合值映射），并解决Henry--Marchetti--Kohn提出的猜想。

Method: 针对正的非恒定实解析normalizer，证明函数在generic情形下确定有效分数与组合值映射，至多相差偶normalizer诱导的符号；在显式normalizer条件下对异常纤维分类，给出局部可辨识的sharp Taylor阶；在query/key同时塌缩附近，于分离有限输入库上计算完整native Jacobian衰减谱。

Result: 证明该猜想在实解析情形（包括softmax）成立；识别塌缩导致后续分数不可观测的条件；得到对常见首个非恒定normalizer次数k，第i层的contact order为2k3^{i-1}-1，并给出精确重数与核维数；高精度与自动微分计算展示了由此导致的数值灵敏度损失。

Conclusion: 注意力参数的可辨识性由normalizer性质与塌缩现象共同决定，实解析情形下猜想成立，同时量化了层级衰减与数值不稳定性。

Abstract: We study which parameters of deep, unmasked, single-head attention are determined by its input--output function. For known positive nonconstant real-analytic normalizers, the function generically determines the effective scores and combined value map up to the signs induced by even normalizers. This proves the real-analytic case of a conjecture of Henry--Marchetti--Kohn, including softmax. We then classify exceptional fibers under explicit normalizer conditions, identifying when collapse makes later scores unobservable, and establish sharp Taylor orders for local identification. Near simultaneous query/key collapse, we compute the complete native Jacobian decay spectrum on separating finite input banks. For common first nonconstant normalizer degree $k$, layer $i$ has contact order $2k3^{i-1}-1$, with exact multiplicities and kernel dimension. High-precision and automatic differentiation calculations illustrate the resulting loss of numerical sensitivity.

</details>


### [166] [Gauss-Newton Accuracy and Indefinite Hessians: Uniform Coexistence in Low-Cost Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09675)
*Kihun Rhee,Hanjoon Byun*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文研究了岭正则化非线性最小二乘中高斯-牛顿曲率的精确性，证明了在特定局部正则性与水平集曲率持续性条件下，曲率存在两种共存状态：全局极小点处相对Hessian误差低于(1+√2)/8，而低代价集内却存在不定Hessian点，其相对误差至少为15/8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯-牛顿方法广泛用于非线性最小二乘，但其曲率近似在岭正则化下的精确性缺乏统一理论，尤其是全局极小点与低代价区域中曲率误差的共存现象尚不明确。

Method: 在局部正则性与沿精确拟合截面的水平集曲率幅度持续性假设下，通过理论分析证明曲率两种状态的共存性；利用基于当前预测水平集的逐点证书控制Hessian修正的法向、混合与切向部分，并推导在预测映射与岭参数变化下的尖锐相对误差界。

Result: 证明了全局极小点存在且相对Hessian误差低于(1+√2)/8；同时低代价集内存在不定Hessian点，相对误差至少15/8。单个正岭帽适用于固定邻域内所有独立中心与标签扰动，且邻域不随岭权趋于零而收缩。还给出了低代价集上Jacobian行满秩及在秩亏参考点附近精确插值的结构结果。

Conclusion: 岭正则化非线性最小二乘中高斯-牛顿曲率并非一致精确，全局极小点附近误差小，但低代价区域可能存在严重曲率误差；所提逐点证书与相对误差界为分析曲率质量提供了工具，且结构结果揭示了Jacobian秩与插值性质。

Abstract: We study the accuracy of Gauss-Newton curvature in ridge-regularized nonlinear least squares. Under local regularity and persistence of level-set curvature magnitude along an exact-fit section, we prove uniform coexistence of two curvature regimes. Global minimizers exist, and every global minimizer has relative Hessian error below $(1+\sqrt2)/8$, while the same low-cost set contains a point with an indefinite Hessian and relative error at least $15/8$. One positive ridge cap works for all independent center and label perturbations in fixed neighborhoods and every positive ridge weight up to the cap. These neighborhoods do not shrink as the ridge weight tends to zero. A pointwise certificate based on the current prediction level set controls the normal, mixed, and tangent parts of the Hessian correction. We prove a sharp relative-error bound over the stated pointwise class when the prediction map and ridge vary. Analytic examples describe the roles of output alignment, curvature orientation, and persistence. A separate structural result gives full Jacobian row rank throughout low-cost sets and exact interpolation near a rank-deficient reference.

</details>


### [167] [Validity Without Ground Truth: What Stated-Preference Economics Offers the Evaluation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10506)
*Daniel Robert Kling Alexander,Catherine Louise Kling*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出将陈述性偏好经济学的效度框架（内容效度、结构效度、效标效度、信度、激励相容性与后果性）作为评估大语言模型的通用方法，并以一项水质估值调查对六个模型进行实证检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多向大语言模型提出的问题（如政策价值、用户选择、价值权衡）并无正确答案可供评分，传统评价方式失效；而陈述性偏好经济学数十年来正是在不知道真实值的情况下评判受访者回答，其效度与相关概念框架可迁移用于大模型评估。

Method: 将陈述性偏好经济学的效度框架逐项映射到LLM评估，明确各概念在LLM语境下的含义；随后使用已发表的水质陈述性偏好经济估值调查（Vossler等，2023）对六个模型施测，并将效度检验具体化为经济学理论预测：需求曲线应向下倾斜，支付意愿应对标的范围（scope）和收入作出响应。

Result: 这些检验清晰区分了各模型：两个较旧模型在家庭收入75,000美元水平上未通过最基本的检验；两个最新模型通过了所有可评分理论效度检验，但在收敛效度上出现分歧。

Conclusion: 通过效度检验只能说明模型的回答具有内在一致性，并不意味着其回答是正确的；效度框架可作为评估无标准答案任务下大语言模型的通用方法。

Abstract: Many of the questions now put to large language models have no correct answer to score against: what a policy is worth, which option a user should choose, how to weigh competing values. Stated-preference economics has faced this problem for decades. It judges survey responses without knowing the true value, through a framework of validity and related concepts: content, construct, and criterion validity, reliability, incentive compatibility, and consequentiality. We argue that this framework is a general method for evaluating language models, and we set out what each concept means for LLM evaluation. We demonstrate the approach using a published water-quality stated preference economic valuation survey (Vossler et al. 2023) administered to six models. In this economic application, the validity tests take the form of predictions from economic theory: demand should slope down, and willingness to pay should respond to the scope of the good and to income. The tests separate the models sharply. Two older models fail the most basic test at a household income level of \$75,000, and the two newest pass every test of theoretical validity we can score, but diverge on convergent validity. Passing validity tests shows that a model's answers are coherent, not that they are correct.

</details>


### [168] [SciExam for ENSO: Can AI Agents Build Climate Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10513)
*Yinling Zhang,Langchen Liu,Dongbin Xiu,Xueyan Zou,Xu Kuang,Mengdi Wang,Shilong Liu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出ENSO科学考试基准，让AI智能体在六小时内仅凭自建诊断从真实观测中构建低阶随机ENSO模型，并由隐藏评分器评估其统计复现、隐变量恢复与预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学智能体评估多依赖已知答案、评分细则或语言模型评审，无法判断新科学模型是否有效；需要一个在无已知答案情形下评估开放科学研究的基准。

Method: 构建SciExam for ENSO基准：智能体在六小时预算内处理观测数据、编写并冻结自身诊断，仅以诊断为反馈开发模型；隐藏评分器检验模型对ENSO统计量、未观测变量与留出年份预测的表现，并对已发表模型同样评分。

Result: 十二个智能体系统中六个模型得分超过已发表模型，主要优势在重建与预测；较强模型的简化形式分别与ENSO冷暖不对称的两种竞争解释之一相容；对顶尖系统的受控实验表明其得分并非来自记忆旧观测记录，且输入信息会影响建模方式。

Conclusion: SciExam for ENSO表明可在无已知答案时评估智能体研究，且智能体已能构建有竞争力的模型，其结构还触及科学家仍在争论的问题。

Abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly asked to carry out open-ended scientific research, yet their results are usually graded against a known answer, a rubric, or a language-model reviewer, none of which can tell whether a new scientific model is valid. The AI Science Exam for El Nino-Southern Oscillation (SciExam for ENSO) is a benchmark in which agents build low-order stochastic models of ENSO, the dominant mode of interannual climate variability, from real observations. Within a six-hour budget, agents process the observations, write their own diagnostics, which are then frozen, and develop a model using only these diagnostics as feedback. Hidden graders then test whether the model reproduces ENSO's statistics, recovers unobserved variables, and forecasts held-out years, and score a published model in the same way. Across twelve agent systems, six produce models that score higher than the published model, mainly through better reconstruction and forecasting. The simplified forms of the stronger models are each compatible with one of the two competing explanations of ENSO's warm-cold asymmetry, an open debate that the task never mentions. Controlled runs of the top system under varied information suggest that its scores do not come from recalling the dated observational record and that the information it receives shapes how it builds its model. SciExam for ENSO can thus evaluate agent research where no answer is known, and the results suggest that agents can already build competitive models whose structures bear on questions that scientists still debate.

</details>


### [169] [AdaPS-LiNGAM: Adaptive Predecessor Selection for Linear Non-Gaussian Acyclic Models under Small-Sample Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09782)
*Shun Yanashima,Kentaro Kanamori,Hirofumi Suzuki*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了DirectLiNGAM在高维小样本下的结构性缺陷，并提出AdaPS-LiNGAM方法实现更稳健的因果发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现中样本量小于变量数时，现有基于线性非高斯无环模型（LiNGAM）的方法如DirectLiNGAM因反复残差化导致退化，无法确定完整因果顺序。

Method: 理论分析DirectLiNGAM在样本量不足时残差化的退化机制，提出活跃边界概念，并设计AdaPS-LiNGAM：利用自适应选择的早期变量稀疏子集直接从原始观测重构残差，同时将子集选择原则用于最终剪枝。

Result: 合成数据实验表明，AdaPS-LiNGAM在样本受限场景下能准确恢复因果结构，且随样本量减少性能退化更平缓。

Conclusion: AdaPS-LiNGAM通过自适应前驱选择克服了DirectLiNGAM在小样本高维下的退化问题，提升了因果结构估计的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Causal discovery becomes particularly challenging when the available sample size is small relative to the number of variables. This challenge also arises in the linear non-Gaussian acyclic model (LiNGAM), an identifiable framework for causal discovery from observational data. DirectLiNGAM estimates a causal order, which arranges variables so that causes precede their effects, by sequentially identifying an exogenous variable and removing its linear effect from the remaining variables. We establish a structural limitation of this procedure: when the number of variables exceeds the sample size, repeated residualization necessarily becomes degenerate before the full causal order can be determined. Our analysis further reveals that each residual can be reconstructed using only a graph-determined subset of variables already placed earlier in the causal order, termed the active boundary. This result motivates AdaPS-LiNGAM (Adaptive Predecessor Selection LiNGAM), which reconstructs each residual directly from the original observations using an adaptively chosen sparse subset of those earlier variables. The same subset-selection principle is also applied to the final pruning step for edge estimation. Experiments on synthetic data demonstrate that AdaPS-LiNGAM provides accurate causal-structure recovery in sample-limited settings and degrades more gradually as the sample size decreases.

</details>


### [170] [Homogenization in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09824)
*Prakhar Ganesh,Kyra Wilson,Luca Zappella,Barry-John Theobald,Nicholas Apostoloff,Lucas Monteiro Paes,Nivedha Sivakumar*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 多智能体系统（MAS）中的智能体交互会导致行为同质化，表现为从众、极端化和惯性增加，从而在设计、招聘和同行评审任务中带来具体风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MAS在复杂任务中取得成功，但智能体间的交互可能导致同质化，减少智能体多样性并放大共同失败，因此需要将同质化作为一种失败模式进行系统评估。

Method: 操作化同质化为三个指标：对多数的从众、向极端的极化以及后续交互中增长的惯性；在代码生成、招聘和科学同行评审任务中评估MAS的同质化。

Result: 同质化在代码生成中隐藏并放大相关错误，形成系统性漏洞；在招聘中使偏见智能体的影响在移除后长期持续；在同行评审中导致不同研究领域的评估标准不均。简单增加多样性的方法（采样随机性和混合模型MAS）未能降低同质化风险。

Conclusion: 同质化是MAS的一种失败模式，MAS评估必须超越聚合性能，深入分析交互动态；需要有效利用智能体多样性的策略。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) leverage interactions between agents to perform complex tasks. Despite their success, we show that these interactions can also lead to homogenization, i.e., agents converging to similar behaviors. Homogenization in MAS can reduce agent diversity and reinforce shared failures. In this paper, we operationalize homogenization using three metrics: conformity to the majority, polarization towards extremes, and growing inertia against changes over subsequent interactions. We evaluate homogenization in MAS for code generation, hiring, and scientific peer review. Across these tasks, we show that homogenization translates to concrete downstream risks: in code generation, it hides and amplifies correlated errors which can create systemic vulnerabilities; in hiring, it allows the influence of biased agents to persist long after their removal; and in peer review, it creates uneven evaluation standards across research areas. Our results establish homogenization as a failure mode of MAS, demonstrating that MAS evaluations must move beyond aggregate performance to carefully analyze interaction dynamics. Finally, we show that simple approaches to increase diversity---leveraging sampling stochasticity and mixed-models MAS---fail to reduce homogenization risks, highlighting the need for strategies to effectively leverage agent diversity.

</details>


### [171] [Eigenvalues of the Hessian in Deep Learning: The Origin of Symmetry and Its Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09919)
*Yossi Arjevani*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文提出，深度模型训练后的Hessian谱呈现近零大簇与少数离群值的层次结构，源于对某个隐藏高对称参考构型的偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有观察表明训练后Hessian谱具有稳定的簇与离群值现象，但缺乏统一解释；作者试图从对称性破缺角度给出自然且一般的机制。

Method: 将原模型视为对附近高对称参考构型的扰动，通过修改架构、数据分布或参数度量来暴露该参考构型，使其Hessian具有超出权重对称性的丰富不变性，并分析三层ReLU网络及卷积、图、Transformer模型和NTK。

Result: 高对称参考构型的Hessian被精确刻画，呈现高维核与大重数特征值；返回原构型后对称性破缺，产生观测到的簇与离群值层次；该机制也出现在逐层Hessian和Gauss-Newton矩阵中。

Conclusion: 训练后Hessian的谱层次可由隐藏高对称参考构型的对称性破缺统一解释，为理解深度网络损失景观的谱结构提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Hessian spectra at trained models in deep learning exhibit a persistent pattern: eigenvalues organize into distinct clusters, including a large bulk near zero and a few isolated outliers. This paper shows that a natural account of these spectral phenomena emerges when the original setting is understood as a departure from a nearby, otherwise hidden, highly symmetric reference.
  Modifications, including changes to the architecture, data distribution, or parameter metric, expose a nearby reference configuration whose Hessian exhibits rich invariances-ones not accounted for by weight symmetries. There, symmetry enables a precise description of the spectra, forcing high-dimensional kernels and eigenvalues of large multiplicity. Returning to the original configuration breaks the Hessian symmetry and thereby produces the observed hierarchy of clusters and outliers.
  The framework is developed in some generality, with a detailed analysis of three-layer ReLU networks and applications to convolutional, graph, and transformer models, as well as to the NTK. The same mechanism is further shown to yield analogous spectral structures in layerwise Hessians and the Gauss-Newton matrix.

</details>


### [172] [Expected Sample Complexity in Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09929)
*Nadav Sukenik,Nadav Merlis*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文研究随机多臂老虎机问题的样本复杂度，提出期望样本复杂度指标及ACE框架，分析算法在已知和未知次优水平ε下的性能，并建立紧致下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 样本复杂度是序列决策中的常用指标，但现有度量多关注高概率保证，缺乏对期望样本复杂度的系统分析，且确定性算法在期望意义下可能表现不佳。

Method: 提出ACE（近似正确期望）框架，将ACE保证转化为显式期望遗憾界，并分别针对ε已知和未知情况设计探索-然后-ε-贪心算法与Thompson采样，分析其期望样本复杂度，同时推导近乎匹配的下界。

Result: ACE保证隐含几乎必然收敛到最优期望奖励；确定性算法无法获得良好ACE界；探索-然后-ε-贪心算法与Thompson采样在相应设置下达到紧致下界，且两种情形存在性能分离。

Conclusion: ACE框架为期望样本复杂度提供了有效分析工具，所提算法在ε依赖上紧致，并揭示了ε已知与未知两种机制间的本质性能差异。

Abstract: Sample complexity is a widely used metric in sequential decision-making problems, defined as the number of suboptimal decisions during the interaction between the agent and an environment. We study the sample complexity of stochastic multi-armed bandit problems and introduce the expected sample complexity performance measure, analyzing it in a novel framework called approximately correct in expectation (ACE). We show that ACE guarantees imply almost sure convergence to the optimal expected reward, in contrast to high-probability guarantees found in other frameworks, and also show how to convert ACE guarantees into explicit expected regret bounds. We further show that, in contrast to existing measures, deterministic algorithms cannot obtain favorable ACE bounds, and analyze stochastic algorithms in two settings: when the allowed suboptimality level $ε$ is known to the algorithm and when it is unknown. In the former, we devise an explore-then-$ε$-greedy algorithm, and in the latter, we analyze the expected sample complexity of Thompson sampling. Finally, we establish nearly matching lower bounds for both settings, showing that the algorithms are tight in $ε$ and proving a performance separation between the two regimes.

</details>


### [173] [Oscillatory Neural Dynamics over Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10018)
*Jan-Willem Van Looy,Alessandro Trenta,Alessio Gravina,Alessio Borgi,Ferdinando Zanchetta,Pietro Liò,Davide Bacciu,Rita Fioresi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出ONDA框架，基于算子值信息波实现图神经网络的长程有效传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络中增加传播深度不能保证远距离节点有效相互影响；sheaf神经网络的矩阵值传输虽增强表达力，但不足以自动实现长程通信。

Method: ONDA让stalk值表示在学习的sheaf传输算子支配下进行二阶动力学演化，将波式传播与局部几何表达相结合，并通过stalk级敏感性分析刻画长程影响。

Result: 分析表明跨stalk影响不会消失；在长程传播、严重图瓶颈、图迁移和异配基准上，ONDA持续优于标量波传播、扩散sheaf基线和当前最优模型。

Conclusion: 将波动力学与矩阵值传输耦合能有效改善长程图学习，验证了ONDA框架的优势。

Abstract: Effective long-range propagation remains a central challenge in graph neural networks, as increasing a model's propagation depth does not guarantee that distant nodes effectively influence each other. Sheaf neural networks enrich graph propagation through matrix-valued transport between stalks; still, this expressivity alone does not automatically imply effective long-range communication. We introduce ONDA, a long-range graph learning framework based on operator-valued information waves. Stalk-valued representations evolve through second-order dynamics governed by learned sheaf transport operators, combining wave-like propagation with expressive local geometry. We characterize long-range influence through a stalk-wise sensitivity analysis and show that the cross-influence never vanishes. Across long-range propagation, severe graph bottlenecks, graph transfer, and heterophilic benchmarks, ONDA consistently improves over scalar wave propagation, diffusive sheaf baselines, and state-of-the-art models, demonstrating the benefit of coupling wave dynamics with matrix-valued transport.

</details>


### [174] [WxFM-XL: Adapting Univariate Foundation Models to Multi-Station Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10057)
*Xiao Wang,Changjian Chen,Zhuo Tang,Rongwen Li,Hongwu Liu,Kenli Li*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文提出 WxFM-XL，将单变量时间序列基础模型适配于多站气象预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多变量扩展主要建模变量间相关性，忽视了多站气象预报中站点空间信息与不同站点相对基础模型的误差先验。

Method: 构建跨站点误差相关先验图以刻画站点级误差先验，并提出动态融合机制，自适应融合空间相关图与误差相关先验图。

Result: 在多个数据集上实验表明，该模型优于当前最先进基线。

Conclusion: 显式引入站点空间信息与误差先验并进行动态融合，可有效提升单变量基础模型在多站气象预报中的性能。

Abstract: With the rise of univariate time series foundation models (e.g., Sundial, Timer), initial efforts have been made to extend them to multivariate settings. However, these models mainly focus on modeling correlations among variables. When they are applied to multi-station weather forecasting, two important factors are often overlooked: (1) the spatial information of stations, and (2) different error priors of different stations relative to the foundation model. In this paper, we propose WxFM-XL, a model for adapting univariate time series foundation models to multi-station weather forecasting. WxFM-XL introduces a cross-station error correlation prior graph to capture stationwise error priors with respect to the foundation model. Building on this, we further propose a dynamic fusion mechanism that adaptively integrates a spatial correlation graph with the error correlation prior graph. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that our model outperforms state of the art baselines.

</details>


### [175] [CAFE+FNO: Fourier Kernel Generation via Multiplicative Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10105)
*Hyungjoon Juen,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出CAFE+FNO，将内容感知频率编码CAFE+融入FNO的傅里叶核生成过程，以增强高频变化的学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO通过傅里叶空间核参数化学习PDE解算子，但频率截断限制高频变化学习；AM-FNO和SirenFNO用共享网络从谱坐标生成所有网格模态的核，其坐标编码与生成器设计至关重要，而近期INR工作表明显式特征组合构建频率交互优于依赖后续MLP隐式形成。

Method: CAFE+通过并行仿射分支与Hadamard积组合Fourier–Chebyshev特征，在两类特征族内及跨族形成交互；核MLP将每个归一化谱坐标的表示映射为复数通道混合矩阵；每层生成器在所有存储模态间共享，使固定架构下可训练参数量与模态数无关。

Result: 在五个PDE基准上将CAFE+FNO与现有FNO变体进行比较，并针对基配置、乘法组合与带宽可学习性开展消融实验。

Conclusion: CAFE+FNO通过内容感知频率编码显式构建频率交互，提升了傅里叶核生成质量，且参数量不随模态数增长；代码与实验配置已开源。

Abstract: The Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) learns solution operators of partial differential equations (PDEs) through Fourier-space kernel parameterization, but frequency truncation can limit the learning of high-frequency variations. AM-FNO and SirenFNO generate kernels for all grid modes from spectral coordinates using shared networks, making coordinate encoding and generator design important. Recent work on implicit neural representations (INRs) has proposed constructing frequency interactions through explicit feature composition rather than relying on subsequent MLPs to form them implicitly. Building on this approach, we propose CAFE+FNO, which incorporates Content-Aware Frequency Encoding+ (CAFE+) into Fourier kernel generation. CAFE+ combines Fourier--Chebyshev features through parallel affine branches and a Hadamard product, forming interactions within and across the two feature families. A kernel MLP maps the resulting representation of each normalized spectral coordinate to a complex channel-mixing matrix. Each layer shares its generator across all stored modes, making the number of trainable parameters independent of the number of modes for a fixed architecture. We compare CAFE+FNO with existing FNO variants on five PDE benchmarks and conduct ablation studies on basis configuration, multiplicative composition, and bandwidth learnability. Code and experimental configurations are available at https://github.com/fabsk101/CAFEPlusFNO.git.

</details>


### [176] [A Unified Information-Theoretic Approach to Constrained Multi-Fidelity Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10174)
*Rikuto Matsumoto,Masanori Ishikura,Masayuki Karasuyama*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于信息增益的统一贝叶斯优化方法，同时选择评估位置和保真度，以识别最高保真度的可行帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标、约束和保真度级别的贝叶斯优化中，联合选择评估位置和保真度以找到最高保真度的可行帕累托前沿是一个挑战。

Method: 从统一的信息论视角，用关于该前沿的信息增益度量查询效用，并推导出变分下界，利用欠截断和过截断近似帕累托一致区域的混合；多保真代理模型将信息传播到任意保真度，形成成本感知的采集函数，无需单独启发式处理保真度选择或约束。

Result: 在合成、基准和真实问题上，跨不同目标、约束和保真度设置均展示了有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法在联合多目标、约束和多保真度设置下有效识别最高保真度的可行帕累托前沿。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization often involves multiple objectives, constraints, and fidelity levels. We address the challenge of jointly selecting where and at which fidelity to evaluate to identify the highest-fidelity feasible Pareto frontier in this combined setting. From a unified information-theoretic perspective, we measure query utility by the information gain about this frontier, provided by an observation. Since this mutual information is intractable, we derive a variational lower bound using a mixture of under- and over-truncated approximations to the Pareto-consistent region. Multi-fidelity surrogate models propagate the information to arbitrary fidelities, yielding a cost-aware acquisition function without separate heuristics for fidelity selection or constraint handling. Experiments on synthetic, benchmark, and real-world problems demonstrate effectiveness across diverse objective, constraint, and fidelity settings.

</details>


### [177] [Finite-Sample Approximation of Hessian-Guided Perturbed Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10218)
*Ryotaro Kawata,Atsushi Nitanda,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 研究带Hessian引导高斯扰动的Wasserstein梯度流（PWGF）用有限粒子近似时，在时间范围增长下保持精度的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PWGF通过高斯扰动逃离非凸问题的鞍点，但有限粒子近似在长时间下是否仍准确尚不明确，需要刻画允许暂时不稳定但仍能精确跟踪的情形。

Method: 沿群体驱动参考路径保留累积曲率；在正则性假设与给定公共扰动计划下，构造保持参考粒子条件独立性的population-first耦合来处理状态依赖高斯跳跃，把跳跃误差化为协方差比较，并在variance-plus-cosine模型中验证条件。

Result: 在高概率事件上证明粒子与目标值的跟踪界，参考路径需满足关于累积曲率的显式条件；负曲率可放大误差、后续正曲率可抑制其影响，variance-plus-cosine模型中曲率恢复给出随时间增长范围的跟踪保证。

Conclusion: 暂时不稳定可与长时间精确跟踪相容；在正则化矩阵分解模型中建立局部吸引、横向下降与正二阶变分，揭示正-负-正曲率模式。

Abstract: Wasserstein gradient flow extends gradient descent to probability measures. Its Hessian-guided perturbed variant (PWGF) adds Gaussian perturbations to escape saddle points in nonconvex problems. We investigate when its approximation by finitely many interacting particles remains accurate over growing time horizons. Our analysis retains the curvature accumulated along the population-driven reference path: negative curvature can amplify approximation errors, while subsequent positive curvature can damp their influence. This captures favorable scenarios in which temporary instability is compatible with accurate tracking over growing horizons. Under regularity assumptions and a prescribed common perturbation schedule, we prove particle and objective-value tracking bounds on a high-probability event for reference paths satisfying explicit conditions on accumulated curvature. To handle state-dependent Gaussian jumps, we construct a population-first coupling that preserves the reference particles' conditional independence and reduces jump errors to covariance comparison. We verify the conditions in a variance-plus-cosine model, where curvature recovery yields a growing-horizon tracking guarantee. We also establish local attraction, transverse descent, and positive second variation in two regions of a regularized matrix-factorization model, motivating a positive-negative-positive curvature pattern.

</details>


### [178] [Stationary Bias and Extrapolation in Nonlinear Two-Timescale Stochastic Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10246)
*A. Ch. Madhusudanarao,Rahul Singh*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究由外生有限状态马尔可夫链驱动的非线性双时间尺度随机逼近中恒定步长导致的非零稳态均值误差，给出了一阶偏差展开并分析其混合步长依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒定步长随机逼近即使经过时间平均仍存在非零稳态均值误差，现有分析对非线性双时间尺度递归的偏差刻画不足，尤其在快慢步长差异极大时缺乏统一误差界。

Method: 在给定光滑性假设和平稳分布条件下，通过快流形坐标保持协方差方程在慢步长远小于快步长时的正则性，推导一阶偏差展开；对纯幂律步长路径分析偏差指数，并用可解非线性马尔可夫例子验证系数。

Result: 偏差展开包含与各步长线性相关的项以及混合项 ε²/η；沿幂律步长路径偏差指数可非整数，Richardson–Romberg外推需匹配路径的权重；在时间差分学习中验证了局部化，并在等更新预算下比较有限次外推，附加耦合假设下界定了两时间尺度尾平均的初始化误差，加性独立噪声下三阶矩抵消给出更紧余项。

Conclusion: 非线性双时间尺度随机逼近的稳态偏差由步长线性项与混合项共同决定，混合项导致偏差指数非整数，因此外推权重必须与步长路径匹配；快流形坐标方法为极小慢步长极限提供了统一误差界。

Abstract: Constant-step stochastic approximation generally has a nonzero stationary mean error that persists under time averaging. This paper studies that error for nonlinear two-timescale recursions driven by an exogenous finite-state Markov chain. Under stated smoothness assumptions and conditions on the stationary distribution, we derive a first-order bias expansion whose error bound remains uniform as the slow step size becomes much smaller than the fast step size. Fast-manifold coordinates keep the associated covariance equation regular in this limit. For fast step $η$ and slow step $\varepsilon$, the expansion reveals a mixed contribution $\varepsilon^2/η$ alongside terms linear in each step size. This dependence matters for bias reduction: along power-law step-size paths, the bias exponents need not be integers, so Richardson--Romberg extrapolation requires weights matched to the path. An exactly solvable nonlinear Markov example verifies the coefficients. We verify localization for temporal-difference learning and compare finite-run extrapolation at equal update budgets. For finite runs, we bound the initialization error of tail averages on both timescales under an additional coupling assumption. In the special case of additive independent noise, signed third-moment cancellation yields a sharper remainder.

</details>


### [179] [Logarithmic Regret via Passive Change Detection in Piecewise-Stationary Self-Tuning Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10250)
*A. Ch. Madhusudanarao,Rahul Singh*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 研究未知自回归含外生输入系统中系数在未知时刻变化时的最小方差控制，提出PIECE-CD算法并证明对数级遗憾界与检测延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切换赌博机中未选臂可无观测变化，而可控对象的变化在利用阶段可被观测：正确控制器下输出仅含扰动，而可检测变化会使旧控制器下输出能量升高，借此实现变化检测与控制。

Method: PIECE-CD在初始阶段和报警后探索，采用门控递归最小二乘进行控制；通过比较窗口输出功率与高于噪声底的阈值做能量检测，并针对不稳定控制器失配额外监控参考控制器的输入建议；控制全时域误报，输入按给定界裁剪。

Result: 在独立有界扰动、固定检测间隔、稳定与可行性条件及变化间足够时间下，以至少1-δ概率获得O((C+1)log((T+1)/δ))遗憾，T为时域、C为变化次数；证明对数检测延迟；在裁剪于有限预热后失效的显式条件下对数遗憾仍成立；扩展检测器在可行性条件下以固定窗口可检测超额能量覆盖失稳变化。

Conclusion: 所提方法在满足条件下实现对数遗憾与对数检测延迟，能量检测可覆盖失稳变化，裁剪条件可保证长期性能。

Abstract: We study minimum-variance control of an unknown autoregressive system with exogenous inputs and coefficients that change at unknown times. Under bounded independent disturbances, fixed detection gaps, stability and feasibility conditions, and sufficient time between changes, we prove \(O((C+1)\log((T+1)/δ))\) regret with probability at least \(1-δ\), where \(T\) is the horizon and \(C\) the number of changes. Unlike switching bandits, where unselected arms can change unobserved, admissible plant changes provide information during exploitation: the correct feasible controller leaves only the disturbance in the output, whereas a detectable change raises output energy under the old controller. PIECE-CD explores initially and after alarms, then uses gated recursive least squares for control. Its energy test compares windowed output power with a threshold above the noise floor; the extension to unstable controller mismatches also monitors the reference controller's input proposal. We control false alarms across the horizon and prove logarithmic detection delay. Inputs are clipped to prescribed bounds. Logarithmic regret also holds under an explicit condition ensuring that clipping becomes inactive after a finite burn-in. Under the stated feasibility conditions, the extended detector covers destabilizing changes with detectable excess energy over a fixed window.

</details>


### [180] [Revisiting Explainable AI through Model-Independent Concept Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10301)
*Thomas Schnake,Doreen Schöppenthau,Alexander Meyer,Jacques Corbeil,Klaus-Robert Müller,Grégoire Montavon*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出DictXAI，一种在输入域通过字典定义概念的可解释AI方法，实现稀疏编码并归因预测，跨模型架构通用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法假设输入特征可解释，或依赖难以表征且高度架构特定的内部抽象，限制了跨模型的一致性使用。

Method: DictXAI在输入域通过字典（大规模可能过完备的预定义元素，每个元素有可解释含义）定义概念：先计算输入的稀疏码，再将模型预测归因于相关字典元素。

Result: DictXAI解释可直接将AI故障（如Clever Hans效应）归因于数据中可识别的伪影模式，促进生物医学信号的人机对齐，并适用于多种字典（学习图像基、心电图解析波形、实验获取字典元素）。

Conclusion: DictXAI比经典XAI或现有概念方法提供更可解释、可操作且架构无关的洞见。

Abstract: Modern applications of AI rely on increasingly complex models. Explainable AI (XAI) has emerged as a set of techniques aimed at improving model transparency. However, existing XAI methods typically assume input features to be inherently interpretable, or they rely on intermediate internal abstractions that are difficult to characterize and highly architecture-specific, hindering consistent use across models. To address these limitations, we propose DictXAI, a method that defines concepts directly in the input domain via a dictionary---a large, potentially overcomplete set of predefined elements, each carrying an interpretable meaning. Technically, DictXAI first computes a sparse code of the input and then attributes the model's prediction to the associated dictionary elements. We demonstrate the actionable nature of DictXAI explanations, showing that they can attribute AI malfunctions (e.g., Clever Hans effects) directly to identifiable artifact patterns in the data, while fostering human-AI alignment on intricate biomedical signals. We further demonstrate our method's ability to operate across a wide variety of dictionaries, including learned image bases, analytically defined waveforms for electrocardiography, and experimentally acquired dictionary elements. Overall, our results show that DictXAI provides more interpretable, actionable, and architecture-agnostic insights than classical XAI or existing concept-based approaches.

</details>


### [181] [Boosting and the Expressive Power of Simple Weak Learners via the $γ$-VC Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10383)
*Arthur da Cunha,Kasper Green Larsen,Liang-Yu Zou*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文通过γ-VC维度研究Boosting中基类结构对分类器表达力的影响，并给出了弱到强学习的样本复杂度刻画及具体概念类的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boosting能将仅比随机猜测略好的弱假设转化为高精度预测器，但最终分类器的表达能力强烈依赖于基类的结构，因此需要量化这种依赖关系。

Method: 利用Alon等人（STOC 2021）引入的γ-VC维度作为分析工具，研究弱到强学习的样本复杂度，并细化经典VC维度与γ-VC维度的一般关系。

Result: 证明γ-VC维度以γ的常数因子缩放刻画了弱到强学习的样本复杂度；改进了VC维度与γ-VC维度的一般关系；对决策树桩和ℝ^d中轴平行矩形这两个基本概念类，给出了γ-VC维度的改进上界和下界。

Conclusion: γ-VC维度是理解Boosting中基类表达力与样本复杂度的关键参数，且针对基本概念类的精细界为相关学习任务提供了更精确的理论保证。

Abstract: Boosting converts weak hypotheses with a small edge over random guessing into highly accurate predictors, but the expressive power of the resulting classifier can depend strongly on the structure of the base class. We study this phenomenon through the $γ$-VC dimension introduced by Alon et al. (STOC 2021). Our first result shows that this parameter characterizes the sample complexity for weak-to-strong learning up to a constant factor scaling in $γ$. We then sharpen the general relationship between the classic VC dimension and the $γ$-VC dimension. Finally, we also give improved upper and lower bounds on the $γ$-VC dimension for the fundamental concept classes of decision stumps and axis-parallel rectangles in $\mathbb{R}^d$.

</details>


### [182] [OrBIT: Structure-Guided Embedding Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10385)
*Yunied Puig,Amit Kumar Jaiswal*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出OrBIT，一种从轨道动力学学习可复用局部几何来约束共享码字的嵌入压缩框架，实现高倍率压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入表压缩方法固定编码几何（如坐标块、低秩子空间或自由码本）并在其中优化，本文则探索编码几何本身能否被学习发现。

Method: OrBIT利用轨道动力学学习可复用局部几何，约束少量共享码字；全局重建残差决定固定编码预算的分配，冗余重叠图使局部误差在拼接后相互补偿；理论分析紧图几何对失真的控制、全局残差引导的顺序分配及数据几何引导的细化；最终编译掉轨道机制，留下紧凑解码器。

Result: 在四个LLM嵌入表上，相对16位存储，GPT-2上实现37.9倍压缩，每个7B表上超过23倍，同时率失真性能与现有量化和低秩基线相当。

Conclusion: 学习到的编码几何可有效指导存储内容、容量分配和局部信息全局组装，实现高压缩率且保持竞争力的率失真表现。

Abstract: Embedding tables are among the largest components of modern language models. Most compression methods fix a coding geometry such as coordinate blocks, low-rank subspaces, or unrestricted codebooks, and optimize within it. We instead ask whether the coding geometry can itself be discovered. We introduce \emph{OrBIT}, a structure-guided embedding compression framework that learns reusable local geometry from orbit dynamics and uses it to constrain a small set of shared codewords. The global reconstruction residual then decides where the fixed coding budget is spent, while redundant overlapping charts let local errors compensate one another after gluing. Our theory shows how tight-chart geometry controls distortion, how the global residual directs sequential allocation, and how data-geometry-guided refinement improves the codec.
