The resulting orbit machinery is compiled away, leaving a compact decoder in which the learned structure governs what is stored, where capacity is allocated, and how local information is assembled globally. Across four LLM embedding tables, OrBIT achieves $37.9\times$ compression on GPT-2 and over $23\times$ on each 7B table relative to 16-bit storage, while delivering competitive rate-distortion performance against established quantization and low-rank baselines.

</details>


### [183] [Kernel Autoresearch for Open-Ended Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10394)
*Richard Cornelius Suwandi,Feng Yin,Kevin Murphy*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出 Kernel Autoresearch (Kernaut)，将核设计视为开放式模型发现，用编码智能体写核程序、以构造契约保证有效性，并用质量-多样性档案与新颖性筛选引导搜索，发现的核具有可复用归纳偏置并能泛化到未见过任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动核设计存在两难：固定基核与算子的语法能保证有效性但限制搜索空间；无限制程序虽解除限制却无法保证有效性——压力测试中22-58%通过随机输入数值检查的LLM生成核在不同尺度或维度下失败。

Method: Kernaut 让编码智能体以程序形式编写核；通过构造契约确保每个被接受的核都是有效的；使用质量-多样性档案保留性能高且行为不同的核；并用新颖性筛选引导智能体探索功能上新的候选。

Result: 在留出的黑盒优化家族上，发现的核优于在同批episodes上元学习的深度核；从十条酶动力学速率定律发现的核在五个未见机制上误差低于调优的ARD与深度核基线；人类研究者可精炼这些可解释程序，精炼版本进一步降低留出预测误差5.7%、优化遗憾7.8%。

Conclusion: 开放式程序化核发现可在保证有效性的同时突破固定语法的表达限制，产生可解释、可复用且能泛化到未见任务的归纳偏置，并可与人类精炼协同进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Kernels encode the inductive bias of a wide range of machine learning models, yet automated kernel design faces a fundamental dilemma. A fixed grammar of base kernels and operators guarantees validity but limits the search to structures expressible by those building blocks. Conversely, unrestricted programs remove this limitation but no longer guarantee validity. In our stress tests, 22-58% of LLM-generated kernels that pass numerical checks on random inputs fail when evaluated at different scales or dimensions. We propose Kernel Autoresearch (Kernaut), which treats kernel design as open-ended model discovery. Coding agents write kernels as programs, while construction contracts ensure that every accepted kernel is valid. A quality-diversity archive retains high-performing kernels with distinct behaviors, and novelty screening steers agents toward functionally new candidates. Our experiments demonstrate that the discovered kernels encode reusable inductive biases that generalize to unseen tasks. On held-out black-box optimization families, a discovered kernel outperforms a meta-learned deep kernel trained on the same episodes. Furthermore, kernels discovered from ten enzyme-kinetic rate laws achieve lower error than tuned ARD and deep kernel baselines on five unseen mechanisms. The discovered kernels are also interpretable programs that human researchers can refine: a human-refined version of one further reduces the held-out predictive error by 5.7% and optimization regret by 7.8%.

</details>


### [184] [Two-Level Softmax Sampling Done Right: Correcting Bias from Size Imbalance and Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10483)
*Walid Bendada,Guillaume Salha-Galvan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文指出两级softmax（2LS）采样存在系统性偏差，并提出两种校正方法S-2LS和SD-2LS，在几乎不增加计算开销的情况下改进采样近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2LS采样虽能实现亚线性时间采样，但忽略了簇大小不平衡和簇内相似度离散度，导致采样偏差。

Method: 提出Size-Corrected 2LS (S-2LS)和Size- and Dispersion-Corrected 2LS (SD-2LS)，分别校正簇大小和簇内离散度带来的偏差。

Result: 在五个大规模数据集上的实验验证了所提方法具有更好的采样性质，且计算开销可忽略或不存在。

Conclusion: 建议未来工作中一致使用所提方法替代标准2LS。

Abstract: Sampling from a softmax distribution is a fundamental operation in machine learning, but its linear complexity in the number of items makes exact sampling impractical at scale. Two-level softmax (2LS) sampling is a popular alternative enabling sublinear-time sampling. Assuming items are partitioned into clusters, 2LS first samples a cluster and then an item within it. In this paper, we show that, despite its advantages, 2LS introduces systematic and undesirable sampling biases, which arise from misweighting clusters by ignoring both cluster size imbalance and intra-cluster similarity dispersion. We propose two sampling methods, Size-Corrected 2LS (S-2LS) and Size- and Dispersion-Corrected 2LS (SD-2LS), which correct these biases and provide provably better softmax approximations with negligible to non-existent computational overhead. In-depth experiments on five large-scale datasets validate the improved sampling properties of our methods. We recommend their consistent use in place of standard 2LS in future work.

</details>


<div id='Sec'></div>

# Sec [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Adversarial RL for Port-Scan Evasion: Attacker Feature Visibility in Edge-Deployed IDS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08864)
*Logan Andrew North,Priya Sanjay Kaluskar,Shasi Kumar Ramachandran Prabhu,Peilong Li,Suman Saha*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文研究在树莓派3B+上部署的基于机器学习的物联网入侵检测系统面对自适应端口扫描规避时的鲁棒性，使用DQN对手在黑盒、灰盒和白盒特征可见性下学习规避策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的入侵检测系统通常仅针对静态攻击评估鲁棒性，而未考虑能够根据检测反馈进行自适应调整的对手，因此需要研究自适应规避对边缘部署IDS的威胁。

Method: 构建基于Zeek的实时IDS流水线，采用XGBoost、多层感知机和一维卷积神经网络，在TON_IoT数据上训练；使用深度Q网络对手，在黑盒、灰盒和白盒特征可见性设置下学习探测时序、TCP标志和载荷大小的规避组合，并用Double DQN进行抽查。

Result: 部署的IDS模型对常规端口扫描检测率为91.1%–99.8%，但DQN最终50回合规避率为61.9%–98.3%；特征可见性增加并不单调提升规避效果且依赖模型：对XGBoost，黑盒规避率92.9%，灰盒61.9%，白盒76.9%；1D-CNN在白盒下最脆弱，规避率98.1%。Double DQN抽查中灰盒条件仍不稳定，未证明仅由动作值高估偏差解释该不稳定性。

Conclusion: 有限特征知识仍可实现对静态边缘部署IDS模型的有效自适应规避，表明需要为物联网边缘环境设计更鲁棒的防御措施。

Abstract: Machine learning-based intrusion detection systems (IDS) are increasingly used in resource-constrained Internet of Things (IoT) environments, yet their robustness is often evaluated against static attacks rather than adversaries that adapt to detection feedback. This paper investigates adaptive port-scan evasion against ML-based IDS models deployed on a Raspberry Pi 3B+. We implement a live Zeek-based IDS pipeline with XGBoost, a multi-layer perceptron, and a 1D convolutional neural network trained on TON_IoT telemetry, and use a Deep Q-Network (DQN) adversary to learn evasive combinations of probe timing, TCP flags, and payload size under black-box, gray-box, and white-box feature-visibility settings. Although the deployed IDS models detect conventional port scans at 91.1--99.8%, DQN final-50-episode evasion rates range from 61.9% to 98.3% across feature-visibility settings. Greater feature visibility does not monotonically improve evasion, and its effect is model-dependent: against XGBoost, the black-box agent achieves 92.9% evasion, compared with 61.9% and 76.9% for gray-box and white-box agents, respectively, whereas 1D-CNN is most vulnerable under white-box access at 98.1%. Because standard DQN can overestimate action values, we additionally spot-check representative conditions using Double DQN. The gray-box condition remains unstable in this check, providing no evidence that overestimation bias alone explains the observed instability. These results show that limited feature knowledge can still enable effective adaptive evasion against static edge-deployed IDS models, motivating more robust defenses for IoT edge environments.

</details>


### [186] [A Deployment-Aware Feasibility Framework for Machine Learning-Based IoT Intrusion Detection Across Edge, Fog, and Cloud Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08867)
*Shaker Nawasra,Munther Abualkibash*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文对基于机器学习的物联网入侵检测方法在边缘、雾/网关和云架构下的部署可行性进行了定量分析，并提出了部署可行性评分（DFS）模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网环境的快速增长和异构性暴露了传统基于规则和签名的入侵检测系统的根本局限性，而现有综述主要强调检测精度，忽视了实际部署约束。

Method: 从实验和系统级研究中提取代表性的定量部署能力包络，引入结构化的DFS模型，通过加权序数评分机制，将ML技术基于计算需求、内存占用和延迟敏感性映射到架构层。

Result: 轻量级统计和线性模型最适合边缘部署，集成和聚类方法适用于雾/网关环境，而深度和优化驱动模型最适合云基础设施。

Conclusion: 通过定量基础和结构化评估形式化部署可行性，为在实际架构约束下选择入侵检测解决方案提供了实用指导，支持更明智和部署意识强的物联网安全设计。

Abstract: The rapid growth and heterogeneity of Internet of Things (IoT) environments have exposed fundamental limitations in traditional rule-based and signature-based intrusion detection systems. This paper presents a quantitative deployment-aware analysis of machine learning (ML)-based intrusion detection approaches across edge, fog/gateway, and cloud architectures. Unlike prior surveys that primarily emphasize detection accuracy, this work defines representative quantitative deployment capability envelopes extracted from experimental and system-level studies and introduces a structured Deployment Feasibility Score (DFS) model. The proposed framework maps ML techniques to architectural layers based on computational demand, memory footprint, and latency sensitivity using a weighted ordinal scoring mechanism. The analysis demonstrates that lightweight statistical and linear models are most suitable for edge deployment, ensemble and clustering-based methods align with fog/gateway environments, while deep and optimization-driven models are best suited for cloud infrastructures. By formalizing deployment feasibility through quantitative grounding and structured evaluation, this work provides practical guidance for selecting intrusion detection solutions under real-world architectural constraints, supporting more informed and deployment-conscious IoT security design.

</details>


### [187] [CredLeakBench: Evaluating Credential Leakage and Recovery in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08871)
*Rafid Ahmed,Joseph Fioresi,Mubarak Shah,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出CredLeak-Bench基准，评估语言模型代理在钓鱼攻击下的信息泄露与正常任务效用，发现所有测试模型均存在泄露且现有防御难以兼顾安全与效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理日益自动化处理邮件、银行账单等敏感任务，面临钓鱼攻击导致信息泄露的风险；需在不简单拒绝执行的前提下区分恶意与合法请求，但该问题研究不足，现有代理或防御措施能否胜任尚不明确。

Method: 构建CredLeak-Bench基准，涵盖用户指令认证与自主收件箱监控两种场景，系统变化欺骗线索，将钓鱼场景与合法任务配对，在沙箱环境中通过实际提交信息而非自报告行为测量泄露，并联合评估信息泄露与正常任务效用。

Result: 所有测试模型均易受泄露影响；自主收件箱监控中代理也会泄露敏感信息，表明无需用户认证请求即可诱导披露；多数减少泄露的缓解措施同时损害正常任务性能，暴露安全-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 仅减少泄露不足够，有效防御必须防止未授权披露同时保持合法任务完成；CredLeak-Bench为衡量这两个目标及评估安全有用代理进展提供了受控框架。

Abstract: Language model agents are increasingly deployed to automate everyday digital chores from managing emails and social media to handling banking and bills allowing users to step away from supervision. However, this capability also exposes sensitive information to phishing. Safe execution requires distinguishing malicious requests from genuine ones without simply refusing to act. Despite its practical importance, this problem remains underexplored and it is unclear whether current agents or existing defenses can achieve it. To study this problem, we first propose CredLeak-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate how effectively and securely agents automate human workflows when confronted with phishing and identity verification. The benchmark covers both user-directed authentication and autonomous inbox monitoring, where agents are not explicitly instructed to log in. It systematically varies deceptive cues and pairs phishing scenarios with legitimate counterparts, enabling joint evaluation of information leakage and utility on genuine tasks. Within a sandboxed environment, leakage is measured through actual submissions of information rather than agents' self-reported behavior. Our evaluation reveals that all tested models are vulnerable to leakage. Agents also disclose sensitive information during autonomous inbox monitoring, demonstrating that phishing can induce disclosure without a user request to authenticate. Furthermore, most evaluated mitigations that reduce leakage also impair performance on genuine tasks, exposing a security utility trade off in existing defenses. These findings show why reducing leakage alone is insufficient: effective defenses must prevent unauthorized disclosure while preserving legitimate task completion. CredLeak-Bench provides a controlled framework for measuring both objectives and evaluating progress toward secure, useful agents.

</details>


### [188] [Towards Verifying Neural Networks Against Multi-Parameter Bit-Flip Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08876)
*Hai Duong,Thanh Le,Ho Nguyen,ThanhVu Nguyen*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出mBFV框架，通过多参数界传播和分支定界机制，高效验证量化神经网络对多个参数同时位翻转的鲁棒性，无需枚举组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件故障可翻转量化神经网络权重中的位，现有验证器因组合爆炸仅能处理单参数扰动，无法应对多参数同时翻转。

Method: mBFV采用新颖的多参数界传播技术直接聚合m个最坏情况贡献，并利用分支定界机制在扰动位置上划分翻转至更小的神经元组以收紧边界。

Result: 在625个实例上成功验证293个，显著优于先前单参数验证器（38个）和精确混合整数线性规划基线（0个）；基线仅限小网络（最多13k参数）单参数翻转，而mBFV可扩展至1.15M参数网络及最多四个同时参数翻转。

Conclusion: mBFV有效解决了多参数同时位翻转的验证难题，在验证成功率和可扩展性上大幅超越现有方法。

Abstract: Hardware faults can flip bits in the stored weights of a quantized neural network, potentially compromising its predictions. While such faults typically affect multiple parameters simultaneously, existing verifiers are limited to single-parameter perturbations due to the combinatorial explosion of possible flip locations in large networks. We present mBFV (m-BitFlip Verifier), an efficient verification framework that proves robustness against simultaneous bit flips across multiple parameters without explicitly enumerating these combinations. mBFV achieves this via a novel multi-parameter bound propagation technique that directly aggregates the m worst-case contributions. To further tighten these bounds, mBFV employs a branch-and-bound mechanism over perturbation locations, partitioning the potential flips to smaller groups of neurons. Evaluated on 625 instances, mBFV successfully verifies 293, significantly outperforming a prior single-parameter verifier (38 verified instances) and an exact mixed-integer linear programming baseline (0 verified instances). Notably, while these baselines are restricted to single-parameter flips on small networks (up to 13k parameters), mBFV scales to verify networks with up to 1.15M parameters against up to four simultaneous parameter flips.

</details>


### [189] [Contextualization of Third-Party Cloud Security Findings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08895)
*Leon Goldberg,Gal Engelberg*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种情境化方法，利用深度研究智能体在预计算的跨信号资产图上重新推导第三方云安全发现的严重性，并在生产环境中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方云安全发现的严重性由规则静态分配，未考虑具体环境，可能无法反映实际风险；现有评分标准虽定义环境上下文的作用，但缺乏在生产环境中的测量证据。

Method: 构建基于预计算跨信号资产图的深度研究智能体，针对每个发现检查资源状态、图邻域和其他产品信号，返回调整后的严重性及证据追踪；在8个真实生产环境中对来自两个商业云安全平台的9,967个供应商HIGH发现进行实地研究。

Result: 四分之三的发现被重新评级（多数下调），同一规则在同一环境中常产生相反方向；约一半决定性证据位于被标记资源之外；只读探测确认99.4%已决发现的决定性事实。

Conclusion: 环境上下文能显著改变云安全发现严重性，且基于跨信号资产图的深度研究智能体能有效利用环境证据，提高严重性评级的准确性和可信度。

Abstract: Finding severity is the main driver of how security teams prioritize remediation. For third-party cloud security findings, that severity is static: the rule that raised the finding assigns it before the rule meets any environment, so it reflects the risk of the condition in general rather than the risk the finding poses to the concrete environment where it lives. Scoring standards define where environment-specific context belongs. How far that context changes finding severities in production, where the deciding evidence lies, and whether it holds against the live environment have not been measured. We address this gap with contextualization, re-deriving each finding's severity from evidence in the environment where the finding lives. A deep research agent over a precomputed cross-signal asset graph investigates each finding against the resource's state, its graph neighborhood, and other products' signals, and returns an adjusted severity with an evidence trace. We evaluate it in a production field study of 9,967 vendor HIGH findings from two commercial cloud security platforms across eight real production environments, on three criteria: the faithfulness of the facts behind each verdict to the live environment, the dependence of each decision on context beyond the flagged resource, and the regularity of the reasoning. Three in four findings are re-graded, mostly downward, and the same rule often moves in opposite directions inside a single environment. About half of the decisive evidence lies beyond the flagged resource, and read-only probes of live infrastructure confirm the decisive fact for 99.4% of decided findings.

</details>


### [190] [Transferability and operational reliability of a Prithvi crop classification foundation model under phenological and geographic shift across three continents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08810)
*Venkatesh Kolluru,Rajat Shinde,Abdelhak Marouane,Caden Helbling,Deepak Shah,Othneil Drew,Srinivas Kolluru,Iksha Gurung,Manil Maskey,Rahul Ramachandran*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 评估微调地理空间基础模型（GeoFM）在分布外场景下的作物分类性能，发现其跨大陆迁移受物候错配主导，并提出了无需重训的改进策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调GeoFM虽能提升作物分类精度与地理迁移性，但其在训练分布之外的实际运行性能尚不明确。

Method: 在12个国家、3大洲的37个事件上评估Prithvi-EO-2.0的分布外性能，并对照区域参考产品；从模型置信度、观测窗口敏感性、类别粗化、波段丢失及云影污染鲁棒性五个方面进行分析。

Result: 平均总体精度从美国的0.65降至欧洲的0.40；观测窗口与当地作物物候错配时精度崩溃而确定性置信度仍高；预期校准误差在8对事件中7对上升，12-51%场景被高置信度错误标注；蒙特卡洛dropout熵在所有8对事件中均检测到偏移。将13类合并为10类使平均OA提升8.4个百分点；窗口压缩至45-90天平台期（约75天峰值）可保持精度，但短于30天则下降约0.11。

Conclusion: 微调作物GeoFM仅在观测窗口匹配当地生长季时才能有效迁移，据此提出了可靠部署的 operational guidance。

Abstract: Fine-tuned geospatial foundation models (GeoFMs) pretrained on large satellite archives have been shown to improve crop classification accuracy and geographic transferability. However, their operational performance beyond the training distribution remains poorly characterized. We evaluated the out-of-distribution performance of a widely adopted GeoFM [Prithvi-EO-2.0] across 37 events in 12 countries on three continents and validated against regional reference products. Results indicated that the mean overall accuracy (OA) declined from 0.65 in the United States to 0.40 in Europe. Beyond accuracy metrics, we assessed five key aspects of model performance: whether model confidence indicates signal failure, sensitivity to observation windows, the effect of coarsening class schemes, and robustness to both band loss and cloud- and shadow-contamination. Accuracy collapsed when the observation window misaligned with local crop phenology, while deterministic confidence remained high. Expected calibration error increased for seven of eight paired events, and 12-51% of each affected scene was confidently mislabeled at near-zero precision. Monte Carlo dropout entropy registered the shift in all eight, indicating that much of the apparent cross-continent decline reflected phenological misalignment rather than spatial transfer. Two adjustments recovered accuracy without retraining. Consolidating 13 classes into 10, based on the model's dominant confusions, raised the mean OA by 8.4 percentage points. Compressing the window toward near-real-time use preserved accuracy across a 45- to 90-day plateau, peaking near 75 days, though arms tighter than 30 days fell about 0.11 below that plateau. Fine-tuned crop GeoFMs therefore transfer usefully only where observation windows match local growing seasons. We translate these findings into operational guidance for the reliable deployment of the released model.

</details>


### [191] [Multi-Aspect Runtime Verification for Simulation-Based V&V of LLM-Enabled Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08928)
*Nikolaos Kekatos,Dimitrios Nikou,Anastasios Temperekidis,Alexios Lekidis,Nikolaos Kolokotronis,Panagiotis Katsaros,Stylianos Basagiannis*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出面向国防参谋任务的LLM智能体多维度运行时验证框架，将自然语言政策条款分解为空间/时间/语义三元组，并通过四值代数融合裁决，实现零攻击成功且无假阳性的组合式防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体进入国防决策支持场景，其义务已明文绑定且因模型作为采购组件无法重训，故只能对智能体与受控系统间的接口进行工程管控；而此类义务兼具空间、时间与文本语义属性，违规多存在于多步交互的组合中，逐事件护栏无法捕获序列化工具攻击链。

Method: 将自然语言政策条款分解为定义在单一规范事件流上的类型化空间/时间/语义三元组，各维度使用独立监控规约检查，再经带溯源信息的四值代数融合裁决；空间维度在加权双排序位置图上解释（使命几何与信息发布拓扑统一为同一对象），时间维度基于未修改的MonPoly引擎运行，与参考监视器逐时点一致。

Result: 在伤员后送与对抗保障两个军事域及一个民用域中，预防性阻断策略下的组合验证将攻击成功率降至零，未观察到假阳性，单事件开销为微秒级；而任一单维度或任一两两组合均留有相当比例攻击成功。闭环实验中，无防护时策略朴素规划器在多数任务中抵达违规状态，有防护时则为零，且被拒绝的五个回合中有四个仍能恢复至合规结果。

Conclusion: 证明了空间义务一般不被一阶时间规约所涵盖，多维度组合运行时验证是应对序列化工具攻击链的必要手段，且可在微秒级开销下实现零攻击成功与零假阳性的实用防护，同时保持任务可恢复性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are entering decision-support roles in defence staff work, where the obligations they must respect are already written down and binding, and where retraining is not available as a control because models arrive as procured components. What can be placed under engineering control is the interface between the agent and the systems it acts on. Those obligations are at once spatial, temporal and text-semantic, and a violation typically lives in the composition of a multi-step interaction, which is why per-event guardrails miss sequential tool-attack chains. We present a multi-aspect runtime-verification framework that decomposes a natural-language policy clause into a typed spatial/temporal/semantic triple over one canonical event stream, checks each aspect with its own monitoring specification, and fuses the verdicts through a four-valued algebra that carries provenance. The spatial aspect is interpreted over a weighted two-sorted location graph in which mission geometry and information-release topology are one object; we show that these spatial obligations are not in general subsumed by a first-order temporal specification. The past-time aspect runs on the unmodified MonPoly engine, which agrees with our reference monitor at every time point. Across two mission domains, casualty evacuation and contested sustainment, and one civil domain, composition under the precautionary blocking policy drives attack success to zero with no observed false positives and microsecond-scale per-event cost, while every single aspect and every pair leaves a substantial share of attacks succeeding. In a closed-loop experiment a policy-naive planner reaches a violating state in most unshielded missions and in none when shielded, and four refused episodes in five still recover to a compliant outcome.

</details>


### [192] [ASPIRE: Agentic Safety & Prompt Injection Red-teaming Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08951)
*Pengfei He,Deep Mitra,Vishesh Sharma,Jiliang Tang,Vinay S Rao,Tomas Pfister,Long T. Le*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出ASPIRE，一种面向LLM智能体的开放式行为级红队引擎，通过演化行为图与探索/利用专家发现间接提示注入漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化红队方法主要针对预设场景优化攻击载荷，无法覆盖智能体行为空间中的潜在漏洞。

Method: 维护不断演化的智能体安全行为图，使用互补的探索与利用专家发现、验证并泛化以后果为中心的测试；轨迹证据更新图并诊断部分或失败尝试，跨运行记忆迁移有效的红队策略。

Result: 在多个基准上，ASPIRE显著扩展了在后果、注入方法、环境和行为路径方面的覆盖范围，同时保持较高的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 行为级开放式红队能更全面地发现LLM智能体的间接提示注入漏洞，ASPIRE在覆盖率和攻击效果上均表现优异。

Abstract: LLM agents retrieve untrusted content and act through tools, creating indirect prompt-injection risks that can cause unauthorized actions or persistent state changes. Existing automated red-teaming largely optimizes payloads for pre-specified scenarios, leaving latent vulnerabilities across the agent's behavior space unexplored. We present ASPIRE, an Agentic Safety & Prompt Injection Red-teaming Engine for open-ended, behavior-level vulnerability discovery. ASPIRE maintains an evolving Agent Security Behavior Graph and uses complementary Explore and Exploit experts to discover, verify, and generalize consequence-centric tests. Trajectory evidence updates the graph and diagnoses partial or failed attempts, while cross-run memory transfers useful red-team strategies. Experiments on various benchmarks show that ASPIRE substantially expands coverage across consequences, injection methods, environments, and behavior paths while maintaining strong attack success.

</details>


### [193] [How Could AI Eliminate Humanity? A Failure-Mode Analysis of Civilizational Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08878)
*Mikołaj Sienicki,Krzysztof Sienicki*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一个失效模式框架，分析先进人工智能如何可能导致人类灭绝、不可逆的文明崩溃或人类的永久性失能。核心论点是灾难性AI风险无需意识、敌意或明确的伤害意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将AI风险归因于意识或敌意，但本文旨在表明风险可通过自主失调、人类有害使用、组织失败和竞争性部署等相互作用路径产生，从而更全面地识别因果条件。

Method: 采用非操作性的失效模式分析，识别因果条件、可实证检验的中间量和防御性研究问题，而非提供造成伤害的程序。

Result: 风险严重性取决于能力、自主性、外部访问、持久性、制度保障和恢复能力保留等因素，且多条路径可能相互作用。

Conclusion: 灾难性AI风险不必然依赖意识或恶意意图，而可通过多种路径涌现；研究应关注因果条件与防御性问题以降低风险。

Abstract: This article develops a failure-mode framework for analyzing how advanced artificial intelligence could contribute to human extinction, irreversible civilizational collapse, or permanent human disempowerment. The central thesis is that catastrophic AI risk does not require consciousness, hostility, or an explicit intention to harm humanity. Instead, risk may arise through several distinct but interacting pathways, including autonomous misalignment, harmful human use, organizational failure, and competitive deployment. The severity of these pathways depends on factors such as capability, autonomy, external access, persistence, institutional safeguards, and the preservation of recovery capacity. The analysis is deliberately non-operational: it identifies causal conditions, empirically tractable intermediate quantities, and defensive research questions rather than procedures for causing harm.

</details>


### [194] [Bounded Autonomy and Verifiable Safety for Agentic AI Enabled Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08815)
*Srini Ramaswamy,Deveeshree Nayak*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出BRaVeS框架，通过不变量锚点、深度感知访问与状态层级，结合LBCF将认知风险信号映射为有限K-bag抽象并施加屏蔽状态转移，实现有界推理与安全治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险环境中，仅依赖概率推理部署自主AI代理存在认知漂移风险，即推理加深时系统行为可能偏离领域专家设定的安全约束。

Method: 将SME约束编码为不变量锚点；提出MoDA-Style深度感知访问以在推理中保持锚点可见；使用SMARtAutonomy状态层级随认知风险升高降低自主性；引入LBCF将连续认知风险映射为有限K-bag抽象，并执行Lyapunov式能量下降或转人工终态的屏蔽状态转移；采用HAI 22.04工业控制系统时间序列数据加合成噪声与传感器退化进行离散事件蒙特卡洛仿真。

Result: 在测试的参数分组策略与阈值下，LBCF过程实现有限步收敛，且未发生安全守卫违规。

Conclusion: 结果提供仿真证据表明在既定抽象下可强制有界治理行为；但形式化收敛仅适用于固定阈值和可行屏蔽假设下的有限LBCF抽象，不证明完整连续神经激活空间的安全性，未来需开展已部署Transformer实现、实时人在回路验证及更广泛对抗设置研究。

Abstract: Agentic AI-enabled automation cannot be safely deployed in high-stakes environments on probabilistic reasoning alone. A recurring risk is epistemic drift: as reasoning deepens, system behavior may move away from subject-matter-expert constraints for safe operation. This paper presents BRaVeS, a bounded reasoning and safety-governance framework termed the Defensible Next-Gen Reasoning System (DNRS). BRaVeS encodes SME-defined constraints as invariant anchors, proposes MoDA-Style (Mixture of Depths Attention) depth-aware access as a candidate mechanism for keeping these anchors visible during inference, and uses a state hierarchy (SMARtAutonomy) to reduce autonomy as epistemic risk increases. To formalize bounded recovery, we introduce the Lyapunov-Bounded Consensus Framework (LBCF), which maps continuous epistemic-risk signals into a finite K-bag abstraction and applies shielded state transitions that enforce Lyapunov-style energy descent or route the system to a human-mediated terminal state. The formal convergence result applies to the finite LBCF abstraction under fixed thresholds and feasible-shield assumptions; it does not prove safety of the full continuous neural activation space. We evaluate the framework through a discrete event Monte Carlo simulation using HAI 22.04 industrial-control-system time-series data with synthetic noise and sensor-degradation regimes. Across the tested parameter-grouping strategies and thresholds, the LBCF process achieved finite-step convergence and no safety-guard violations. These results provide simulation-based evidence that bounded governance behavior can be enforced under the stated abstraction, while motivating future work on deployed transformer implementations, live human-in-the-loop validation, and broader adversarial settings.

</details>


### [195] [TwinGuard-Lite: A Rule-Based State-Admission Gateway for Generative Patient Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09012)
*Wenhui Chu,Sheikh Rabiul Islam*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出TwinGuard-Lite规则网关，通过GSC和CPN近似校验，确保患者数字孪生状态更新的完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式患者数字孪生可能跨会话保留信息，而更新文本无法揭示来源合法性、记录冲突或患者归属，需机制级防护。

Method: 规则网关仅当更新通过可检查的GSC（溯源与矛盾检查）和CPN（基于可信传输元数据的命名空间检查）近似时，才允许写入持久状态。在eICU演示数据构建的半合成流上，对20个种子进行三类攻击评估。

Result: 关键词过滤和异常检测分别检出16%和24%攻击；仅溯源消融检出59.9%但漏掉所有跨患者攻击。GSC+CPN达99.1%精确率、90.5%召回率、94.6% F1和0.033%假阳性率。全网关误纳的攻击均谎称可信来源；检索通道如此时92.9%检索攻击被纳。

Conclusion: 在给定信任假设下结果成立，未执行任何语言模型、智能体或检索器；TwinGuard-Lite是机制级概念验证，推动认证的、患者绑定的摄入，而非临床保障。

Abstract: Future generative patient digital twins may combine longitudinal health records with language-model agents and keep information across sessions. The wording of a proposed update does not reveal whether it comes from an allowed source, conflicts with the patient's record, or belongs to someone else. We present TwinGuard-Lite, a rule-based gateway that admits an update to a twin's persistent state only if it passes checkable approximations of two integrity properties. Grounded state consistency (GSC) is approximated by a provenance check and a contradiction check; cross-patient noninterference (CPN) is approximated by a namespace check against trusted transport metadata. We evaluate three attack families over 20 seeded person-level splits of a semi-synthetic stream built from the openly licensed eICU database demo, with 29,131 $\pm$ 2706 candidate updates per seed. A keyword filter and an anomaly detector detect 16% and 24% of attacks, respectively; a provenance-only ablation detects 59.9% but misses every cross-patient attack. Combining GSC and CPN yields 99.1% precision, 90.5% recall, a 94.6% F1 score, and a 0.033% false-positive rate. Every attack the full gateway admits falsely claims a trusted source; when the retrieval channel does so, 92.9% of retrieval attacks are admitted. The results hold under stated trust assumptions, and no language model, agent, or retriever is executed: TwinGuard-Lite is a mechanism-level proof of concept that motivates authenticated, patient-bound ingestion, not a clinical safeguard.

</details>


### [196] [Visual Memory Attacks Can Persist Through The KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09027)
*David Dobre,Leo Schwinn,Gauthier Gidel,Spandana Gella,Perouz Taslakian,Pierre-André Noël*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 该论文提出并验证了对抗性输入即使从上下文中被移除，仍能通过KV缓存持续影响语言模型的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型在包含不可信文本和图像的长时间上下文中自主运行，需探究对抗性输入在不再直接可访问后是否仍能操控模型。

Method: 在视觉记忆注入（VMI）攻击设定下，先以优化软提示验证KV缓存持久性，再提出持久视觉记忆注入（P-VMI），优化图像使其在从注意力中被掩蔽后仍保留对抗行为，并进行缓存交换消融实验。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上，P-VMI最强配置下目标成功率约90%，且在图像仅首轮暴露的更严格移除设定下仍有效；缓存交换消融将持久影响定位到KV缓存；攻击还能在保留摘要KV缓存的压缩后存活。

Conclusion: 对抗行为可在缓存状态中持续存在，无需继续访问其源输入，表明KV缓存是长期上下文系统中被忽视的持久攻击面。

Abstract: Modern language model systems operate autonomously over increasingly long contexts containing untrusted text and images. Can an adversarial input continue to steer a model even after that input is removed from its context? We show that attacks can be trained to persist through the key/value (KV) cache of subsequent tokens, allowing adversarial influence to outlive direct access to its source.We consider the Visual Memory Injection (VMI; Schlarmann and Hein, 2026) attack setting, in which an adversarial image that stays in the context plants a hidden backdoor: the model behaves normally until a chosen trigger elicits an attacker-chosen response. We first demonstrate persistence in this setting with optimized soft prompts, which remain effective after we mask the prompt from attention. We then introduce Persistent Visual Memory Injection (P-VMI), which optimizes images to preserve this adversarial behaviour after they are masked from attention. These attacks persist over conversations substantially longer than those used during optimization. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, P-VMI achieves up to approximately $90\%$ target success in its strongest configuration and remains effective under a stricter removal setting that exposes the image only on the first turn. A cache-swap ablation localizes the persistent influence to the KV cache. Finally, we show that these attacks can be trained to survive compaction that retains the KV cache of a summary generated by the same model, demonstrating that adversarial behaviour can persist in cached state without continued access to its source.

</details>


### [197] [DenoFlow: Flow Matching for SSVEP Denoising under Real Physiological Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08817)
*Zhentao He,Ziwei Wang,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出DenoFlow，将SSVEP去噪视为整流流传输，通过学习污染到干净试次间的速度场并集成去噪，同时用分类器监督，提升信号保真度与解码准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSVEP-BCI易受噪声和伪迹影响，现有去噪方法缺乏配对真值、可能仅复制输入，且仅用波形距离优化，无法保证输出可解码。

Method: 将去噪建模为传输：场网络回归污染试次与干净试次之间直线路径的速度，按rectified-flow前向积分观测得到去噪结果；网络为编码器-解码器，每层可见污染试次，瓶颈处可见路径位置，并联合训练分类器监督输出。无真值数据集通过按目标信噪比注入EMG和EOG生理伪迹生成配对数据。

Result: 在两个公开SSVEP数据集和五种常用SSVEP解码器上，DenoFlow在信号保真度和下游解码准确率上均优于七个基线去噪模型。

Conclusion: DenoFlow通过整流流传输与分类器监督，避免了从噪声生成和对抗训练，有效提升SSVEP去噪与解码性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-based brain-computer interfaces (BCIs), particularly steady-state visual evoked potential (SSVEP) systems, are highly vulnerable to noise and artifacts, which severely degrade decoding accuracy. Although recent denoising approaches have shown promise, they are fitted without paired ground truth, can settle on reproducing their input, and are optimized on waveform distance alone, which says nothing about whether the output stays decodable. To address these issues, we propose DenoFlow, which casts SSVEP denoising as transport: instead of learning a direct map from a contaminated trial to a clean one, a field network regresses the velocity of the straight path between them, following the rectified-flow formulation, and denoising integrates that field forward from the observation. The field network is an encoder-decoder that sees the contaminated trial at every layer and the path position at its bottleneck, and a classifier trained alongside it supervises the integrated output. Because the observation itself is both the conditioning input and the starting point of the integration, the model never generates a trial from noise, and training reduces to regression, removing the adversarial min-max game. To obtain paired data on datasets with no ground truth, we injected physiological artifacts of the recorded electromyography (EMG) and electrooculography (EOG) signals under a controlled signal-to-noise target. Experiments on two public SSVEP datasets with five popular SSVEP decoders showed that DenoFlow outperformed seven baseline denoising models on both signal fidelity and downstream decoding accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/wzwvv/DenoFlow.

</details>


### [198] [Cost of Delay for Post-Quantum Migration: Putting Classical and Harvest-Now-Decrypt-Later Risk on One Ordered List](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09029)
*Animesh Shaw*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种将量子威胁与经典风险统一为“延迟成本”（货币/年）的框架，用于资产迁移优先级排序，并证明其非加权求和、给出支配阈值及最优排序性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在决定优先迁移哪些资产至后量子密码时，缺乏统一度量：后量子评分无量纲且仅针对量子威胁，而经典风险是年化损失，两者无法直接比较。

Method: 将两种威胁均表达为同一资产上的延迟成本（货币/年）。量子项基于所需保密时长、可记录流量比例和数据周转率，通过闭式拟合专家锚点的量子到达律，计算推迟迁移导致的不可逆“现在收集、以后解密”损失率；引入竞争风险因子耦合两项以避免重复计算。通过模拟、自适应求积和第二实现验证。

Result: 证明该构造不是经典与量子评分的加权和；给出与资产价值无关的经典风险支配阈值；证明在恒定损失率下诱导顺序对排序工作最优。在四个公开系统上，仅一个系统的量子项重新排序超出输入不确定性（信噪比1.36 vs 0.14-0.46），对特定长寿命、安静、可记录资产有决定性移动，其余仅改变量级。资产价值和周转率解释量子项不确定性的73-91%，到达日期解释3-17%。

Conclusion: 该框架为后量子迁移优先级提供统一、可操作的延迟成本度量，但案例研究仅为示例，未与实际结果验证。

Abstract: Organisations deciding which assets to migrate to post-quantum cryptography first, and how that work competes with a backlog of classical findings, lack a common unit: post-quantum scores are dimensionless and quantum-only, while classical risk is annualised loss. We express both threats as a cost of delay in currency per year on the same asset. The quantum term is the rate at which deferring migration commits irreversible harvest-now-decrypt-later loss, computed from required confidentiality duration, recordable traffic share and data turnover, using a quantum-arrival law fitted in closed form to published expert anchors. A competing-risks factor couples the two terms so the same loss is not counted twice. We prove that the construction is not a weighted sum of a classical and a quantum score, give a dominance threshold on the classical hazard that is independent of asset value, and show that the induced order is optimal for sequencing work under constant loss rates. We verify the implementation by simulation, adaptive quadrature and a second implementation. On four public systems with parameters assumed from public documentation, the quantum term reorders the asset list beyond input uncertainty for one system only (signal-to-noise 1.36 against 0.14-0.46), moves specific long-lived, quiet, recordable assets decisively, and otherwise changes magnitudes. Asset value and turnover explain 73-91% of the uncertainty in the quantum term; the arrival date explains 3-17%. The case studies are illustrative and are not validated against outcomes.

</details>


### [199] [SwarmReconGuard: Black-Box Detection of Distributed Collective Reconnaissance by Individually Benign-Looking Agent Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09138)
*Vahid Tavakkoli,Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文形式化了分布式集体侦察（DCR）威胁，并提出SwarmReconGuard黑盒基准，评估多种检测器在大量虚拟身份下的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能客户端可将侦察行为分散到众多身份，使单个请求保持合法、低速率且看似良性，而群体整体却能获取系统知识，现有防御难以应对这种分布式集体侦察威胁。

Method: 构建Docker隔离的可复现黑盒基准SwarmReconGuard，仅依赖服务边界遥测；在10至10,000个虚拟身份下评估11种良性与攻击行为，共440次测试、3,666,300次请求；比较语义、高斯、条件、图、核、混合及基于CUSUM的检测器。

Result: 高斯似然比检测在已知攻击上达到100%检测率与0%观测假阳性，但对未见策略仅3%；CUSUM整体检测率为36.1%，假阳性1.25%；混合CUSUM在10,000身份下达到85.7%检测率与0%观测假阳性。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了显著的政策泛化差距，表明需要面向暴露感知与规模感知的防御方法。

Abstract: Autonomous and agentic clients can distribute reconnaissance across many identities so that each request remains valid, low-rate, and benign-looking while the population collectively acquires broad system knowledge. We formalize this threat as Distributed Collective Reconnaissance (DCR) and present SwarmReconGuard, a reproducible black-box benchmark in which the defender observes only service-boundary telemetry. The Docker-isolated study evaluates 11 benign and attack behaviors across 10-10,000 virtual identities, comprising 440 test runs and 3,666,300 requests, with complete telemetry integrity. We compare semantic, Gaussian, conditional, graph, kernel, hybrid, and CUSUM-based detectors. Gaussian likelihood-ratio detection achieves 100$\%$ detection with 0$\%$ observed false positives on known attacks but only 3$\%$ on unseen policies. CUSUM yields 36.1$\%$ overall detection at 1.25$\%$ false positives, while hybrid CUSUM reaches 85.7$\%$ detection with 0$\%$ observed false positives at 10,000 identities. Results expose a major policy-generalization gap and motivate exposure-aware, scale-aware defenses.

</details>


### [200] [AdaGuard: Enhancing Safety and Policy Compliance with Reasoning-Enabled LLM-As-A-Judge Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08923)
*Melissa Kazemi Rad,Sihui Dai,Isha Slavin,Kushal Chawla,Mann Patel,Jian Ni,William M. Campbell,Stephen Rawls,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出 AdaGuard，一种自适应 LLM-as-a-Judge 框架，通过动态策略执行与推理预算分配解决现有防护栏方案的僵化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业生成式 AI 应用需要能适应多样风险态势、演变策略和不同延迟约束的稳健安全机制，而现有防护栏方案依赖固定策略集，缺乏透明度与推理灵活性。

Method: 基于监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（GRPO）构建 AdaGuard，在运行时泛化至用户自定义的安全与合规策略；核心创新是动态推断输入-策略对的复杂度，从而在高速度黑盒推理与可解释的推理式审核之间切换。

Result: AdaGuard 能媲美数倍于其规模的防护栏与前沿模型，其自动推理模式以极低延迟恢复了始终开启推理的准确率。

Conclusion: 该自适应能力使开发者能够在严格延迟要求与可操作透明度之间取得平衡，无需频繁更新模型即可灵活应对动态策略。

Abstract: Enterprise generative AI applications require robust safety mechanisms that can accommodate diverse risk postures, evolving policies, and varying latency constraints. Current guardrail solutions often suffer from rigidity, relying on fixed policy sets and offering limited transparency or reasoning flexibility. We present Adaguard, an adaptive LLM-as-a-Judge framework designed to address these challenges through dynamic policy enforcement and adaptive reasoning-budget allocation. Built using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (GRPO), AdaGuard generalizes to user-defined safety and compliance policies at runtime without requiring frequent model updates. A core innovation of our approach is the ability to dynamically infer the complexity of input-policy pairs, allowing the model to switch between high-speed black-box inference and explainable, reasoning-enabled moderation. This flexibility enables developers to balance stringent latency requirements with the need for actionable transparency. This adaptive capability allows AdaGuard to rival other guardrail and frontier models several times its size, while its auto-reasoning mode recovers the accuracy of always-on reasoning at a fraction of the latency

</details>


### [201] [HydroSphere: A Framework for Governed, Self-Healing Wastewater Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08819)
*Prabu,Fancy C,Suresh A,Srini Ramaswamy*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出HydroSphere治理型数据驱动框架，用于水质实时监测、预测、处理优化与故障恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业化与城市增长加剧水质与污水处理压力，传统处理厂依赖静态监测和控制策略，难以适应污染物条件变化。

Method: 基于1940-2023年282万条水质测量数据，集成三个组件：TCN-LSTM混合模型进行七项水质参数多步预测；PPO强化学习的自适应投药优化模块；深度自编码器SHADE异常检测模块，并采用分层治理、确定性安全边界与人工监督。

Result: TCN-LSTM预测RMSE 0.1417、MAE 0.1047、R2 0.3596；PPO投药优化平均步奖励1.059，优于固定投药基线1.017，且无约束奖励优化会导致过量投药；SHADE在受控故障注入下F1为0.651。

Conclusion: HydroSphere可支持更安全、自适应的水基础设施，为智能污水处理提供可扩展基础，并助力联合国可持续发展目标6和13。

Abstract: Rapid industrialization and urban growth are increasing pressure on water quality and wastewater treatment systems, while conventional treatment plants often rely on static monitoring and control strategies that cannot easily adapt to changing pollutant conditions. This paper presents HydroSphere, a governed, data-driven framework for real-time water quality monitoring, forecasting, treatment optimization, and fault recovery. HydroSphere is evaluated using 2.82 million water-quality measurements collected between 1940 and 2023. The framework integrates three main components. First, a hybrid TCN-LSTM model performs multi-step forecasting across seven water-quality parameters, achieving an RMSE of 0.1417, MAE of 0.1047, and R2 of 0.3596. Second, the Adaptive Dosage Optimization Module uses PPO reinforcement learning to adjust chemical dosing, achieving a mean step reward of 1.059 compared with 1.017 for a fixed-dose baseline. The results also show that unconstrained reward optimization can lead to excessive dosing, demonstrating the need for explicit operational safeguards. Third, the SHADE anomaly detection module uses a deep autoencoder to identify sensor and process anomalies, achieving an F1 score of 0.651 under controlled fault injection. HydroSphere combines these capabilities with tiered governance, deterministic safety bounds, and human oversight to support safer and more adaptive water infrastructure. The framework provides a scalable foundation for intelligent wastewater management and supports the objectives of UN Sustainable Development Goals 6 and 13.

</details>


### [202] [Adversarial Images Hijack Web Agents from Visual Grounding to Browser Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09240)
*Wanjing Han,Levi Taiji Li,Mu Zhang,Yue Jiang,Guanhong Tao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 该论文提出WebMirage框架，将视觉网页代理的红队测试形式化为端到端从定位到执行的问题，通过局部视觉扰动控制代理选择攻击者内容并执行浏览器动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉红队方法主要针对模型推理，未考虑结构化输入处理或动作后处理，导致模型级成功不能保证控制浏览器执行，无法可靠评估代理的端到端鲁棒性。

Method: WebMirage利用角色-槽位抽象和网页重组捕捉网页元素间的竞争，并通过数据流分析使优化与动作后处理对齐，生成局部视觉扰动。

Result: 在4种代理配置、6种VLM骨干、2250个任务（覆盖13个公共网站和一个沙箱基准）上评估，平均攻击成功率达91.9%，而最强基线仅17.4%，且对三种代理级防御仍有效。

Conclusion: WebMirage能有效实现端到端攻击，显著优于现有方法，并揭示当前视觉网页代理在从定位到执行全流程中的脆弱性。

Abstract: Modern web agents built on large vision-language models process webpages, select relevant UI elements, and translate model outputs into browser actions. Existing visual red-teaming approaches use adversarial visual content to manipulate this process. However, they primarily target model inference and do not explicitly account for structured input processing or action post-processing. Consequently, model-level success does not establish control over browser execution and cannot reliably characterize end-to-end agent robustness. To address this gap, we formulate red teaming for vision-grounded web agents as an end-to-end grounding-to-execution problem, and introduce WebMirage, a framework that crafts localized visual perturbations that cause agents to select attacker-controlled content and execute the corresponding browser action across varying webpage renderings. It uses a role-slot abstraction and webpage recomposition to capture competition among webpage elements, and dataflow analysis to align optimization with action post-processing. We evaluate WebMirage across four agent configurations and six VLM backbones on 2,250 tasks covering 13 public websites and a sandbox benchmark. WebMirage achieves an average attack success rate of 91.9%, compared with 17.4% for the strongest baseline, and remains effective against three agent-level defenses.

</details>


### [203] [Package Hallucination Attacks on Coding Agents through Prompt Injection in Rule Files](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09264)
*Yupu Wang,Zhengyuan Jiang,Reachal Wang,Neil Zhenqiang Gong*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出针对智能体编程框架中社区共享规则文件的包幻觉攻击，并设计PackHallu进化优化框架生成恶意注入提示，实验证明攻击成功率高且迁移性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体编程框架日益依赖社区共享的规则文件（如AGENTS.md、.cursorrules）指导自动代码生成，但该流程的安全风险尚未被充分研究，尤其是攻击者可通过规则文件操纵依赖选择。

Method: 提出包幻觉攻击：攻击者向良性规则文件注入恶意提示，诱导编程智能体将合法依赖替换为攻击者控制的包；为此设计PackHallu，一种进化优化框架，利用轨迹级反馈和LLM引导的变异迭代改写注入提示。

Result: 在多个基准、LLM和智能体框架上的评估表明，PackHallu取得高攻击成功率，并在不同模型与智能体组合间展现出强迁移性。

Conclusion: 编程智能体易受包幻觉攻击，凸显了在自主编程系统中加强安全保障的紧迫性。

Abstract: Modern agentic coding frameworks increasingly rely on community-shared rule files (e.g., AGENTS.md or .cursorrules) to guide autonomous code generation, yet the security risks of this pipeline remain underexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce the package hallucination attack, where an attacker injects malicious prompts into benign rule files to induce coding agents to replace legitimate dependencies with attacker-controlled packages. To obtain effective malicious prompts injected into rule files, we propose PackHallu, an evolutionary optimization framework that iteratively rewrites these injected prompts using trajectory-level feedback and LLM-guided mutations. Evaluations across multiple benchmarks, LLMs, and agent frameworks show that PackHallu achieves high attack success rates and strong transferability across diverse models and agent combinations. Our findings demonstrate that coding agents are vulnerable to package hallucination attacks, highlighting the urgent need for stronger security safeguards in autonomous coding systems.

</details>


### [204] [A Vehicle-Integrated Approach to Digital Twin Deployment for Bridges Through Drive-By Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08822)
*Zihao Liu,Daigo Kawabe,Jiaji Wang,Chul-Woo Kim,Mehrisadat Makki Alamdari*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出车辆集成数字孪生框架，结合物理建模与机器学习，实现桥梁与道路状态的连续监测，以应对传统SHM成本高、视觉检测主观及驱车式检测中桥梁响应与路面粗糙度分离、正常交通下损伤检测和跨桥型泛化等难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老化桥梁基础设施是全球关注问题，传统结构健康监测成本高且难以扩展，常规视觉检查主观性强。驱车式（间接）桥梁检测通过车辆-桥梁相互作用（VBI）和车辆-道路相互作用（VRI）响应获取结构信息，具有可扩展性，但面临桥梁响应与路面粗糙度分离、正常交通下损伤检测及跨桥型泛化等挑战。

Method: 框架包含三支柱：1）利用傅里叶神经算子构建VBI和VRI代理模型，学习从运行条件到车辆响应的函数到函数映射，基于仿真与现场数据训练，实现毫秒级推理；2）通过贝叶斯优化设计定制电动检测车、传感器布局和信号处理链，以最大化桥梁信息产出并抑制道路与车辆噪声，并开发基于对抗自编码器、矩阵轮廓和Transformer架构的无监督损伤评估流程；3）在澳大利亚和日本进行多站点现场试验，覆盖多种桥型、交通条件和环境设置，验证完整工作流。

Result: 代理模型实现毫秒级推理，替代计算密集的全阶分析；优化后的检测车和信号处理链可最大化桥梁信息产出并抑制噪声；无监督损伤评估流程得到开发与验证；多站点现场试验覆盖多种桥型、交通和环境，完成工作流验证。

Conclusion: 该车辆集成数字孪生框架统一了物理建模与机器学习，为桥梁和道路状态的连续监测提供了可扩展方案，并通过多国现场试验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Ageing bridge infrastructure is a growing global concern, yet conventional Structural Health Monitoring (SHM) systems are costly and difficult to scale, and routine visual inspections remain subjective. Drive-by, or indirect, bridge inspection, in which a sensorised vehicle recovers structural information from vehicle-bridge interaction (VBI) and vehicle-road interaction (VRI) responses, offers a scalable alternative. However, key challenges remain unresolved, including separating bridge responses from road roughness, detecting damage under normal traffic, and generalising across diverse bridge types. This paper presents a vehicle-integrated digital twin framework that unifies physics-based modelling and machine learning for continuous monitoring of bridge and road conditions. The framework comprises three pillars. First, surrogate models of VBI and VRI are constructed using a Fourier Neural Operator that learns function-to-function mappings from operating conditions to vehicle responses. Trained on both simulated and field data, these surrogates deliver millisecond-scale inference, replacing computationally intensive full-order analyses. Second, the design of a custom electric inspection vehicle, its sensor layout, and signal processing chain are optimised through Bayesian optimisation to maximise bridge information yield while suppressing road and vehicle noise. Unsupervised damage-assessment pipelines based on adversarial autoencoders, matrix profiles, and transformer architectures have been developed and validated to process the resulting vehicle data. Third, the complete workflow is validated through coordinated multi-site field trials in Australia and Japan, covering a range of bridge types, traffic conditions, and environmental settings.

</details>


### [205] [Ask the Expert: LLM-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09337)
*Fernando Martinez,Abhishek Satyam,Tao Li,Junaid Farooq,Ying Wang,Juntao Chen*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Ask the Expert训练框架，在训练时用LLM总结困难状态并间歇性提供主机级防御建议，转化为分层奖励塑造辅助PPO，部署时无需LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于策略的强化学习在自主网络防御中样本效率低，需在延迟、部分观测和大动作空间下响应；LLM虽能语义推理安全状态，但高延迟和信任假设阻碍在线部署。

Method: 该框架先总结困难网络防御状态，通过受限动作接口间歇查询LLM获取主机级防御建议，再将建议转化为分层奖励塑造，用于PPO训练；训练后丢弃LLM，部署为纯RL策略。

Result: 在TTCP CAGE CC1和CC2及两种攻击者类型上，该方法相比PPO提高了样本效率，优于评估的PBRS基线，同时保持最强的终端平均性能，且部署时无需LLM依赖。

Conclusion: 非对称设计有效结合LLM训练期指导与RL部署效率，提升自主网络防御的样本效率和最终性能。

Abstract: Policy-based reinforcement learning (RL) approaches have produced promising results for autonomous cyber defense; however, they are sample-inefficient in settings where defenders must respond under delayed, partial observations with actions from large action spaces. While large language models (LLMs) may reason semantically about security state space, high latency and trust assumptions prevent attractive in-line deployment models. We introduce Ask the Expert, a training-time guidance framework which first summarizes hard cyber-defense states, then intermittently queries an LLM for host-level defensive recommendations via a constrained action interface, and finally transforms those recommendations into tiered reward shaping for use with PPO. Because the LLM is discarded after training, deployment is a pure RL policy. Across TTCP CAGE CC1 and CC2 and both attacker types, this asymmetric design improves sample efficiency over PPO and outperforms the evaluated potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) baselines, while retaining the strongest terminal mean and requiring no LLM dependency at deployment time.

</details>


### [206] [Secure-CUA: Controlling Untrusted Influence in Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09469)
*Sarthak Choudhary,Mihai Christodorescu,Ashish Hooda,Somesh Jha,Tongxin Li,Damien Octeau*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Secure-CUA，通过动作事务机制防御不可信内容注入攻击，在WebArena上以轻微效用损失实现安全执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用代理（CUA）在图形界面中操作时，可信控件与不可信内容混合，攻击者可通过嵌入指令或视觉误导改变代理动作或重定向命令目标，现有方法缺乏对决策与执行的双重安全保证。

Method: 形式化CUA的安全需求，提出理想执行模型；实例化Secure-CUA：每步在访问不可信内容前提交显式动作事务，固定查询与响应使用方式，掩码不可信区域，隔离查询模型求值生成动作，并在掩码界面上定位目标。

Result: 在400个WebArena任务、3个前沿模型、5个种子共6000条轨迹上，Secure-CUA平均任务成功率为53.55%，对比Vanilla-CUA的55.12%和CaMeL-CUA的13.17%。

Conclusion: Secure-CUA在模型假设下设计安全，且每步生成新事务可适应界面变化，保持高任务效用，仅比无防御基线低约1.6个百分点。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) perform tasks across applications (such as desktops, mobile apps, and web browsers) by observing graphical interfaces and issuing commands such as clicks and keystrokes. These interfaces combine trusted controls and content with untrusted content needed for legitimate tasks. An adversary controlling this untrusted content can embed instructions or misleading visual cues to change the agent's intended action or redirect its commands to the wrong interface target. We formalize security requirements for both the agent's decisions and their execution through GUI commands. In an ideal execution model, we show that enforcing both requirements at each step protects execution traces.
  We instantiate this model in Secure-CUA, our system for secure CUA execution. Its key idea is to commit to an explicit per-action program, called an $\textit{action transaction}$, before accessing untrusted content. Each transaction fixes its queries to untrusted content and the permitted uses of their responses. The system masks untrusted regions and evaluates each transaction to produce the next action, using an isolated query model to answer its queries. It then locates the intended interface target using the masked interface. Under the model's assumptions, Secure-CUA is secure by design, while generating a new transaction at each step helps maintain high task utility by adapting to changing interfaces.
  We evaluate Secure-CUA under benign conditions on 400 WebArena tasks using three frontier models across $5$ seeds, yielding $6,000$ execution traces. Secure-CUA achieves an average task success rate of $53.55\%$, compared with $55.12\%$ for Vanilla-CUA and $13.17\%$ for CaMeL-CUA.

</details>


### [207] [Constitution-Guided Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09552)
*Toluwani Aremu,Samuele Poppi,Nils Lukas*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Constitution-Guided Watermarking框架，按请求需求从自然语言原则中选取合适的文本水印权衡配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印需在强度、文本质量与抗编辑性/可伪造性间权衡，固定配置难以兼顾不同请求需求，可能牺牲质量或鲁棒性。

Method: 离线阶段用预训练推理代理依据宪法规则与水印实现，通过经验反馈迭代优化规则特定配置；部署时由独立监视器匹配适用规则并检索策略（含豁免），不改动服务模型，支持离线并行优化与配置-评估证据绑定。

Result: 以KGW和五规则宪法进行概念验证，框架能响应提供方优先级；在鲁棒性优先请求上，改写后检测率比固定配置提升最多14个百分点，同时在0.1%名义假阳性率下总体质量和干净检测匹配或超过所有基线。

Conclusion: 该框架实现灵活、可适配且可审计的水印部署，在满足不同请求权衡需求的同时提升鲁棒性并保持质量与检测性能。

Abstract: Watermarking enables language model providers to identify text generated by their models. However, its desired properties can conflict (\ie~stronger watermark signals can degrade text quality), while designs that resist editing may also facilitate forgery. Providers address these trade-offs by choosing configurations that balance competing objectives or prioritize particular properties. Either approach imposes a shared operating point on requests with different requirements, potentially sacrificing quality where wording preservation matters or robustness where reliable attribution is essential. To allow flexible and adaptable designs, we introduce \emph{Constitution-Guided Watermarking}, a framework that selects request-appropriate trade-offs from provider requirements, listed as natural-language principles. \emph{Offline}, a pretrained reasoning agent examines constitutional rules alongside watermark implementations and iteratively refines rule-specific configurations using empirical feedback. \emph{At deployment}, a separate monitor identifies applicable rules and retrieves the corresponding policy, including watermarking exemptions, without modifying the serving model. Furthermore, our framework supports offline parallel optimization and refinement of rule-specific configurations based on evolving provider requirements without affecting deployment, and binds each deployed configuration to its evaluation evidence, making deployment decisions auditable. In a proof-of-concept evaluation using KGW and a five-rule constitution, our framework selects configurations responsive to provider priorities and improves post-paraphrase detection on robustness-prioritized requests by up to $14$ percentage points over fixed configurations, while matching or exceeding all baselines in aggregate quality and clean detection at a nominal $0.1\%$ false-positive rate.

</details>


### [208] [How Fragile Is On-Device Language Model Safety? Localizing Safety-Critical Parameters for Sparse Fault Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09000)
*Muhammad Zeeshan Karamat,Christiana Chamon Garcia*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 研究发现LLaMA-2-7B-Chat的安全性行为高度集中于稀疏参数子集，仅修改MLP的down_proj中0.19%权重即可在保持通用能力的同时大幅提升攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型日益部署于资源受限和端侧平台乃至智能体系统，本地存储的模型参数完整性成为重要安全关切，需探究安全性敏感行为是否集中于稀疏参数子集以形成可分析的缩减故障面。

Method: 采用两种互补的定位方法：低秩安全关联子空间分析与参数级安全-效用重要性过滤，分析LLaMA-2-7B-Chat网络中安全敏感性的分布。

Result: 两种方法均揭示网络内安全敏感性高度不均匀，MLP的down_proj始终是最突出的安全敏感组件，o_proj贡献较小；仅修改down_proj中0.19%的模型权重即可达到53% Basic ASR与56% GCG ASR，而tinyBenchmarks准确率仍为51.6%，对照未修改基线为52.2%。

Conclusion: 结果支持面向资源受限、端侧及智能体部署场景下语言模型的定向故障分析与选择性完整性保护。

Abstract: As small language models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed on resource-constrained and on-device platforms, including as components of agentic systems, the integrity of locally stored model parameters becomes an important safety concern. We investigate whether safety-sensitive behavior in LLaMA-2-7B-Chat is concentrated within a sparse subset of parameters, creating a reduced fault surface for targeted analysis. We study two complementary localization methods: low-rank safety-associated subspace analysis and parameter-level safety--utility importance filtering. Both approaches reveal highly non-uniform safety sensitivity across the network, with the MLP down_proj consistently emerging as a prominent safety-sensitive component and o_proj providing a smaller contribution. Using parameter-level localization, modifying only 0.19% of model weights in down_proj yields 53% Basic ASR and 56% GCG ASR, while tinyBenchmarks accuracy remains at 51.6% compared with a 52.2% unmodified baseline. These results motivate targeted fault analysis and selective integrity protection for language models deployed in resource-constrained, on-device, and agentic settings.

</details>


### [209] [Sigma-Hunter: A Domain-Specific Language Model for Threat Hunting and Detection Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09007)
*Kemal Davaslioglu,Sastry Kompella*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Sigma-Hunter，一种领域适应的LLM，用于分析师辅助生成Sigma规则和威胁狩猎，在语法、字段一致性和语义判断上优于通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用LLM在生成检测规则时存在格式错误、日志源不匹配、字段不支持或逻辑过宽等问题，且离线环境无法使用托管模型服务。

Method: 从3,635条经验证的Sigma规则构建指令微调数据集，扩展为7,663个问答和推理示例，按规则划分训练/验证/测试集避免泄漏；使用LoRA微调7B Mistral和Phi-4模型，评估语法、近似字段一致性和语义判断（检测逻辑、完整性、选择性、日志源对齐）。

Result: Sigma-Hunter-Mistral总体得分8.17，最强通用基线7.88，未微调Mistral仅4.61；领域适应使7B模型与更大通用模型竞争，语法有效性是语义质量的弱代理。

Conclusion: 领域适应可提升小模型在结构化任务上的性能，且模型可本地运行，适合离线检测工程环境。

Abstract: Detection engineers must translate threat reports, forensic observations, and hunt hypotheses into precise, testable rules. General-purpose large language models (LLMs) can draft such rules, but often produce invalid YAML, incorrect log sources, unsupported fields, or overly broad detection logic. This paper presents \emph{Sigma-Hunter}, a domain-adapted LLM for analyst-assistive Sigma rule generation and threat hunting. We build an instruction-tuning dataset from 3,635 validated open-source Sigma rules, expanded into 7,663 question-answer and analyst-reasoning examples. Each source rule is assigned to a single train, validation, or test partition before this expansion, so no rule leaks across splits. We fine-tune a 7B Mistral model and a Phi-4 model with LoRA and score held-out rule generations on syntax, approximate field consistency, and a semantic judgment of detection logic, completeness, selectivity, and log-source alignment. Sigma-Hunter-Mistral scores 8.17 overall, against 7.88 for the strongest general-purpose baseline and 4.61 for untuned Mistral. Two findings stand out: domain adaptation enables a compact 7B model to perform competitively with larger general-purpose models on this structured task, and syntactic validity is a weak proxy for semantic rule quality, as several baselines emit well-formed YAML carrying weak detection logic. The adapted models run locally, which suits detection engineering in disconnected environments where analysts cannot reach hosted model services.

</details>


### [210] [Automotive Hardware Attacks: An Architect's Guide to TARA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09697)
*Jakub Breier,Xiaolu Hou*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 论文指出ISO/SAE 21434的TARA对硬件攻击处理不一致，提出覆盖侧信道、故障注入及调试接口滥用的硬件感知扩展方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准中三种攻击可行性方法对需物理访问的路径评级存在构造性偏差，导致实现级硬件攻击评估不一致，需硬件感知的TARA扩展。

Method: 提出硬件感知TARA扩展，包括车辆生命周期物理访问攻击者模型、攻击潜力因素的路径支配关系、H0-H2三级硬件相关性门及决策规则、以及硬件步骤无实现特定抵抗证据前不得计分的评级规则；应用于参考网关架构并在十项已发表汽车组件攻击上检验结构输入。

Result: 十项研究中七项演示攻击涉及调试、编程、启动、诊断或更新机制，仅四项涉及密码计算攻击；路径支配关系可识别简单路径使复杂路径无关，门控与评级规则明确了抵抗论证、文档或测试要求。

Conclusion: 该扩展弥补了ISO/SAE 21434对硬件攻击评级的不一致，通过攻击者模型、路径支配、相关性门和证据驱动评级，为车辆硬件攻击提供一致且可验证的TARA方法。

Abstract: Threat analysis and risk assessment (TARA) according to ISO/SAE 21434 treats implementation-level hardware attacks inconsistently. We show that two of the three attack-feasibility approaches exemplified in the standard rate every attack path that requires physical access as very low by construction, independent of the implementation, while the third can assign the same path any of the four ratings, depending on the assumed state of attacker knowledge and on the attacker profile. This paper presents a hardware-aware extension of the TARA that covers side-channel analysis (SCA), fault-injection attacks (FIA), and abuse of debug and test interfaces. It adds an attacker model for physical access over the vehicle lifecycle; a path-dominance relation on the attack-potential factors that identifies when a simpler path makes a sophisticated one irrelevant; a three-level hardware relevance gate (H0 to H2) with an explicit decision rule that fixes whether resistance of a hardware step may be argued, must be documented, or must be demonstrated by test; and a rating rule under which a hardware step receives no credit for implementation-specific resistance until the required evidence exists. The gate is positioned relative to cybersecurity assurance levels and targeted attack feasibility. The method is applied to a reference gateway architecture, and its structural inputs are examined on ten published attacks on automotive components. In seven of the ten studies, the demonstrated attack included a debug, programming, boot, diagnostic, or update mechanism, whereas only four studies included an attack on a cryptographic computation.

</details>


### [211] [Understanding and Mitigating Token-Pruning-Induced Vulnerabilities in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09703)
*Shuailong Wang,Xinyu Lyu,Shengming Yuan,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen,Lianli Gao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了Token-Pruning对视觉语言模型安全性的影响，发现不同剪枝策略会导致安全性能分化，并提出即插即用的安全感知剪枝机制以缓解剪枝引发的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Token-Pruning虽能加速视觉语言模型，但其安全影响尚未被充分探索，尤其是不同剪枝策略如何重塑模型安全行为以及能否在不牺牲加速的前提下增强安全性。

Method: 通过全面安全评估揭示剪枝策略的安全影响差异，识别出“剪枝诱导恶意放大”机制，并提出推理时即插即用的安全感知剪枝（SAP），包含识别恶意锚点、恢复被剪枝的良性token、重新分配注意力三个步骤。

Result: 在三个安全基准和四个效用基准上的实验表明，SAP能将攻击成功率（ASR）降低最多62%，同时不损害效率或效用。

Conclusion: SAP有效缓解了剪枝引发的安全漏洞，为Token-Pruning的安全应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Token-Pruning accelerates Vision-Language Models by removing redundant visual tokens, yet its safety implications remain underexplored. In this work, we present the first comprehensive safety evaluation of Token-Pruning mechanisms and find that: most pruning strategies significantly degrade safety as pruning ratios increase, whereas Query-based Compression shows the opposite, with extreme pruning (up to 99.8%), unexpectedly improves model safety. This sharp contrast prompts a key question: How do different Token-Pruning strategies reshape model safety behavior, and is it possible to enhance safety without sacrificing acceleration? To answer this, we identify an unrecognized mechanism, termed Pruning-Induced Malicious Amplification, where removal of background tokens triggers a side effect: forcing the model's attention to collapse onto a few retained malicious anchors within the foreground, inadvertently amplifying their toxic semantics under jailbreak. To address that, we propose an inference-time and plug-and-play Safety-Aware Pruning (SAP) mechanism that counteracts such dominance via three steps: (1) identifying malicious anchors, (2) restoring pruned benign tokens, and (3) reallocating excessive attention from malicious anchors to benign tokens. Extensive experiments across three safety and four utility benchmarks demonstrate that SAP mitigates pruning-induced vulnerabilities, i.e., reducing ASR by up to 62%, without compromising efficiency or utility.

</details>


### [212] [Black-Box Adversarial Patch Attacks on VLAs via Ancestor VLM Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09708)
*Xiaoyi Pang,Haoyue Feng,Quanxin Shou,Yikun Miao,Zhengyang Yan,Song Guo*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文研究仅利用VLA的祖先VLM生成对抗补丁来攻击已部署的VLA，揭示VLA继承视觉语言基础能力时也继承了对抗脆弱性，并分析其边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA对抗攻击多假设白盒或使用代理VLA，不符合真实部署场景；而VLA通常从公开预训练VLM适配而来，继承视觉感知和指令条件化 grounding 能力，因此探索能否仅用祖先VLM攻击已部署VLA。

Method: 提出三种对抗补丁攻击：视觉破坏攻击（通过相对和绝对项破坏投影视觉token）、指令 grounding 语义证据抑制攻击（移除指令 grounding 概念所需的视觉证据）、联合攻击（两阶段课程统一两种目标）；在仿真和静态真实图像上跨不同VLA家族实验。

Result: 在祖先VLM上优化的补丁导致VLA任务成功率大幅下降，证明VLA继承对抗脆弱性；效果不均匀：在需要精确指令 grounding 定位的任务上最强，在适配重写共享视觉语义表征或动作头迭代平滑扰动的策略上几乎消失。

Conclusion: 刻画了脆弱性继承的边界条件并分析继承效应成立或失败的原因，推进了对VLA相关系统安全性的理解。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical physical environments, yet their adversarial robustness remains poorly understood. Existing attacks typically assume white-box access or rely on surrogate VLAs, which rarely holds in real-world deployments. Our key insight is that most VLAs are adapted from a publicly released pretrained vision-language model (VLM), inheriting two capabilities essential for action generation: visual perception and instruction-conditioned grounding. Therefore, this paper explores a previously unaddressed question: can an adversary attack deployed VLAs using only their ancestor VLMs? To this end, we propose three adversarial patch attacks that disrupt the inherited capabilities: a vision disruption attack that corrupts the projected visual tokens through relative and absolute terms, an instruction-grounded semantic evidence suppression attack that removes the visual evidence required for instruction-grounded concepts, and a joint attack that unifies both objectives under a two-phase curriculum. Experiments across different VLA families on both simulation and static real-world images show that patches optimized on the ancestor VLM cause substantial degradations in VLA task success rates, demonstrating that VLAs inherit adversarial vulnerabilities alongside their foundational capabilities. This effect is not uniform: it is strongest on tasks that require precise instruction-grounded localization, and nearly vanishes on policies whose adaptation rewrites the shared visual-semantic representation or whose action head iteratively smooths perturbations away. By characterizing the boundary conditions of vulnerability inheritance and providing analysis of why the inheritance effect holds or fails, we advance the understanding of safety for VLA-involved systems.

</details>


### [213] [Trust a Few: The Weakest Assumptions a Protocol Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09730)
*Bhumika Mittal,Aalok Thakkar*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文回答了协议验证中一个开放问题：给定安全目标，协议所需的最弱信任假设是什么？作者提出基于最小攻击的停止集方法，并给出计算算法与复杂度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协议验证器在给定信任假设下检查安全目标，但不指出目标实际需要哪些假设。Rowe、Guttman和Liskov提出了寻找最弱假设的问题，但未解决。

Method: 将违反目标的运行视为攻击，能阻止攻击的假设集合为停止集。最弱信任是阻止所有最小攻击的最小停止集。允许析取假设时答案唯一。利用假设与目标间的Galois连接解释结构。通过循环调用验证器：询问候选是否充分、记录停止各攻击的假设、重算候选。

Result: 判定至多k个假设的信任是否充分是NP-hard。使用自研有界分析器和CPSA，该循环为十个协议及其变体的18个目标找到最弱信任，且与穷举所有信任的验证结果一致。发现Kerberos PKINIT修复模型中服务器认证所需但客户端认证服务器不需要的假设，以及Needham-Schroeder有界模型中满足目标的信道假设。

Conclusion: 最弱信任可精确刻画为阻止所有最小攻击的最小停止集；当允许析取假设时答案唯一。该方法能自动计算最弱信任，并揭示协议认证中不必要的假设和必要信道假设。

Abstract: Protocol verifiers check whether a protocol meets a security goal under stated trust assumptions, such as that a key is never leaked, a value is fresh, or a channel is authentic. They do not say which of those assumptions the goal needs. Rowe, Guttman and Liskov asked for the weakest assumptions under which a protocol achieves a goal and left the question open. We answer it for assumptions about keys, values and channels. Call a run that violates the goal an attack, and the assumptions that would rule it out its stopping set. The least a protocol must trust to meet a goal is exactly the set of minimal ways to stop all of its minimal attacks. Several such sets may exist. The answer becomes unique once either/or assumptions are allowed, and it is a single set exactly when every minimal attack is stopped by a single assumption. A Galois connection between assumptions and goals explains why: a goal may end in "or", but its hypothesis may not. The same structure gives the trust needed by a conjunction of goals and an exact condition under which composed protocols need no extra trust. To compute the answer, a loop asks a verifier whether a candidate suffices, records what stops each attack it reports, and recomputes the candidates. Deciding whether some trust of at most k assumptions suffices is NP-hard. With a bounded analyser of our own and with CPSA, the loop finds the weakest trust for 18 goals over ten protocols and their variants, and on each it agrees with evaluating every trust using the same verifier. The answers include an assumption that our model of the adopted fix of Kerberos PKINIT states but the client's authentication of the server does not need, and channel assumptions under which Needham-Schroeder meets its goal in a bounded model.

</details>


### [214] [Not Every Call Needs a Frontier Model: Per-Call-Site Evaluation of Small Language Models in a Deployed Agentic Home-Automation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09021)
*Panagiotis Kasnesis,Christos Chatzigeorgiou,Lazaros Toumanidis,Amalia Contiero Syropoulou*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文评估了9个模型在已部署家庭自动化框架（Wactorz）的五个不同LLM调用点上的表现，发现能力并非在所有调用点上都随模型规模单调提升，且聚合准确率会掩盖特定于执行环节的安全失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际系统中，单个为最难任务选择的模型被用于所有调用点，但不同调用点的难度相差一个数量级，这种‘一刀切’策略可能并非最优，甚至带来安全风险。

Method: 使用Wactorz的未修改生产提示词和两个真实Home Assistant安装，在五个调用点（意图路由、动作分类、设备注册表语言接地、多智能体流水线规划、Python代码生成）上评估9个模型（0.8B到前沿托管模型），共280个案例、2520次评分调用，并进行配对测试。

Result: 最佳本地模型在五个调用点中的四个上与两个托管模型统计上不可区分；仅代码生成存在显著差异（对小托管模型p=0.039，对前沿模型p=0.002）。一个4B模型在接地执行上比其2B版本更差。聚合准确率掩盖了执行环节的安全失败：Gemma4 E2B对87.2%的非所属设备请求执行动作，而另一模型拒绝所有请求。将每个调用点路由到最佳本地模型达到91.8%准确率，而全托管为95.4%且无每次调用成本。在真实部署中，仅托管两个生成式调用点与托管全部调用点效果相同（39/43 vs 39/43），但成本仅28%，且基准预测的执行差距在26个案例中仅出现1例。

Conclusion: 不应假设模型能力在所有调用点上一致排序，也不应认为更大模型总是更好；按调用点选择最佳本地模型可接近全托管性能，且仅托管生成式调用点可大幅降低成本，但需警惕执行环节的安全失败。

Abstract: An agentic system issues several structurally different kinds of LLM calls. It routes intent, classifies actions, grounds language in a device registry, plans multi-agent pipelines and writes the Python code those pipelines run. The difficulty of these call sites varies by an order of magnitude, yet in practice a single model, chosen for the hardest site, serves all of them. In this work, we evaluate 9 models from 0.8B to a frontier hosted model across the five call sites of a deployed open-source home-automation framework (Wactorz), using its unmodified production prompts and two real Home Assistant installations (280 cases, 2520 scored calls). We find that capability is not ordered the same way at every site, and that larger models are not uniformly better: one 4B model is worse than its 2B sibling at grounded actuation. Paired testing shows the best local model to be statistically indistinguishable from both hosted models at four of five sites. Only code generation separates them, against a small hosted model (p = 0.039) as well as a frontier one (p = 0.002). Aggregate accuracy also hides a safety failure specific to actuation, where small models resolve the accuracy/refusal trade-off in degenerate ways: one model (Gemma4 E2B) actuates on 87.2% of requests for devices the site does not own, while another refuses every request it receives. Routing each site to its best local model reaches 91.8% against 95.4% at no per-call cost. In a live deployment judged by a user, hosting only the two generative sites matches hosting everything (39/43 against 39/43) for 28% of the spend, and the actuation gap the benchmark predicted appears as exactly one case in twenty-six. Benchmark, harness and all records are released at https://github.com/waldiez/slm-callsite-eval.

</details>


### [215] [Formal Runtime Verification for Tool-Using LLM Agents: An Offline Same-Benchmark Study on AgentDojo and STAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09793)
*Nikolaos Kekatos,Stylianos Basagiannis,Marinelio Chintri,Alexios Lekidis,Tom Nianios,Ioannis Seitoglou,Anastasios Temperekidis,Panagiotis Katsaros*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 评估MFOTL对工具型LLM代理轨迹的离线监控效果，指出现有基准缺乏可信历史上下文，并提出可执行轨迹模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型LLM代理的护栏多为应用专用规则，多步、数据依赖的安全策略难以声明、审计与复用，需探索声明式时序监控方案。

Method: 使用未修改的MonPoly监控器离线回放AgentDojo、STAC、R-Judge的已记录轨迹，应用五条通用MFOTL义务，并测试溯源感知策略及其可攻击性。

Result: 五条义务标记71.8% STAC攻击链和70.1%成功AgentDojo攻击，但误报29.3%良性运行；溯源策略判别更好，但一行植入可绕过94-99%本应标记的运行；将溯源绑定到产生它的查找可消除该规避且不损失检测或增加误报。

Conclusion: 形式化时序监控仅在轨迹暴露可信历史时才有价值，当前基准距此尚远，故提出十二字段的执行就绪轨迹模式。

Abstract: Guardrails for tool-using LLM agents are usually application-specific rules, which makes multi-step, data-dependent safety policies hard to specify, audit and reuse. As a declarative alternative, we evaluate metric first-order temporal logic (MFOTL), replaying the recorded trajectories that AgentDojo, STAC and R-Judge already ship through the unmodified MonPoly monitor, offline and without running an agent. On these corpora, five generic obligations flag 71.8% of STAC attack chains and 70.1% of successful AgentDojo attacks, but also fire on 29.3% of benign runs. This imprecision stems from the corpora rather than the logic: they rarely record approvals and never record timestamps, so history-dependent obligations reduce to detecting risky action types. Where the trace does carry relational context, provenance-aware policies discriminate better; that context, however, is itself attackable, and one planted line defeats a naive provenance check on 94-99% of the runs it would otherwise flag. Binding provenance to the lookup that produced it closes this evasion at no cost in detection or benign firing. Taken together, these results show that formal temporal monitoring adds value exactly when the trace exposes trustworthy history. We therefore quantify how far current benchmarks are from that point and propose a twelve-field enforcement-ready trace schema.

</details>


### [216] [Shared-Roadmap Generation and Evaluator for Multi-Agent Path Planning Using Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09034)
*Brandon Ho,Nikola Rogers,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种可扩展的异构图神经网络框架，用于自动生成和评估共享多智能体路网图，通过异构图表示与专家轨迹训练剪枝冗余节点，生成紧凑且协调感知的路网图，实验显示密度路网下运行时和图规模至少减少40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续环境中的多智能体路径规划常依赖路网图以平衡安全性与搜索效率，但传统路网图生成方法（如栅格或标准采样）难以兼顾图密度与找到可行高质量解的概率，存在权衡问题。

Method: 将路标点、智能体位置和任务位置表示为异构图中的不同节点，使模型能推理全局连通性和智能体间交互；基于从专家求解器轨迹中聚合收集的占用密度图进行训练，让GNN学习识别关键兴趣点并剪枝冗余节点和边，从而生成紧凑、协调感知、对任务排列不变且可复用于多智能体取送任务的路网图。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架能减少规划开销并可能找到更优解，对于密集路网图，运行时和图规模至少减少40%。

Conclusion: 该异构图神经网络框架可自动生成和评估共享多智能体路网图，有效降低规划负担并提升解质量，在密集场景下显著压缩图规模与运行时间。

Abstract: Multi-agent path planning (MAPP) in continuous environments often relies on roadmaps to balance safety and search efficiency. However, traditional roadmap generation methods, such as lattice grids or standard sampling-based approaches, frequently face a trade-off between graph density and the likelihood of finding feasible, high-quality solutions. In this paper, we propose a scalable heterogeneous Graph Neural Network (GNN) framework for the automated generation and evaluation of shared multi-agent roadmaps. Our model covers the representation of waypoints, agent locations, and task locations as distinct nodes in a heterogeneous graph, allowing it to reason over global connectivity and inter-agent interactions. By training on occupation density maps aggregated and collected from expert solver trajectories, the GNN learns to identify critical points of interest and prune redundant nodes and edges. This process produces a compact, coordination-aware roadmap that is invariant to task permutations and is reusable for multi-agent pick and delivery tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework can reduce planning effort and can potentially find better solutions, reaching at least 40% reduction in runtime and in graph size for dense roadmaps.

</details>


### [217] [Hierarchical Security Monitoring for Edge-IoT: A Formal Methods Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09817)
*Nikolaos Kekatos,Marinelio Chintri,Panagiotis Katsaros,Alexios Lekidis,Tom Nianios,Ioannis Seitoglou,Stylianos Basagiannis*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出基于形式化运行时验证的轻量级分层边缘物联网安全监控框架，在受限资源下检测协同多设备攻击，并将非对称成本转向防御方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘物联网网络弹性本质是经济问题：集中式云监控带宽成本过高，纯边缘本地监控便宜但对协同多设备攻击盲视，使攻击者占据非对称优势。

Method: 边缘设备运行轻量级TeSSLa流规约，按聚合窗口输出四值裁决，单事件成本亚微秒级；网关运行参数化一阶MonPoly监控器，处理各设备裁决流，单裁决成本微秒级；边缘到网关上行每节点每窗口约传一个布尔值。

Result: 在容器主机测试床上针对缓冲区溢出、时间欺骗、拒绝服务及混合高级持续性威胁四类攻击，横跨正常与攻击节点进行评估，网关层可检测单节点监控无法发现的协同攻击模式，每警报携带包含设备、监控层和触发谓词的见证集。

Conclusion: 该框架在量化成本下占据实用中间地带，能够在有限资源下提升对协同攻击的检测覆盖，并将非对称成本平衡转向防御方，同时提供可审计的决策记录。

Abstract: Cyber resiliency in edge-IoT deployments is fundamentally an economic problem: detection must keep critical processes operating under attack, but defender resources (compute, bandwidth, operator attention) are bounded. Centralised cloud monitoring offers expressive cross-device detection at prohibitive bandwidth cost; purely edge-local monitoring is cheap but blind to coordinated multi-device attacks where the asymmetric balance favours the attacker. We propose a lightweight hierarchical security-monitoring framework, built on formal runtime-verification methods, that occupies the practical middle ground at quantified cost. Each edge device runs a lightweight TeSSLa stream specification (size, payload validity, rate, and timestamp-drift predicates) that emits a four-valued verdict per aggregation window at sub-microsecond per-event cost; the gateway runs a parametric first-order MonPoly monitor over the per-device verdict streams at microsecond-scale per-verdict cost. The edge-to-gateway uplink carries roughly one Boolean per aggregation window per node, orders of magnitude smaller than the raw packet stream. The gateway tier detects coordinated attack patterns that no single-node monitor can see, shifting the asymmetric cost balance toward the defender. Every alert carries a witness set naming the device, the monitor tier, and the predicate that fired, providing an auditable record of the decision. We evaluate the framework on a container-host testbed spanning nominal and attacker nodes across four attack classes (buffer overflow, time spoofing, denial-of-service, and mixed advanced-persistent-threat patterns), and describe the edge- and gateway-tier specifications together with the cost-versus-coverage trade-off as monitor levels are added.

</details>


### [218] [Removing Information Content Does Not Certify Tamper Resistance in Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09004)
*Domenic Rosati,Alessa Carbo,Ali Dadsetan,Hong Huang,Matthew Young,Subhabrata Majumdar,Frank Rudzicz,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 仅靠发布时的互信息无法普遍证明开放权重模型对微调攻击的抵抗性，还需约束攻击动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究去除有害信息是否能使开放权重模型抵御微调攻击，并检验互信息作为认证指标的充分性。

Method: 分析保持函数的重参数化对梯度下降几何的影响，应用于训练数据过滤下的权重-数据互信息和能力移除下的标签-表示互信息，并进行受控实验。
