  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者常用RankMe等谱统计量监测LLM后训练中的表示健康度，并假设表示退化时秩会下降。论文旨在检验该假设是否安全，以及谱监测能否有效预警退化。

Method: 以Qwen3-0.6B为对象，在四种退化模式和三个随机种子下进行受控研究；比较原始与中心化RankMe、协方差有效秩、k95等统计量；区分RankMe（奇异值归一化）与协方差有效秩（特征值归一化）；并在预训练模型中间层状态上分析大规模激活的影响；最后测试双向、多通道序贯监测器，采用独立校准与测试数据，进行预注册的共享前缀、留一种子评估。

Result: 数据重复使留出损失相对健康恶化75%，同时提升原始与中心化RankMe（后者变化达13.5个合并标准差），协方差有效秩升至健康值近两倍；学习率错误配置降低中心化RankMe和k95，但非中心化RankMe在不同种子间不一致。在预训练模型原始中间层状态上，大规模激活使协方差有效秩接近1/d，而RankMe保留可用范围。双向监测器在分叉后10-60步内检测到全部三种损害机制，并能通过触发方向区分分散与降秩损害，但从不早于留出探测损失，且用两个种子校准时对留出健康种子产生误报。

Conclusion: 谱监测可诊断失效机制，但不会比留出损失更早预警；方向性是机制-统计量对的属性，无法仅通过重校准修正；有效性声明需要留出健康数据。

Abstract: Post-training adapts language models in non-stationary environments. Practitioners monitor representation health with RankMe and related spectral statistics, often assuming that rank falls when representations degrade. We show that this assumption is unsafe for LLM post-training. In a controlled study of Qwen3-0.6B with four degradation modes and three seeds, data duplication worsens held-out loss by 75% relative to healthy while increasing both original and centred RankMe; the latter changes by 13.5 pooled standard deviations. Covariance effective rank rises to nearly twice its healthy value. This failure is spectral dispersion rather than collapse, so a one-sided monitor rates the worst checkpoint as the healthiest. By contrast, a learning-rate misconfiguration lowers centred RankMe and k95, while uncentred RankMe is inconsistent across seeds. Direction is therefore a property of the regime-statistic pair and cannot be fixed by recalibration alone. We also distinguish two often-conflated statistics: RankMe normalises singular values, whereas covariance effective rank normalises eigenvalues. On raw intermediate-layer states in the pretrained model, massive activations pin the latter near 1 out of dimension d while RankMe retains usable range. We then test a two-sided, multichannel sequential monitor with separate calibration and test data. In a pre-registered shared-prefix, leave-one-seed-out evaluation, it detects all three damage regimes in every fold 10 to 60 steps after the fork and separates dispersion from downward-rank damage by firing direction. However, it never precedes held-out probe loss, and calibration with two seeds produces false alarms on the held-out healthy seed. Spectral monitoring can diagnose failure regimes, but it does not warn earlier than held-out loss, and validity claims require held-out healthy data.

</details>


### [326] [Before They Can Solve: Predicting Post-Training Coding-Agent Performance from Base Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10478)
*Tan Yu,Alexander Bukharin,Khushi Bhardwaj,Jennifer Williams,Zirui Liu,Jonathan Lingjie Li,Soumye Singhal,Joseph Jennings,Sanjeev Satheesh,Yash Jain,Ashish Vaswani,Venkat Krishna Srinivasan,Matthew Papakipos,Hyunwoo Kim,Jian Zhang,Oleksii Kuchaiev,Markus Kliegl,Mostofa Patwary,Mohammad Shoeybi,Bryan Catanzaro,Jonathan Cohen,Jiantao Jiao*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种通过成功后训练智能体轨迹作为前瞻信号来预测基础检查点是否值得昂贵后训练的方法，避免依赖基础模型从头驱动工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端pass@K测试不适合智能体编码场景，因为许多基础检查点无法可靠生成合规的工具调用以完成任务；单步或短视任务虽规避工具调用失败，却忽略了在多步工具使用中保持连贯状态的核心能力。

Method: 将成功后训练轨迹视为基础模型潜力的前瞻信号；重放每条轨迹并在每个代码变更步骤后重跑测试，识别决定性步骤（累积补丁首次使仓库从失败转为通过的步骤）；基于智能体轨迹的覆盖原则构建三种筛选器：决定性动作BPB、补丁MCQ、前缀条件pass@K。

Result: 在十对公开基础与后训练模型上，三种筛选器的排序与后训练SWE-bench Verified pass@1高度一致。

Conclusion: 仅需基准的成功轨迹和验证器，即可将未来智能体编码基准转化为基础模型评估，用于预测基础检查点的后训练价值。

Abstract: How can we predict which base checkpoint is worth an expensive round of agentic post-training? End-to-end pass@$K$ tests whether successful behavior already appears in a base model's distribution, but it is a poor fit for agentic coding: many base checkpoints cannot reliably produce the well-formed tool invocation required to complete a task end-to-end. Single-shot or short-horizon tasks avoid these tool-calling failures by collapsing a multi-step interaction into a fixed prompt and a single patch, but they sidestep the core capability we care about: maintaining coherent state over many tool-using steps as the repository evolves. To bridge this gap, we treat successful post-trained agent trajectories as a lookahead signal of base-model potential. Replaying each trajectory and rerunning tests after every code-changing step identifies the decisive step: the first step whose cumulative patch flips the repository from failing to passing, certifying that the recorded action solves the task given the prior context. Motivated by a coverage principle for agentic traces, we build three screens at this step that do not require a base checkpoint to drive the harness from a cold start: (i) Decisive-Action BPB (bits per byte) measures the probability mass on the certified action, (ii) Patch MCQ tests the checkpoint's choice between that action and alternatives rejected by the same verifier, and (iii) prefix-conditioned pass@$K$ evaluates support for functionally-correct generations and credits any continuation that the tests accept. Across ten pairs of public base and post-trained models, all three screens rank the cohort in close agreement with post-trained SWE-bench Verified pass@$1$. As our methods need only a benchmark's successful trajectories and its verifier, they can be applied to turn future agentic coding benchmarks into base-model evaluations.

</details>


### [327] [DSTNet: Dynamic Spectral Trajectory Network for Causal Multi-Horizon Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09654)
*Aashish Bohra,Lokendra Vishwakarm*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出DSTNet，一种基于因果动态谱轨迹的小波金融预测模型，在多个股票指数和黄金上优于持久性基准，但下游资产配置诊断未显示股票择时优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于小波的金融预测器仅用小波去噪，或在预测起点缩减为单一谱快照，且卷积通常为双边，会读取预测起点之后的未来信息，导致信息泄露。

Method: DSTNet将近期滤波器组幅值演化保留为因果动态谱轨迹，基于七个滞后技术指标、二十天回看窗口、单边Morlet派生滤波器组，并显式预热左边界瞬态。采用分解的尺度-时间谱Transformer分别沿时间轴和滤波器组轴注意力，学习门融合谱分支与CNN-BiLSTM，特定horizon门单次输出1、3、5、10天预测。在七个股票指数和黄金上，采用扩展窗口协议和未触碰的一年留出集，对比九种学习基线和随机游走持久性基准。

Result: 在MAE和MAPE下，持久性在32个序列-horizon单元中的29个为十个固定竞争者中最强，DSTNet是唯一在所有单元中低于持久性的模型，1天低0.7-0.9%，10天低3.4-4.5%。1天时配对检验支持DSTNet优于较弱学习基线，但与持久性和最强学习预测器相比不确定。下游配置诊断不支持七个指数中任何一个的股票择时优势。

Conclusion: DSTNet在点预测精度上一致优于持久性基准，但未能证明具有实际股票择时价值。

Abstract: Wavelet-based financial forecasters typically use the transform only to denoise, or reduce it to a single spectral snapshot at the forecast origin, and the convolution that produces the coefficients is usually bilateral, so it can read past the forecast origin. DSTNet instead retains the recent evolution of filter-bank magnitudes as a causal Dynamic Spectral Trajectory, built from seven trailing technical indicators over a twenty-day lookback with a one-sided Morlet-derived filter bank and an explicit burn-in for the left-boundary transient. A factorized Scale-Temporal Spectral Transformer attends along the time and filter-bank axes separately, a learned gate fuses the spectral branch with a CNN-BiLSTM, and horizon-specific gates emit one, three, five, and ten day forecasts in a single pass. We evaluate seven equity indices and gold under a common expanding-window protocol and an untouched one-year hold-out, against nine learned baselines and a random-walk persistence benchmark. Under MAE and MAPE, persistence is the strongest of the ten fixed competitors in 29 of the 32 series-horizon cells and DSTNet is the only model below it in every cell, by 0.7 to 0.9 percent at one day and 3.4 to 4.5 percent at ten days. At one day, paired testing favours DSTNet against the weaker learned baselines but is inconclusive against persistence and the strongest learned forecasters. A downstream allocation diagnostic does not support an equity-timing advantage on any of the seven indices.

</details>


### [328] [SoftSEEPS improves ML-based precipitation forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09752)
*Jost Arndt,Utku Isil,Noelia Otero,Rodrigo Almeida,Wojciech Samek,Jackie Ma*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出可微分近似SEEPS评分SoftSEEPS，用于直接训练降水预测机器学习模型，并在IMERG数据集上结合RMSE联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SEEPS评分不可微分，无法直接用于机器学习模型的训练，因此需要一种可微分的近似方法以实现降水预测的端到端训练。

Method: 开发SoftSEEPS作为SEEPS的可微分近似，在预训练低分辨率预报模型的潜在空间上训练降水解码器，并在IMERG数据集（0.1度分辨率）上进行测试，同时将SoftSEEPS与RMSE结合为联合目标函数。

Result: 在IMERG数据集上成功训练了降水解码器，联合目标函数在SoftSEEPS和RMSE指标上均实现了可接受的权衡。

Conclusion: SoftSEEPS使得机器学习模型能够直接优化降水预测，并且可以与RMSE联合使用，尽管存在轻微的指标折衷。

Abstract: In this paper we have developed a differentiable approximation of the well-known SEEPS score, which we name SoftSEEPS. This allows the training of a Machine Learning model to forecast precipitation directly. We test SoftSEEPS on the IMERG dataset (0.1 degree resolution) by training a decoder for precipitation on the latent space of a pre-trained low-resolution forecasting model. Combining SoftSEEPS and RMSE in a joint objective is possible with marginal trade-offs in either metric.

</details>


### [329] [Fluctuations of Nonlinear Observables in Mean Field Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09768)
*Arnaud Descours,Geoffrey Lacour*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 该论文研究了宽神经网络均值场训练动态中参数分布的非线性可观测量，建立了其中心极限定理并给出了协方差显式表达式，同时分析了可观测量能否恢复感兴趣的物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均值场极限通常关注参数经验分布本身，但实际关注的是参数分布的非线性可观测量，需要将均值场涨落传播到有限维非线性可观测量上，并判断所选观测是否包含识别目标量的信息。

Method: 在加权Sobolev空间中构造极限涨落过程，采用普通Fréchet可微性下的泛函Delta方法（避免对测度变量的Lions导数），推导可观测量的中心极限定理；在常数秩假设下，通过微分核包含关系证明目标量局部因子化通过观测泛函的充要条件。

Result: 得到了可观测量的中心极限定理，并在微分表示下给出了继承自均值场涨落理论的显式协方差公式；证明了目标量局部因子化通过观测泛函当且仅当观测微分核包含目标量微分核。

Conclusion: 该框架既能量化有限宽度下统计或物理意义可观测量的不确定性，也能评估所选观测是否包含识别目标量所需的信息。

Abstract: Mean field limits describe the training dynamics of wide neural networks through the evolution of the empirical distribution of their parameters. Although functional central limit theorems characterize the asymptotic fluctuations of this distribution, quantities of practical interest are typically nonlinear observables of the parameter distribution rather than the distribution itself. In this work, we show how these mean field fluctuations propagate to finite dimensional nonlinear observables for shallow neural networks trained by stochastic gradient descent. Working in the weighted Sobolev space in which the limiting fluctuation process is constructed, we apply a functional Delta method under ordinary Fr{é}chet differentiability, without requiring Lions derivatives with respect to the measure variable. We obtain a central limit theorem for the observables and, under a suitable representation of their differentials, an explicit covariance formula inherited from the underlying mean field fluctuation theory. We also study whether prescribed quantities of interest can be recovered from the selected observations. Under a constant rank assumption, we prove that a quantity of interest factors locally through the observation functional if and only if, throughout a neighborhood, the kernel of the differential of the observation is contained in that of the quantity of interest. Thus, a differential condition expressed directly in the ambient Sobolev space yields an exact nonlinear local factorization. These results provide a framework both for quantifying finite-width uncertainty on observable, statistically or physically meaningful quantities and for assessing whether the chosen observations contain the information required to identify them.

</details>


### [330] [A Proof-of-Concept Study of Weakly Supervised Labeling of Fine-Grained EEG Components for Artifact Attenuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09792)
*Lu Wang-Nöth,Hai Huang,Philipp Heiler,Shuqiong Wu,Liyun Zhang,Helmut Mayer*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出频域感知高维表征与多实例学习框架，在缺乏细粒度真值下检测并衰减多通道头皮EEG中的EMG伪迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG易受EMG伪迹干扰，盲源分离后仍存在混合源；现有方法受限于组件级真值稀缺，专家标注昂贵且主观，且无可靠的基于仿真的真值。

Method: 将分离组件展开为频率分辨的子组件，构建神经与肌肉活动更可分的表征空间；采用多实例学习，从epoch级弱标签学习各子组件的伪迹似然分数，进而支持细粒度检测与分数引导衰减。

Result: 在留出受试者上，框架学到有信息量的子组件分数，对紧咬下颌的伪迹相关频谱偏差降低最明显，抬眉中等，皱眉有限。

Conclusion: 频域展开结合多实例学习可在无细粒度真值条件下实现细粒度EMG伪迹检测与衰减，效果因伪迹类型而异。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is highly susceptible to electromyographic (EMG) artifacts, whose temporal heterogeneity and spatial-spectral overlap with neural activity can leave mixed sources after blind source separation. Existing artifact-removal methods are further limited by scarce reliable component-level ground truth: expert annotations are costly and subjective, while no established method provides realistic simulation-based ground truth for EMG contamination in multichannel scalp EEG. To address these limitations, we propose a framework combining a frequency-aware high-dimensional representation with Multi-Instance Learning. The representation unfolds separated components into frequency-resolved intra-components, creating a space in which mixed neural and muscular activity becomes more separable, while the weakly supervised learning formulation enables artifact-likelihood scores for individual intra-components to be learned from epoch-level labels without finer-grained ground truth. The resulting intra-component classifier supports fine-grained EMG artifact detection and score-guided attenuation. Experiments on held-out subjects show that the framework learns informative intra-component scores and reduces artifact-related spectral deviations most clearly for jaw tension, with moderate effects for raising eyebrows and limited effects for frowning.

</details>


### [331] [Stream-Based Active Learning with Cooperative Neural Networks for Data-Efficient Partial Inverse Design: An Automotive Glass Run Channel Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09848)
*Agung Nugraha,Hyerin Kwon,Heungjun Im,Gian Antariksa,Jihwan Lee*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出CoNN-AL框架，将流式主动学习与CoNN-DAE结合，实现数据高效的部分逆向设计，并在汽车玻璃导槽数据集上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程逆向设计中，每个标记样本需昂贵仿真，构建大数据集慢且成本高；部分逆向设计仅指定部分设计变量，需推断其余变量以达到目标性能。

Method: 在CoNN-DAE基础上加入基于流的主动学习；通过蒙特卡洛dropout估计预测不确定性，实时决定哪些候选样本值得标记，以将有限标记预算用于最具信息量的设计。

Result: 在超过90万模拟设计的汽车玻璃导槽数据集上，仅用20,000个主动选择标签（约训练池的2.3%），CoNN-AL在所有缺失变量水平上R²达0.967-0.982，接近使用更多数据训练的上界模型；在较难缺失变量水平上，比随机采样少30-40%标签即达R²≥0.95；在最难水平上，是唯一达到R²=0.98的策略。

Conclusion: CoNN-AL能显著减少标记需求，实现数据高效的部分逆向设计，并公开数据集以支持未来数据驱动设计研究。

Abstract: Inverse design in engineering often runs into a simple problem. Each labeled training sample must be produced through expensive simulation, so building a large dataset is slow and costly. This study addresses that problem for partial inverse design, where only some design variables are specified and the rest must be inferred to reach a target performance value. We propose CoNN-AL, a framework for data-efficient partial inverse design that adds stream-based active learning to the Cooperative Neural Network with Denoising Autoencoder (CoNN-DAE). The model estimates predictive uncertainty through Monte Carlo dropout and uses it to decide, in real time, which incoming candidate samples are worth labeling, so the limited labeling budget is spent on the most informative designs. We validate the framework on a real-world automotive glass run channel dataset of more than 900,000 unique simulated designs. With only 20,000 actively selected labels, about 2.3% of the training pool, CoNN-AL reaches R-squared values of 0.967 to 0.982 across all missing-variable levels, approaching the upper-bound models trained on far more data. It reaches R-squared of at least 0.95 with 30 to 40% fewer labels than random sampling at the more difficult missing-variable levels and, at the most challenging level, is the only strategy in this study to reach R-squared of 0.98. Together with this work, we publicly release the dataset to support future research on data-driven design.

</details>


### [332] [KGATE : a Knowledge Graph Embedding Training Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09927)
*Benjamin Loire,Galadriel Brière,Célia Brahimi,Antoine Toffano,Anaïs Baudot*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: KGATE是一个模块化知识图谱自编码器训练环境，支持构建完整编码器-解码器架构，兼顾可复现性与特征丰富性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱嵌入库多不支持完整自编码器，维护不足，超参数未文档化，跨库结果不可比。

Method: 基于PyTorch Geometric与TorchKGE构建Python库，将初始化器、编码器、解码器、损失、负采样和评估指标模块化，并内置防数据泄露预处理与训练流水线。

Result: 与六个现有KGE库基准测试表明，训练时间与最快库相当，同时提供更广泛功能集。

Conclusion: KGATE为知识图谱自编码器提供完整、可复现、模块化的训练环境，在效率与功能覆盖上优于现有库。

Abstract: Knowledge graph embedding (KGE) models encode the entities and relations of a knowledge graph into a low-dimensional latent space, enabling tasks such as classification or link prediction. Most KGE models follow an autoencoder architecture, in which an encoder projects the knowledge graph into the latent space and a decoder reconstruct it. Combining both encoder and decoder components is increasingly needed, yet existing libraries rarely support complete autoencoders, are often unmaintained, rely on undocumented default hyperparameters, and produce results that cannot be compared across libraries. Here we present KGATE (Knowledge Graph Autoencoder Training Environment), a modular Python library built on PyTorch Geometric and TorchKGE. KGATE lets users assemble initializers, encoders, decoders, losses, negative samplers, and evaluation metrics as building blocks, or plug in their own block. KGATE includes a preprocessing procedure that controls data leakage, a builtin training pipeline, and reproducibility by design. Benchmarks against six existing KGE libraries show that KGATE training time is comparable with the fastest libraries while offering a broader set of features.

</details>


### [333] [Force without transmission: a depth-induced rank collapse that no loss on the representation reopens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09958)
*Martin Hofmann,Patrick Mäder*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 研究训练中Transformer的秩坍缩能否通过附加损失项修复，发现修复取决于梯度路径是否畅通，而非损失项的强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明带限修正力的损失项能修复注意力坍缩，作者欲探究该损失项是否也能修复秩坍缩。

Method: 通过削弱跳跃连接使小型Transformer发生秩坍缩，对坍缩网络的副本施加不同强度的附加损失项，并分析任务梯度与附加项梯度在查询、键权重及各块中的传播路径。

Result: 没有任何附加损失项能修复坍缩，即使较强项推力约为任务梯度的十分之一；原因是任务梯度不再到达查询和键权重，附加项梯度在坍缩形成块之前已衰减。恢复跳跃连接（不改变任何权重）立即重开该路径，秩随之恢复但仅远高于坍缩尺度；高学习率爆发后路径保持开放且秩在无干预下恢复。由路径得出的预测能对恢复时间排序，但不能迁移到该致因。所有情况下秩恢复后损失仍高于健康网络。

Conclusion: 坍缩网络能否修复取决于梯度是否仍能到达必须改变的权重，而不是损失项推力有多强。

Abstract: Training can drive a transformer into a rank collapse: all token representations point in one direction, and learning stops. In a related collapse of attention, a loss term with a bounded corrective force repairs the network during the run. We ask whether such a term repairs rank collapse. We collapse small transformers by weakening their skip connection and treat copies of the collapsed network. No added loss term repaired the collapse, although the stronger kind pushed with about a tenth of the task gradient. The reason was the path, not the strength. The task gradient no longer reached the query and key weights, which decide where attention looks, and the added term's gradient faded before the blocks where the collapse forms. Restoring the skip connection, which changes no weight, reopened this path at once. The rank then recovered, but only far above the scale of collapse. After a burst of high learning rate the path stayed open and the rank recovered untreated. Registered predictions from the path ranked recovery times but did not transfer to this cause. In every case the loss stayed above that of a healthy network after the rank recovered. Whether a collapsed network can be repaired depends on whether the gradient still reaches the weights that must change, not on how strongly a loss term pushes.

</details>


### [334] [Temporal Predictive Multiplicity: Equally Accurate Time Series Models Yield Different Forecast Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09994)
*Emanuele Albini,Francesca Toni,Saumitra Mishra,Francesco Leofante*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 该论文提出时间预测多重性框架，揭示在时间序列预测中，预测性能相近的模型在完整预测轨迹上存在显著分歧，且这种轨迹级分歧与逐时间点分歧基本无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测多重性研究多关注单个标量输出，而时间序列预测中跨时间点的预测共同定义轨迹，逐时间点比较会掩盖预测行为的重要差异，因此需要新框架刻画完整轨迹上的分歧。

Method: 提出时间预测多重性框架，通过约束预测性能与逐时间点多重性，分析轨迹级多重性；在11个数据集上对19种神经预测架构进行实验。

Result: 仅约束预测性能仍允许广泛的不同轨迹；约束逐时间点多重性只能部分减少但无法消除轨迹级多重性；近优模型在预测轨迹上表现出显著变异性，且轨迹级分歧与逐时间点分歧 largely 无关。

Conclusion: 现有多重性研究存在空白：预测性能不可区分的模型可能隐含根本不同的时间轨迹，并带来重要的下游影响。

Abstract: Models with near-identical predictive performance can yield substantially different predictions, a phenomenon known as predictive multiplicity. Prior work has mostly studied this at the level of individual scalar outputs. In time-series forecasting, however, predictions across horizons jointly define a trajectory, and horizon-wise comparisons can hide important differences in predictive behavior. To address this problem, we introduce temporal predictive multiplicity, a framework that characterizes disagreement over complete forecast trajectories among models with near-identical predictive performance. We show that constraining predictive performance alone can still admit a broad range of different trajectories. We further show that constraining multiplicity at individual horizons partially reduces, but does not eliminate, trajectory-level multiplicity. Experiments with 19 neural forecasting architectures on 11 datasets confirm that near-optimal models can exhibit substantial variability in the forecast trajectories they produce, and trajectory-level disagreement is largely unrelated to horizon-wise disagreement. Our framework, therefore, exposes a gap in existing multiplicity studies: models with indistinguishable predictive performance imply fundamentally different temporal trajectories, with consequential downstream effects.

</details>


### [335] [Pre-training of Bayesian Optimization Algorithm through Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10186)
*Satoshi Katayama,Shoyo Hunt,Shintaro Masuda,Masayuki Karasuyama*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种利用高斯过程样本路径优化贝叶斯优化超参数的框架，通过经验累积遗憾估计并采用外层贝叶斯优化选择参数配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化算法的性能高度依赖需预先指定的参数，而现有方法缺乏基于初始信息的自适应参数选择机制。

Method: 从初始信息推断高斯过程，采样其样本路径；在内层运行贝叶斯优化算法，估计给定参数配置的期望累积遗憾；将参数优化视为黑盒问题，用外层贝叶斯优化进行求解。

Result: 实验表明，该框架能在候选参数配置中有效选出性能优良的配置。

Conclusion: 基于高斯过程样本路径的外层贝叶斯优化可自适应地选择贝叶斯优化参数，提升算法在未知黑盒函数上的累积遗憾表现。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is widely used as a standard approach for expensive black-box optimization. However, BO algorithms often involve parameters that must be specified in advance, and their performance can strongly depend on these choices. We propose a framework for optimizing such parameters using sample paths drawn from a Gaussian process (GP) inferred from the information available at the start of BO. We use cumulative regret as the performance metric for a BO algorithm. By running the BO algorithm on the generated sample paths, we obtain an empirical estimate of its expected cumulative regret for a given parameter configuration. Optimizing this estimate allows us to identify parameter configurations that, given the currently available information, are expected to achieve low cumulative regret. Since this parameter optimization is itself a black-box optimization problem, we employ another BO procedure to solve it, which we refer to as outer BO. Through experiments, we demonstrate that the proposed framework can effectively select parameter configurations that achieve strong performance among a range of candidate configurations.

</details>


### [336] [MorphCL: Morphological Contrastive Learning for Inertial-based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10245)
*Marius Bock,Yuwei Zhang,Juergen Gall,Michael Moeller,Kristof Van Laerhoven,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出MorphCL自监督预训练框架，通过结构感知分组建模大规模运动数据的全局结构，显著提升惯性数据下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法依赖随机采样批次和局部比较，未能充分利用大规模运动数据的全局结构，对以静止、低方差行为为主的野外惯性数据效果不佳。

Method: MorphCL基于运动基元（motif）发现和领域特定特征描述符，采用结构感知分组将全局结构显式建模注入惯性自监督学习。

Result: 在线性探测和微调任务中F1-score提升最多15个百分点；与现有基础模型相比，使用少4600倍数据达到相当或更优的线性探测性能；嵌入空间呈现形态学意义的聚类结构，且运动学相似活动类别的分离度提升。

Conclusion: MorphCL有效利用全局结构提升惯性运动表示学习，在少量数据下媲美或超越现有基础模型，并产生形态学可解释的嵌入空间。

Abstract: Despite the ubiquity of sensors in wearable and mobile devices and the abundance of human movement data they generate, translating unlabeled recordings into foundational motion models remains an open challenge. Self-supervised learning (SSL) has alleviated the need for costly annotations, yet existing approaches leave the global structure of large-scale motion data largely untapped, relying on randomly sampled batches and local comparisons that become particularly problematic for in-the-wild inertial data dominated by stationary, low-variance behaviors. Here we introduce Morphological Contrastive Learning (MorphCL), a self-supervised pretraining framework that uses structure-aware grouping to inject explicit modeling of global structure into inertial-based SSL approaches. Building on two well-established pillars of motion analysis, the discovery of motion primitives, or motifs, and domain-specific feature descriptors, we show that MorphCL substantially improves linear probing and finetuning results of learned encoders by up to 15 percentage points in F1-score. In a comparison with existing foundation models, we demonstrate that MorphCL-pretrained encoders match or surpass them models in linear probing performance while trained on $4600\times$ less data. Qualitative analysis of the resulting embedding spaces further reveals morphologically meaningful cluster structure, with improved separation of kinematically similar activity classes.

</details>


### [337] [A Closed-Loop Non-Asymptotic Convergence Analysis of PPO with Learned Critics and Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10273)
*Junwei Su,Mengfan Liu,Yanyong Zhang,Chuan Wu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文对带裁剪的近端策略优化（PPO-Clip）进行了非渐近闭式演员-评论家系统分析，刻画了评论家学习、裁剪与轨迹复用之间的耦合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO-Clip 广泛使用但难以调参，其评论家学习、裁剪与轨迹复用之间的相互作用尚不完全清楚，缺乏非渐近理论保证。

Method: 将 PPO-Clip 建模为闭式演员-评论家系统，在显式覆盖与评论家正则性假设下，结合原始 GAE 与蒙特卡洛评论家目标，分析演员-评论家耦合、非光滑概率比裁剪、有限批量复用与可预测早停；采用同步与非同步保证，利用分层 MDP 中裁剪梯度类的均匀界替代完整轨迹计数，并设计两时间尺度调度。

Result: 同步与非同步保证刻画了策略平稳性与评论家跟踪精度对算法参数的显式依赖；给出优化、评论家跟踪、裁剪与有限批量误差的统一放大界；非同步结果需要依赖延迟的评论家步长限制；验证的增长视界族具有多项式样本复杂度，两时间尺度调度达到 O(T^{-2/5}) 的平稳性与评论家跟踪界，并显式计入新轨迹。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了对 PPO 的理论理解，并为调参提供了理论指导；违反所给条件并不证明发散。

Abstract: Despite its widespread use, Proximal Policy Optimization with clipping (PPO-Clip) remains difficult to tune, and the interactions among critic learning, clipping, and rollout reuse remain incompletely understood. We develop a \emph{non-asymptotic} analysis of PPO-Clip as a \emph{closed-loop actor--critic} system. It captures actor--critic coupling, nonsmooth probability-ratio clipping, finite-batch reuse, and predictable early stopping under explicit coverage and critic regularity assumptions, using raw GAE and Monte Carlo critic targets. Our synchronous and asynchronous guarantees jointly characterize policy stationarity and the tracking accuracy of the learned critic, with explicit dependence on algorithmic parameters. A sufficient coupling condition gives optimization, critic tracking, clipping, and finite-batch errors a common amplification bound. The asynchronous result also requires a delay-dependent critic stepsize restriction; violating these conditions does not establish divergence. For finite layered MDPs with tabular critics, a uniform bound on the actual clipped-gradient class replaces complete-trajectory counting. A verified growing-horizon family has polynomial sample complexity, and a two-time-scale schedule gives $O(T^{-2/5})$ stationarity and critic-tracking bounds with explicit fresh-rollout accounting. These results together advance our understanding about PPO and provide theoretical guidance in tuning.

</details>


### [338] [Physics-Aligned Electronic Ground-State Learning Improves Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10298)
*Eike S. Eberhard,Xaver Kainz,Viktor Kotsev,Abdulrahman Aldossary,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 该论文提出电子基态描述符模型（GSMs），在MLIPs与KS-DFT之间建立成本-精度帕累托前沿，通过物理约束和优化目标对齐提升分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）在分布内任务表现优异，但分布外泛化能力差，难以兼顾成本与精度。

Method: 设计可观测无关的GSMs，对齐KS-DFT控制方程，施加物理约束并消除无关自由度优化压力；提出ON-Loss、GROOT和ROCKET等训练方法，并采用自洽拒绝准则和无标签自洽微调。

Result: 从QM9到QM40的尺寸外推中，密度GSMs能量和力MAE分别降低79.1%和83.4%；哈密顿GSMs分别降低99.8%和95.9%；在QMugs上拒绝率低于0.4%，能量MAE达0.07 mHa；在Transition1x上能量误差低于化学精度。

Conclusion: GSMs成功在成本与精度间取得平衡，显著提升分布外泛化能力，并高效迁移至反应化学任务。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) excel at in-distribution tasks, accelerating drug and material development, yet they struggle to generalize out-of-distribution. We propose to push the cost-accuracy Pareto frontier by designing observable-agnostic electronic ground-state descriptor models (GSMs) with computational costs situated between MLIPs and Kohn-Sham density functional theory (KS-DFT). We align the learning objectives and architectures of GSMs with the governing equations of KS-DFT by enforcing physical constraints and removing optimization pressure on unphysical or irrelevant degrees of freedom. In our size-extrapolation experiments from QM9 to QM40, our combined contributions OrthoNormal-Loss (ON-Loss) and Grassmann Restricted Occupied-Orbital Training (GROOT) reach a 79.1% energy and 83.4% force mean absolute error (MAE) reduction over previous state-of-the-art density GSMs. For Hamiltonian GSMs, ON-Loss and Residual Optimal-gauge Conditioning-aware KS-Eq. Training (ROCKET) together reduce the energy and force MAEs of the strongest baseline by 99.8% and 95.9%, respectively. Using a self-consistency rejection criterion, we filter out extrapolation errors on QMugs, rejecting fewer than 0.4% of predictions while reaching an energy MAE of 0.07 mHa. Finally, we demonstrate the efficiency of label-free self-consistency fine-tuning, and transfer GSMs to reactive chemistry in Transition1x, reaching energy errors below chemical accuracy.

</details>


### [339] [Shared Gaussianization: What Gaussian Regularizers Certify About Contrastive Learning, and What They Miss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10299)
*Ruoyu Zhao,Yuting Chen,Jinheng Zhang,Zhehao Zou,Tong Che*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文研究了对比学习中分布匹配正则化器（如SIGReg）的认证能力，通过共享高斯化（SG）检验推导出InfoNCE超额的尖锐界，并分析了对齐权重和批次大小的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究分布匹配正则化器能否为对比学习提供理论保证，特别是理解SIGReg等正则化器在InfoNCE优化中的作用机制。

Method: 提出共享高斯化（SG）——对两个归一化视图的平均值进行特征函数高斯性检验，并用独立χ_d半径缩放；理论推导SG损失与InfoNCE超额之间的界，并分析对齐项、均匀性检验谱条件、各向同性干扰通道及有限批次U统计量。

Result: SG在种群InfoNCE的对齐均匀极小值处消失；等边际下，InfoNCE超额被SG损失的平方根（常数4·3^{3/4}β）加线性项界定，该速率和维度无关常数是尖锐的；显式对齐项下，旋转不变均匀性检验当且仅当其谱主导InfoNCE核e^{βu^T v}时给出线性界，SG满足，高斯核e^{-γ||u-v||^2}在γ≥β/2时恰好满足，矩匹配永不满足；各向同性干扰通道中，纯SG在共享编码非均匀时通过增加每视图干扰降低损失，对齐权重大于通道增益可消除；LeJEPA存在随批次大小减小的临界SIGReg权重；有限批次下，非对角U统计量消除朝向错位的插值偏差；在受控潜变量模型中，纯SG保留每视图风格，对齐权重超过测量增益可去除，且测量增益能区分保留风格的编码器；InfoNCE训练达到比从头SG训练更低的SG_{0.2}损失，提示优化差距。

Conclusion: SG提供了对比学习的可认证保证，其平方根界是尖锐且维度无关的；对齐权重和批次大小对正则化效果有明确影响，纯SG可能保留 nuisance 风格，需要适当对齐权重；InfoNCE与SG优化存在差距，U统计量可纠正偏差。

Abstract: What can a distribution-matching regularizer such as SIGReg in LeJEPA certify about contrastive learning? We study shared Gaussianization (SG), a characteristic-function Gaussianity test on the average of two normalized views, scaled by an independent $χ_d$ radius. Because disagreeing views shorten the average, one test detects both misalignment and non-uniformity. SG vanishes exactly at the aligned, uniform minimizers of population InfoNCE, and under equal marginals it bounds the InfoNCE excess by $4\cdot 3^{3/4}β$ times the square root of the SG loss, plus a term linear in the loss. The square-root rate and this dimension-free constant are sharp, and no squared mean-embedding distance on view pairs achieves a faster rate. With an explicit alignment term, a rotation-invariant uniformity test gives a linear bound if and only if its spectrum dominates that of InfoNCE's kernel $e^{βu^\top v}$; SG's own test does, Gaussian kernels $e^{-γ\|u-v\|^2}$ qualify exactly when $γ\ge β/2$, and moment matching never does. Away from the optimum, the objectives differ. Along an isotropic nuisance channel, pure SG lowers its loss by adding per-view nuisance whenever the shared code is non-uniform. An alignment weight above the channel's gain makes the nuisance-free solution a strict local minimizer; for LeJEPA, the same rule gives a critical SIGReg weight that decreases with the batch size. At finite batch size, an off-diagonal U-statistic removes a plug-in bias toward misalignment. In controlled latent-variable models, pure SG retains per-view style, an alignment weight above the measured gain removes it, and for LeJEPA at three batch sizes the measured gain separates the encoders that retain style from those that do not. InfoNCE training also reaches a lower SG$_{0.2}$ loss than SG$_{0.2}$ training from scratch, which points to an optimization gap.

</details>


### [340] [PoreML: A Data-Driven Framework for Learning Multiphase Flow in Porous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10314)
*Chunyang Wang,Mingrui Zhang,Yuyan Zhang,Linqi Zhu,Xin Ju,Edo Sicco Boek,Martin J. Blunt,Gege Wen*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出开源框架PoreML，统一多孔介质多相流的数据生成、模型训练与评估，并发布大规模数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多孔微结构中多相流对CO2封存、燃料电池和倒装芯片封装至关重要，但润湿性和孔隙几何导致界面非线性演化难以预测；机器学习有潜力，却受限于稀缺的时间分辨3D数据集和缺乏统一工作流。

Method: PoreML包含三部分：(a) GPU原生格子玻尔兹曼求解器，经解析解和已发表实验验证，支持可复现数据生成；(b) 3.3 TB数据集，含560次模拟和158,546个存储时间步，覆盖四个应用场景，轨迹涵盖合成结构及真实材料微CT扫描几何，并包含不同润湿条件和粘度比；(c) 统一学习框架，评估单步预测和自回归展开，采用领域特定评估协议衡量预测精度和物理一致性；在协议下评估五种不同架构模型，并设置两个互补挑战：迁移到更大域和从合成结构迁移到微CT衍生结构。

Result: 构建了经验证的GPU格子玻尔兹曼求解器、大规模多场景数据集和统一评估框架，并对五种模型完成了单步预测、自回归展开、域迁移及合成到真实结构迁移的评估。

Conclusion: PoreML为多孔介质多相流的机器学习研究提供共享基础，旨在帮助社区开发可靠预测模型并推动领域发展。

Abstract: Multiphase flow in porous microstructures is central to CO$_2$ storage, fuel-cell operation, and flip-chip packaging. Predicting these flows remains challenging because wettability and complex pore geometry govern the nonlinear evolution of fluid interfaces. Machine learning holds substantial promise for advancing the field, but progress is constrained by scarce time-resolved 3D datasets and a lack of a unified workflow for training and evaluating models. To fill this critical gap, we introduce PoreML, an open-source framework unifying data generation, model training, and evaluation grounded in pore-scale physics. The framework comprises three core components. (a) A modern GPU-native lattice Boltzmann solver, validated against analytical solutions and published experiments, enables reproducible data generation. (b) A 3.3 TB dataset contains 560 simulation runs and 158,546 stored time steps across four application-driven scenarios. These trajectories span synthetic structures and geometries derived from micro-CT scans of real materials, covering diverse wetting conditions and viscosity ratios. (c) A unified learning framework evaluates one-step prediction and autoregressive rollouts. Its domain-specific evaluation protocols assess predictive accuracy and physical consistency. We evaluate five models of diverse architecture under these protocols. Two complementary challenges assess transfer to larger domains and from synthetic to micro-CT-derived structures. PoreML provides a shared foundation for machine-learning research on multiphase flow in porous media, with the aim of empowering the community to develop reliable predictive models and advance the field.

</details>


### [341] [Q-Learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10437)
*Yonghoon Dong,Minsung Yoon,Jaehyuk Kim,Jungwoo Park,Changyeon Kim,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出SQAM方法，通过标量伴随匹配简化流策略的离策略强化学习微调，避免逐步向量-雅可比积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流策略微调时，基于价值函数的更新需将价值信息从最终动作传播到每个流步骤，伴随匹配需逐步计算向量-雅可比积，成本随流步骤和策略规模增长。

Method: 观察到预训练流策略的批次平均速度雅可比集中于对角线，据此推导出闭式标量伴随，将最终动作的价值梯度按流时间缩放，消除每步向量-雅可比积；并引入对策略生成动作处评论家价值的惩罚，形成Q-learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching (SQAM)。

Result: 在四个最难的OGBench域上，SQAM成功率超过最强基线18至35个百分点；在真实双臂机器人上微调视觉-语言-动作策略，三个任务均优于监督微调。

Conclusion: SQAM有效简化了流策略的离策略强化学习微调，并在困难基准和真实机器人任务上取得显著提升。

Abstract: Flow policies capture rich and diverse action distributions, and fine-tuning them with off-policy RL to improve beyond the demonstrations has drawn growing interest. However, fine-tuning a flow policy against a learned value function is not trivial, because the policy generates its action over many flow steps. Adjoint matching offers a principled way to update the flow model itself by propagating value information from the final action back to each flow step, but it requires a vector--Jacobian product through the policy at every step, a cost that grows with the number of flow steps and the policy size. We observe that the batch-averaged velocity Jacobian of pretrained flow policies concentrates on its diagonal. Motivated by this finding, we derive a closed-form scalar adjoint that scales the value gradient at the final action by the flow time, eliminating the per-step vector--Jacobian products. We further find that controlling the critic's value at policy-generated actions is particularly important under the scalar adjoint. Based on these findings, we propose Q-learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching (SQAM), which combines the scalar adjoint with a value penalty at those actions. SQAM's gains concentrate on the four hardest OGBench domains, where its success rate exceeds that of the strongest baseline in each domain by 18 to 35 percentage points. To test whether SQAM extends to large pretrained policies, we also fine-tune a vision-language-action policy on a real bimanual robot. SQAM improves over supervised fine-tuning on all three tasks.

</details>


### [342] [Average-Reward Reinforcement Learning for Multichain MDPs: A Hierarchical Decomposition Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10326)
*Huizhen Yu,Isaiah Heidt*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文研究平均回报多链MDP中的最优策略学习，提出首个无需归约到折扣问题的本质无模型强化学习算法，并给出收敛性保证与实验对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均回报多链MDP中，最优增益可能依赖初始状态，且不同策略的常返结构不同，给强化学习带来挑战；现有方法多需归约到折扣问题或依赖模型知识。

Method: 提出基于异步值迭代的RL算法，仅需MDP转移图知识，利用Bather分解将状态空间分层划分为通信子系统与瞬态状态，将全局决策问题重构为结构化子问题；并在此基础上发展两个算法：一个近似求解多链平均最优方程以获得近增益最优策略，另一个通过逼近最优偏差函数并利用基础算法求解诱导的平均回报多链MDP以接近偏差最优。

Result: 证明三个算法均几乎必然收敛，基础算法能在有限时间内收敛到最优增益并产生增益最优策略；实验比较了算法间的权衡，后两个算法相比基础算法持续改善瞬态性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出了首批针对一般多链MDP、无需归约到折扣问题的本质无模型平均回报强化学习算法，并提供了收敛性保证与实证验证。

Abstract: We study learning optimal policies in average-reward multichain Markov decision processes (MDPs), where the optimal gain may depend on the initial state and recurrence structures vary across policies, creating challenges for reinforcement learning (RL) methods. We propose an asynchronous value-iteration-based RL algorithm that requires no model knowledge beyond the MDP's transition graph and leverages Bather's decomposition to hierarchically partition the state space into communicating subsystems and transient states. This decomposition induces a recasting of the global decision problem into structured subproblems, which our algorithm exploits. We show that the algorithm converges to the optimal gain and produces gain-optimal policies after finite time. Building on this base algorithm, we develop two further algorithms: one approximately solves the multichain average optimality equations to obtain near gain-optimal policies, and another targets near bias-optimality by approximating the optimal bias function and solving an induced average-reward multichain MDP using the base algorithm. We provide almost-sure convergence guarantees for all three algorithms and empirically compare their tradeoffs, showing that the latter two also consistently improve transient performance relative to the base algorithm. To our knowledge, these are the first essentially model-free average-reward RL algorithms for general multichain MDPs without reductions to discounted problems.

</details>


### [343] [AutoAdapt: Automatic Domain Discovery Enables Low-Cost Extensibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10349)
*Josh McGiff,Salma Mekaoui,Robert Shanahan,Nikola S. Nikolov*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: AutoAdapt是一个模块化框架，通过单适配器训练来集成新领域和数据，无需修改其他适配器或重训练全模型，并保持整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调模型部署在异质且不断演化的领域环境中，但添加新领域或数据通常需要昂贵的重训练，且多领域训练会导致领域干扰。

Method: 自动发现潜在领域，为每个领域独立并行训练LoRA适配器，并执行无参数路由。

Result: 在14个领域特定基准和GPT-4o成对评判中，AutoAdapt达到了与在所有领域上训练的单一LoRA适配器相当的性能，且无需全模型重训练；还发现独立发现方法间存在专业化效应收敛的证据。

Conclusion: 每个适配器在自身领域上训练，从构造上避免了领域干扰，从而实现了模块化、无分类法的领域专业化，既无整体性能损失，也无需全模型重训练。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned models are deployed into environments where domains are heterogeneous and evolve, yet adding new domains or data typically requires costly retraining. We present AutoAdapt, a modular framework that incorporates new domains and data via targeted single-adapter training without modifying other adapters. The framework automatically discovers latent domains, uses them to train per-domain Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapters independently in parallel and performs parameter-free routing. Across 14 domain-specific benchmarks and GPT-4o pairwise judgements, AutoAdapt achieves parity with a LoRA adapter trained on all domains without requiring full-model retraining. We also find evidence of specialisation effect convergence across independent discovery methods. Overall, training each adapter on its own domain prevents domain interference by construction, thus enabling modular, taxonomy-free domain specialisation without aggregate performance loss or full model retraining.

</details>


### [344] [Composing What Each Teacher Learned: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation through Teacher-Relative Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10460)
*Hejian Sang,Zhengze Zhou,Shayan Mohajer Hamidi,Xiaomin Li,Rohit Jain,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出Δ-MOPD，将教师相对其基座模型的logit偏移重新锚定在学生冻结初始化上，替代传统端点策略监督，用于多教师在线策略蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）通常迁移教师端点策略，而端点策略混合了后训练带来的改变与继承自教师基座的偏好，导致继承性基座拉力可能超过后训练偏移，干扰蒸馏信号。

Method: 定义Δ-MOPD，迁移每个教师的 teacher-minus-base logit shift，并锚定于学生冻结初始化；在公共域组合与路由域蒸馏两类设置下，保持教师选择固定，与端点监督进行对比，并分析教师项范数比与目标-学生KL。

Result: 去除继承基座拉力可降低教师项范数比与目标-学生KL；三教师组合时Δ-MOPD较端点组合提升4.11 Math和1.95 five-benchmark点，两教师时精度相当；分阶段路由下平均性能更高，顺序差距从10.50降至6.42点；交错路由下二者表现相近。

Conclusion: 目标构造是MOPD中独立于教师选择的设计轴；当教师信号在某一状态被组合时，迁移偏移目标尤为有效，且其收益可能延伸至跨训练阶段累积的信号。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) is used in two settings. In common-domain composition, several teachers score each student rollout from one prompt domain and their signals form a single target; in routed-domain distillation, prompts from different domains are assigned to the corresponding specialist. Both settings usually transfer each teacher's endpoint policy, which mixes what post-training changed with preferences inherited from the teacher's base. We introduce $Δ$-MOPD, which transfers each teacher's teacher-minus-base logit shift re-anchored at the student's frozen initialization, and compare it with endpoint supervision in both settings while holding teacher selection fixed. We first expose the mechanism that impedes endpoint transfer: inherited base pull can exceed the post-training shift. Removing it reduces the teacher-term norm ratio and target--student KL.
  Across our experiments, the results suggest that shift targets are particularly useful when teacher signals are combined at a state. With three composed teachers, $Δ$-MOPD exceeds endpoint composition by $4.11$ Math and $1.95$ five-benchmark points; with two, it matches endpoint accuracy. Under phased routing, it achieves higher mean performance in both phase orders and reduces the observed order gap from $10.50$ to $6.42$ points. Under interleaved routing, where each update involves one teacher, the two targets perform comparably. The phased results provide supporting evidence that the benefit may extend to signals accumulated across training phases. Target construction is thus an independent design axis in MOPD, complementary to teacher selection.

</details>


### [345] [ORDERS: An Empirical Study of Norm-Rank Aggregation for Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10361)
*Koffka Khan*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文研究ORDERS个性化联邦学习配置，通过控制局部训练和评估选择，分离服务器加权规则的真实贡献，发现其性能优势有限且对训练细节敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化联邦学习常将共享表示与客户端特定预测器结合，但服务器加权规则的贡献常被局部训练和评估选择所掩盖，因此需要明确其独立效果。

Method: 研究ORDERS配置：共享骨干、私有残差适配器与分类器、按更新范数降序分配的几何权重、特征对齐及私有参数扰动。服务器对同一广播模型所得更新进行加权求和。完整评估包含80次最终运行：8种配置、2个数据集、每个数据集固定划分下的5个训练种子。

Result: 在每客户端两类的CIFAR-10上，ORDERS原生平均客户端准确率为80.51±0.79%，FedPer-R1为79.02±1.42%，匹配均匀权重控制为80.27±0.73%；经共同局部微调后，与FedPer-R1差距缩小至0.32个百分点。在Sent140上，ORDERS达74.71±0.49%，仅比事后客户端训练多数诊断高0.69个百分点。消融实验对范数排序和对齐提供有限且依赖端点的证据，扰动无明确收益。参数负载节省分别为5.47%和0.78%。

Conclusion: ORDERS的服务器加权规则并未带来显著额外优化效果，其性能优势在控制局部训练和评估后大幅减弱，表明个性化联邦学习中服务器加权规则的贡献有限且易被其他因素掩盖。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning combines shared representations with client-specific predictors, but the contribution of a server weighting rule can be obscured by local training and evaluation choices. We study ORDERS, a configuration that combines a shared backbone, a private residual adapter and classifier, geometric weights assigned by descending update norm, feature alignment, and private-parameter perturbations. The server computes a weighted sum of updates obtained from the same broadcast model; it does not obtain an additional optimization effect from sequential addition. A fully specified evaluation comprises 80 final runs: eight configurations, two datasets, and five training seeds on one fixed partition per dataset. On two-class-per-client CIFAR-10, ORDERS achieves $80.51 \pm 0.79\%$ native mean client accuracy, compared with $79.02 \pm 1.42\%$ for FedPer-R1 and $80.27 \pm 0.73\%$ for the matched uniform-weight control. After common local fine-tuning, the difference from FedPer-R1 narrows to 0.32 percentage points. On Sent140, ORDERS reaches $74.71 \pm 0.49\%$, only 0.69 points above a post hoc client training-majority diagnostic. Ablations provide limited, endpoint-dependent evidence for norm ranking and alignment, and no clear benefit from perturbations. Parameter-payload savings are 5.47% and 0.78%, respectively.

</details>


### [346] [Koopman Observers for Diffusion Acceleration: Correcting Feature Forecasts with Shallow Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10366)
*Hanru Bai,Yuanchao Xu,Fengyi Li*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种观测校正的Koopman框架，利用廉价的新鲜浅层特征作为观测来校正深层特征预测，从而加速冻结扩散模型的采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征缓存方法仅基于历史特征预测，无法融入当前去噪状态的变化；作者探究是否可用廉价的新鲜计算特征作为观测来校正预测。

Method: 使用校准轨迹辨识有限维、时变的Koopman近似，联合描述浅层与深层网络特征的增量；加速采样时用算子预测深层特征演化，并用观测到的浅层特征新息校正预测状态，周期性全评估刷新观测器，生成模型参数保持不变。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和十类ImageNet子集上各进行三次10000张图像运行，相比同四部分步调度下的通道仿射预测，配对Inception特征MSE分别降低19.9%和11.9%；匹配消融显示观测校正额外贡献4.54%和4.67%的降低；相对DDIM-50实测加速1.89倍和1.85倍。

Conclusion: 该框架可在不重新训练去噪器的情况下提升参考采样器保真度并实现加速，同时支持对时间预测与观测校正进行受控比较。

Abstract: Feature caching accelerates diffusion sampling by replacing expensive network evaluations with predictions from previously computed activations. However, forecasts based only on past features cannot directly incorporate changes in the current denoising state. We investigate whether inexpensive, freshly computed features can serve as observations for correcting these predictions. We introduce an observation-corrected Koopman framework for accelerating frozen diffusion models. Using calibration trajectories, we identify finite-dimensional, time-dependent Koopman approximations that jointly describe the increments of shallow and deep network features. During accelerated sampling, these operators predict the evolution of expensive deep features, while innovations in the observed shallow features correct the predicted state. Periodic full evaluations refresh the observer, and all generative-model parameters remain unchanged. This formulation enables controlled comparisons of temporal prediction and observation correction. Across three 10,000-image runs per dataset, our method reduces paired Inception-feature MSE by $19.9\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $11.9\%$ on a ten-class ImageNet subset relative to channelwise affine prediction under the same four-partial-step schedule. Matched ablations attribute additional reductions of $4.54\%$ and $4.67\%$ to observation correction. The observer achieves $1.89\times$ and $1.85\times$ measured speedups over DDIM-50, supporting improved reference-sampler fidelity without retraining the denoiser.

</details>


### [347] [Executing Causal Structure Learning with Linear-Attention Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10395)
*Amartya Roy,Sayar Karmakar*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 该论文研究Transformer能否精确执行因果发现算法，通过显式构造固定权重Transformer来复现连续优化方法的单步更新，并验证其执行精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer是否不仅能处理输入数据，还能执行算法，特别是因果发现中的连续优化方法，以区分算法执行与因果恢复的准确性。

Method: 针对一种强制无环性的标准连续因果发现方法，显式构造固定权重Transformer，使其前向传播精确复现一次更新，并通过重复块模拟优化轨迹；Transformer在更新间携带当前图和乘子，并分析乘子保留的必要性及收敛条件。

Result: 构造的块与参考更新在浮点精度上一致，在合成数据和七个基准网络拓扑上算术重放继承参考求解器的成功与失败；普通注意力模型在训练预算下无法可靠执行更新或迁移到更大图。

Conclusion: 实现了精确的算法执行，但将其与准确的因果恢复分离；梯度训练能否在该架构类中学习执行器仍待研究。

Abstract: Transformers can execute algorithms on data given in their input. We ask whether they can do the same for causal discovery. We study a standard continuous method that repeatedly updates a candidate causal graph while enforcing acyclicity. We explicitly construct a fixed-weight transformer whose forward pass exactly reproduces one update of this method, so repeated blocks reproduce its optimization trajectory. The transformer carries the current graph and the algorithm's multiplier between updates. We show that retaining the multiplier is essential for exact execution, since different multiplier values can lead to different next updates. We also give conditions under which, within a fixed stage, the number of updates needed to reach a target accuracy can be computed in advance and rounding errors stay bounded as depth grows. Experiments show that the constructed block agrees with a reference update to floating-point precision, while arithmetic replay on synthetic data and seven published benchmark network topologies inherits the reference solver's successes and failures. This separates accurate algorithm execution from accurate causal recovery. In contrast, the ordinary attention models tested under our training budgets do not reliably execute the update or transfer to larger graphs. Whether gradient training can learn an executor in the architecture class of the construction remains open.

</details>


### [348] [Cross-Domain Pretraining for Steady-State Neural CFD Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10398)
*Anthony Zhou,Amir Barati Farimani,Shirley Ho,Rudy Morel*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 跨域预训练可显著提升CFD神经代理模型的泛化能力，减少对昂贵数值求解器生成新数据的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD神经代理模型难以泛化到训练集之外的几何与工况，而针对新应用生成数据集需运行昂贵的数值求解器。

Method: 研究跨不同几何、边界条件和保真度训练的神经代理模型，进行跨域预训练，并与从头训练及域内专家迁移进行对比；分析其有效原因，发现简单合并稳态数据集即足够有效。

Result: 跨域预训练提升零样本和少样本性能；微调后相同样本量误差降低2-3倍，达到相同误差所需样本减少8倍；该优势与架构无关，随模型规模和预训练数据多样性增加而提升。

Conclusion: 利用现有数据集进行跨域预训练是应对CFD数据高成本、扩展代理模型至新问题的有价值策略。

Abstract: Neural surrogates for computational fluid dynamics (CFD) have the potential to greatly enhance engineering innovation through accelerating simulation. However, the primary limitation for neural surrogates is the lack of generalization to geometries and applications beyond the training set, which is significant given the diversity of engineering scenarios. Currently, this is addressed by generating a new dataset for a specific application; however, this requires running costly numerical solvers. In this work, we take a step toward addressing this by studying neural surrogates trained across different geometries, boundary conditions, and fidelities. We find that cross-domain pretraining improves zero- and few-shot performance on held-out datasets relative to both training from scratch and transferring from domain-specific experts. In particular, finetuning a pretrained, cross-domain model can achieve 2-3x lower errors at the same sample size and use 8x fewer samples to achieve the same error, compared to training from scratch. This benefit is architecture agnostic and improves with model size and pretraining dataset diversity. Furthermore, we study how and why cross-domain pretraining works in CFD surrogates, and find that simply pooling steady-state datasets is both sufficient and effective. Given the high cost of generating CFD data, leveraging existing datasets through cross-domain pretraining will likely be a valuable strategy as future surrogates expand to tackle new problems and use cases.

</details>


### [349] [Which Rollout Taught It That? BehaviorTrace and the Limits of Training-Data Attribution in Online RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10422)
*Amit Nautiyal*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 该研究检验了在线RL微调中归因方法能否识别出教会模型新行为的训练rollout，并揭示现有信号多受混淆因素影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可让语言模型习得新行为，但能否追溯是哪些训练rollout导致该行为存疑；同时归因方法声称能做到时，其结果真实性缺乏评估标准。

Method: 基于GRPO在线RL微调，使用已知原因的植入行为，发布BehaviorTrace评估框架，整合全梯度草图、植入行为设置，并控制梯度幅度、流畅度、余量及种子和生成抽样变异；在Qwen2.5-1.5B上测试三个种子，评估GAS和TRAK式估计器。

Result: 多数表观归因信号来自混淆：仅按梯度大小排序训练步的对照达随机水平的4.2-4.5倍，并在三个种子中的两个上匹配或超越最佳目标估计器；饱和检查点下流畅度预测行为标签不差于所有梯度方法；控制流畅度后，逐rollout结果随种子和生成抽样变化。唯一在三个种子上稳定的信号是触发token的梯度与行为实际发生处构建的目标对齐。

Conclusion: 现有归因估计器在RL微调中易受混淆影响，单次运行无法定论；提出评估RL归因的检查清单，未提出新估计器。

Abstract: When reinforcement learning teaches a language model a new behavior, can we find the training rollouts that taught it? And when an attribution method says it can, how do we know the answer is real? We study both questions on online RL fine-tuning with GRPO, using a planted behavior with a known cause. We release BehaviorTrace, an open evaluation harness that combines full-gradient sketching, the planted-behavior setup, and controls for gradient magnitude, fluency, headroom, and variation across seeds and generation draws. Across three seeds on Qwen2.5-1.5B, much of the apparent attribution signal comes from confounds. A control that ranks training steps by gradient size alone, with no behavior target, reaches 4.2 to 4.5 times chance and matches or beats the best targeted estimator on two of three seeds. At saturated checkpoints, model fluency predicts the behavior label at least as well as every gradient method we compared it with. Once fluency is controlled, the per-rollout results change from seed to seed and from one generation draw to the next, so a single run cannot settle the question. One signal does hold on all three seeds. The gradient of the trigger tokens aligns with a target built where the behavior actually occurs. We turn these findings into a checklist for evaluating attribution in RL. We test existing estimators, including GAS (renormalized TracInCP) and a TRAK-style estimator, and do not propose a new one.

</details>


### [350] [Seq-Flow: Efficient Probabilistic Forecasting with Self-Rollout Error Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10440)
*Yinan Huang,Shitij Govil,Bo Dai,Pan Li*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: Seq-Flow是一种条件流模型，通过ODE将前一预测分布输运至更新分布，并采用自滚动训练实现少步高质量预测更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学预测任务需随新观测更新未来轨迹分布，传统扩散/流模型从高斯噪声生成需多步采样，热启动方法因模型未针对更新训练而在少步采样下质量下降。

Method: 提出Seq-Flow，条件流模型以ODE从前一预测分布输运到更新分布；因相邻预测差异小，可从有信息分布起步少步更新；采用自滚动训练，用模型移动平均副本生成预测作为后续流初始状态，避免误差累积。

Result: 在粒子加速器束流溢出预测中，少NFE采样预算下CRPS降低65%；在流体动力学预测中与强基线相当；虽仅用最多四次更新的自滚动训练，连续400余次更新仍保持准确。

Conclusion: Seq-Flow通过分布输运与自滚动训练，实现了少步、长序列的科学预测更新，代码已开源。

Abstract: Many scientific forecasting tasks require updating a distribution over future trajectories as new observations arrive. Conventional diffusion and flow models generate each forecast from Gaussian noise, often at the cost of many sampling steps. Warm-start methods reuse earlier predictions to reduce this cost, but their models are not trained to perform the forecast update itself, which can compromise quality under few-step sampling. In this work, we introduce Seq-Flow, a conditional flow model whose ODE transports samples from the previous forecast distribution to the updated one. Because successive forecasts often differ only modestly, this transport starts from an informative distribution and can produce accurate updates with few flow evaluations. Recursive reuse also creates a challenge: errors in one forecast become errors in the initial states of subsequent flows. We address this with self-rollout training, in which a moving average copy of the model generates forecasts that initialize later training updates. Unlike self-forcing methods, which reuse generated outputs as conditioning context, Seq-Flow reuses them as the source of the next flow. Experiments On particle-accelerator beam spill forecasting show Seq-Flow reduces CRPS by 65% under a few-NFE sampling budget, while remaining competitive with strong baselines on fluid-dynamics forecasting tasks. Although trained on self-rollouts of at most four updates, Seq-Flow remains accurate over more than 400 consecutive updates. Our code is available at https://github.com/Graph-COM/Seq-Flow.

</details>


### [351] [Why Forget-Only Unlearning Needs Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10519)
*Luka Radić,Vikrant Singhal,Amartya Sanyal*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文研究仅遗忘式机器遗忘的可行性，通过下界分析表明：删除所需信息可能远超普通训练保留的信息，仅遗忘式遗忘未必总可行，且模型可能需保留更多训练信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在仅获得已训练模型和待遗忘样本、无保留数据或额外训练信息时，能否始终实现与从头重训相近的遗忘效果。

Method: 通过构造不同数据集产生相同训练模型但删除后需不同输出的现象，推导遗忘精度下界；并针对标准学习算法实例化；同时推导算法为处理任意删除请求所需记忆训练数据的信息下界。

Result: 得到仅遗忘式遗忘匹配重训的精度下界；对简单阈值学习器，所需信息可高达整个数据集，尽管普通训练仅保留一个边界点。

Conclusion: 普通学习丢弃的信息可能在后续删除时被需要，因此面向仅遗忘式遗忘的模型可能需比标准训练保留更多信息。

Abstract: Machine unlearning asks for a deletion algorithm whose output is close to retraining from scratch without the selected forget examples. In this work, we study forget-only unlearning, where the deletion algorithm receives only the trained model and the examples to forget, with no retained data or extra training information. We ask whether forget-only unlearning is always possible. We first show that this depends on the learning method: different datasets can produce the same trained model but require very different outputs after the same examples are removed. Using this observation, we derive lower bounds on how accurately unlearning can match retraining and instantiate them for several standard learning algorithms. We then ask what must be true when forget-only unlearning succeeds. To this end, we derive lower bounds on what an algorithm must memorize about the training data to handle arbitrary deletion requests. For simple threshold learners, the required information can be as large as the entire dataset, even though ordinary training keeps only one boundary point. Overall, our results show that information discarded during ordinary learning may be needed later for deletion, so models designed for forget-only unlearning may need to retain more information than standard training does.

</details>
